FILE: app/globals.css

@import "tailwindcss";
@import "katex/dist/katex.min.css";

@theme {
  --color-canvas: #f7f7f5;
  --color-surface: #ffffff;
  --color-surface-subtle: #f0f0ed;

  --color-ink: #111111;
  --color-ink-soft: #686865;
  --color-ink-faint: #989895;

  --color-line: #deded9;
  --color-line-strong: #c9c9c3;

  --color-accent: #3157d5;
  --color-accent-strong: #2547b8;
  --color-accent-soft: #e9edff;

  --color-success: #208a5a;
  --color-success-soft: #e8f5ee;
  --color-danger: #c83b3b;
  --color-danger-soft: #fbeded;
  --color-warning: #b7791f;

  --font-sans:
    var(--font-geist), "Inter", system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
  --font-serif:
    var(--font-source-serif), "Source Serif 4", Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif;
  --font-mono: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", "Geist Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;

  --radius-sm: 6px;
  --radius-md: 10px;
  --radius-lg: 12px;
  --radius-xl: 16px;

  --ease-soft: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);

  --shadow-card: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04), 0 10px 30px -18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16);
  --shadow-float: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05), 0 18px 40px -24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28);

  --breakpoint-desk: 75rem;
}

/*
 * Theme selection (see src/lib/theme.ts). `data-theme="light"` / "dark" on
 * <html> selects the scheme; the JS bootstrap always sets it (defaulting to the
 * OS preference). Without JS the attribute is absent and the media query below
 * keeps the dark theme working. Every utility compiles to `var(--color-*)`, so
 * nothing else has to change.
 *
 * A media query cannot be merged with a selector, so the dark token set is
 * written twice (forced dark + OS fallback). Keep the two blocks in sync.
 * Contrast was checked for text, links, KaTeX, the derivative graph and
 * `.option` states.
 */
:root[data-theme="dark"] {
  color-scheme: dark;

  --color-canvas: #131312;
  --color-surface: #1c1c1a;
  --color-surface-subtle: #24241f;

  --color-ink: #f1f1ec;
  --color-ink-soft: #b3b3ac;
  --color-ink-faint: #8b8b84;

  --color-line: #35352f;
  --color-line-strong: #4a4a42;

  --color-accent: #8ea6ff;
  --color-accent-strong: #b0c1ff;
  --color-accent-soft: #1f2742;

  --color-success: #58d19a;
  --color-success-soft: #14271e;
  --color-danger: #f28c88;
  --color-danger-soft: #2b1616;
  --color-warning: #e0b062;

  --shadow-card: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4), 0 10px 30px -18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  --shadow-float: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5), 0 18px 40px -24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
}

@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
  :root:not([data-theme]) {
    color-scheme: dark;

    --color-canvas: #131312;
    --color-surface: #1c1c1a;
    --color-surface-subtle: #24241f;

    --color-ink: #f1f1ec;
    --color-ink-soft: #b3b3ac;
    --color-ink-faint: #8b8b84;

    --color-line: #35352f;
    --color-line-strong: #4a4a42;

    --color-accent: #8ea6ff;
    --color-accent-strong: #b0c1ff;
    --color-accent-soft: #1f2742;

    --color-success: #58d19a;
    --color-success-soft: #14271e;
    --color-danger: #f28c88;
    --color-danger-soft: #2b1616;
    --color-warning: #e0b062;

    --shadow-card: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4), 0 10px 30px -18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
    --shadow-float: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5), 0 18px 40px -24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
  }
}

:root[data-theme="light"] {
  color-scheme: light;
}

@layer base {
  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
    color-scheme: light dark;
    -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%;
  }

  body {
    background-color: var(--color-canvas);
    color: var(--color-ink);
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 17px;
    line-height: 1.6;
    overflow-x: hidden;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
  }

  p,
  li,
  blockquote {
    text-wrap: pretty;
  }

  h1,
  h2,
  h3 {
    text-wrap: balance;
  }

    body {
      font-size: 16px;
    }
  }

    color: inherit;
    text-decoration: none;
  }

  }
}

FILE: app/layout.tsx

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Geist, Source_Serif_4 } from "next/font/google";
import Script from "next/script";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/constants/site";
import { themeInitScript } from "@/lib/theme";
import { YandexMetrika } from "@/lib/analytics/yandex-metrika";
import "./globals.css";

const geist = Geist({
  subsets: ["latin", "cyrillic"],
  variable: "--font-geist",
  display: "swap",
});

const sourceSerif = Source_Serif_4({
  subsets: ["latin", "cyrillic"],
  style: ["normal", "italic"],
  variable: "--font-source-serif",
  display: "swap",
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  metadataBase: new URL(siteConfig.url),
  title: "Математика и информатика — задачи, разборы и занятия",
  description: siteConfig.description,
  applicationName: siteConfig.name,
  formatDetection: { telephone: false, address: false, email: false },
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html
      lang="ru"
      className={`${geist.variable} ${sourceSerif.variable}`}
      data-scroll-behavior="smooth"
      suppressHydrationWarning
    >
      <body className="flex min-h-dvh flex-col" suppressHydrationWarning>
        <Script
          id="theme-init"
          strategy="beforeInteractive"
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: themeInitScript }}
        />
        {children}
        <YandexMetrika />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

FILE: app/robots.ts

import type { MetadataRoute } from "next";

import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/constants/site";

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  // The `Host` directive expects a hostname, not a full URL.
  const host = new URL(siteConfig.url).host;

  return {
    rules: [
      {
        userAgent: "*",
        allow: "/",
        disallow: ["/api/"],
      },
    ],
    sitemap: `${siteConfig.url}/sitemap.xml`,
    host,
  };
}



FILE: app/(public)/error.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect } from "react";

import { Container } from "@/components/layout/container";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export default function PublicError({
  error,
  reset,
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    console.error("[public] route error", error);
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 items-center py-32">
      <Container>
        <span className="eyebrow">Ошибка</span>
        <h1 className="heading-2 max-w-[20ch]">Что-то пошло не так</h1>
        <p className="body-large mt-6">
          Страница не загрузилась. Попробуйте обновить — если ошибка повторяется,
          вернитесь на главную или в банк задач.
        </p>
        {error.digest ? (
          <p className="text-ink-faint mt-4 text-sm">Код ошибки: {error.digest}</p>
        ) : null}
        <div className="mt-10 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <Button type="button" onClick={reset}>
            Попробовать снова
          </Button>
          <Button asChild variant="secondary">
            <Link href="/">На главную</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Container>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/(public)/articles/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx

import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";

import { ImageResponse } from "next/og";

import { getArticleBySlug } from "@/features/articles/api";

export const alt = "Статья с разбором по математике или информатике";
export const size = { width: 1200, height: 630 };
export const contentType = "image/png";

const regular = await readFile(
  join(process.cwd(), "src/assets/fonts/noto-sans-regular.ttf"),
);
const bold = await readFile(
  join(process.cwd(), "src/assets/fonts/noto-sans-bold.ttf"),
);

export default async function Image({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
}) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const article = await getArticleBySlug(slug);
  const title = article?.title ?? "Статья";
  const exam = article?.exam ?? "";

  return new ImageResponse(
    (
      <div
        style={{
          width: "100%",
          height: "100%",
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          background: "#f7f7f5",
          padding: "72px 80px",
          fontFamily: "Noto Sans",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: "14px",
            fontSize: 24,
            letterSpacing: 2,
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            color: "#686865",
          }}
        >
          <span>Разбор</span>
          {exam ? <span style={{ color: "#3157d5" }}>· {exam}</span> : null}
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            fontSize: 60,
            lineHeight: 1.1,
            fontWeight: 700,
            letterSpacing: "-1.2px",
            color: "#111111",
          }}
        >
          {title}
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            fontSize: 26,
            color: "#989895",
          }}
        >
          <span>Как думать, а не заучивать</span>
          <span>Математика · Информатика</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
    {
      ...size,
      fonts: [
        { name: "Noto Sans", data: regular, weight: 400, style: "normal" },
        { name: "Noto Sans", data: bold, weight: 700, style: "normal" },
      ],
    },
  );
}




FILE: app/(public)/articles/[slug]/page.tsx

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { PageHero } from "@/components/layout/page-hero";
import { Section } from "@/components/layout/section";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { JsonLd } from "@/components/seo/json-ld";
import { ArticleBody } from "@/features/articles/components/article-body";
import { ArticleMeta } from "@/features/articles/components/article-meta";
import { ArticleToc } from "@/features/articles/components/article-toc";
import { ArticleRenderer } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/article-renderer";
import { RelatedArticles } from "@/features/articles/components/related-articles";
import { TutorCta } from "@/features/tutor-leads/components/tutor-cta";
import {
  getArticleBySlug,
  getArticleSlugs,
  getArticleToc,
  getRelatedArticles,
} from "@/features/articles/api";
import { articleJsonLd } from "@/lib/seo/json-ld";
import { buildMetadata } from "@/lib/seo/metadata";

type ArticlePageProps = { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> };

export async function generateStaticParams() {
  const slugs = await getArticleSlugs();
  return slugs.map((slug) => ({ slug }));
}

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: ArticlePageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const article = await getArticleBySlug(slug);

  if (!article) {
    return buildMetadata({
      title: "Статья не найдена",
      description: "Запрашиваемая статья не найдена.",
      path: `/articles/${slug}`,
      noIndex: true,
    });
  }

  return buildMetadata({
    title: article.title,
    description: article.description,
    path: `/articles/${article.slug}`,
    type: "article",
    publishedTime: article.published,
    modifiedTime: article.updated ?? article.published,
  });
}

export default async function ArticlePage({ params }: ArticlePageProps) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const article = await getArticleBySlug(slug);
  if (!article) notFound();

  const related = await getRelatedArticles(article, 2);
  const toc = getArticleToc(article);
  const midSlot = <TutorCta placement="article-mid" className="my-2" />;

  return (
    <>
      <JsonLd
        data={articleJsonLd({
          title: article.title,
          description: article.description,
          path: `/articles/${article.slug}`,
          published: article.published,
          modified: article.updated,
        })}
      />

      <PageHero
        breadcrumbs={[
          { name: "Главная", href: "/" },
          { name: "Статьи", href: "/articles" },
          { name: article.title },
        ]}
        eyebrow={`${article.exam} · разбор`}
        title={article.title}
      >
        <div className="mt-6">
          <ArticleMeta article={article} />
        </div>
        <p className="body-large mt-6">{article.description}</p>
      </PageHero>

      <Section bordered>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-12 items-start gap-4 md:gap-6">
          {toc.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="desk:col-span-3 desk:mb-0 col-span-12 mb-8">
              <ArticleToc items={toc} />
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div
            className={
              toc.length > 0
                ? "desk:col-span-8 desk:col-start-5 col-span-12"
                : "col-span-12"
            }
          >
            {article.content ? (
              <ArticleRenderer
                content={article.content}
                articleSlug={article.slug}
                midSlot={midSlot}
              />
            ) : (
              <ArticleBody blocks={article.blocks} midSlot={midSlot} />
            )}
            <TutorCta placement="article-end" className="mt-8" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Section>

      <RelatedArticles articles={related} />

      <Section bordered={false} className="pt-0 pb-16 md:pb-24">
        <Button asChild variant="secondary">
          <Link href="/articles">Все статьи</Link>
        </Button>
      </Section>
    </>
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";

import { PageHero } from "@/components/layout/page-hero";
import { Section } from "@/components/layout/section";
import { DemoNotice } from "@/components/ui/demo-notice";
import {

FILE: app/(public)/zadachi/page.tsx

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Suspense } from "react";

import { PageHero } from "@/components/layout/page-hero";
import { Section } from "@/components/layout/section";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { DemoNotice } from "@/components/ui/demo-notice";
import { StateMessage } from "@/components/ui/state-message";
import { getTasks, type TaskFilters } from "@/features/tasks/api";
import { Pagination } from "@/features/tasks/components/pagination";
import { TaskFilters as TaskFiltersBar } from "@/features/tasks/components/task-filters";
import { TaskList } from "@/features/tasks/components/task-list";
import { TaskListSkeleton } from "@/features/tasks/components/task-list-skeleton";
import {
  parsePageParam,
  parseTaskFilters,
  type RawSearchParams,
} from "@/features/tasks/search-params";
import { describeApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";
import { buildMetadata } from "@/lib/seo/metadata";
import { formatTaskCount } from "@/lib/utils/format";
import { buildQueryHref } from "@/lib/utils/url";
import Link from "next/link";

export const metadata: Metadata = buildMetadata({
  title: "Банк задач по математике и информатике",
  description:
    "Задачи ОГЭ и ЕГЭ по математике и информатике с ответами и пошаговыми решениями. Фильтры по предмету и сложности, без регистрации.",
  path: "/zadachi",
});

const BASE_PATH = "/zadachi";

function catalogueHref(filters: TaskFilters, page: number): string {
  return buildQueryHref(BASE_PATH, {
    subject: filters.subject,
    level: filters.level,
    page: page > 1 ? page : undefined,
  });
}

async function TaskCatalogue({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<RawSearchParams>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const filters = parseTaskFilters(params);
  const page = parsePageParam(params.page);

  let result;
  try {
    result = await getTasks(filters, page);
  } catch (error) {
    return (
      <StateMessage
        title="Не удалось загрузить задачи"
        description={describeApiError(error)}
        action={
          <Button asChild variant="secondary" size="sm">
            <Link href={BASE_PATH}>Сбросить фильтры</Link>
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  const isFiltered = Boolean(filters.subject || filters.level);

  return (
    <div>
      <TaskFiltersBar basePath={BASE_PATH} filters={filters} />

      {result.items.length === 0 ? (
        <StateMessage
          title="Ничего не найдено"
          description="По выбранным фильтрам задач нет. Попробуйте изменить условия."
          action={
            isFiltered ? (
              <Button asChild variant="secondary" size="sm">
                <Link href={BASE_PATH}>Показать все задачи</Link>
              </Button>
            ) : undefined
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <p className="text-ink-soft mb-6 text-sm">
            {formatTaskCount(result.total)}
            {result.pageCount > 1
              ? ` · страница ${result.page} из ${result.pageCount}`
              : null}
          </p>

          <TaskList tasks={result.items} />

          <Pagination
            className="mt-12"
            page={result.page}
            pageCount={result.pageCount}
            hrefForPage={(targetPage) => catalogueHref(filters, targetPage)}
          />
        </>
      )}

      {result.isDemo ? (
        <div className="mt-10">
          <DemoNotice>
            Показан встроенный демонстрационный набор задач. Он будет заменён содержимым
            банка, как только будет подключён API.
          </DemoNotice>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function TasksPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<RawSearchParams>;
}) {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        breadcrumbs={[{ name: "Главная", href: "/" }, { name: "Задачи" }]}
        eyebrow="Банк задач"
        title="Задачи по математике и информатике"
        description="Условие, ответ и решение по шагам. Фильтруйте по предмету и сложности — ссылку можно сохранить или отправить."
      />
      <Section bordered>
        <Suspense fallback={<TaskListSkeleton />}>
          <TaskCatalogue searchParams={searchParams} />
        </Suspense>
      </Section>
    </>
  );
}

import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";

import { ImageResponse } from "next/og";

import { getTaskById } from "@/features/tasks/api";
import { SUBJECT_LABEL } from "@/features/tasks/labels";

export const alt = "Задача по математике или информатике с решением";
export const size = { width: 1200, height: 630 };
export const contentType = "image/png";

const regular = await readFile(
  join(process.cwd(), "src/assets/fonts/noto-sans-regular.ttf"),
);
const bold = await readFile(
  join(process.cwd(), "src/assets/fonts/noto-sans-bold.ttf"),
);

export default async function Image({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const task = await getTaskById(id);
  const title = task?.title ?? "Задача";
  const subject = task ? SUBJECT_LABEL[task.subject] : "";
  const exam = task?.examLabel ?? "";

  return new ImageResponse(
    (
      <div
        style={{
          width: "100%",
          height: "100%",
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          background: "#f7f7f5",
          padding: "72px 80px",
          fontFamily: "Noto Sans",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: "14px",
            fontSize: 24,
            letterSpacing: 2,
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            color: "#686865",
          }}
        >
          <span>{subject}</span>
          {exam ? <span style={{ color: "#3157d5" }}>· {exam}</span> : null}
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "22px" }}>
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              fontSize: 68,
              lineHeight: 1.08,
              fontWeight: 700,
              letterSpacing: "-1.5px",
              color: "#111111",
            }}
          >
            {title}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 30, color: "#3157d5" }}>
            Ответ и решение по шагам
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            fontSize: 26,
            color: "#989895",
          }}
        >
          <span>Задача #{id}</span>
          <span>Математика · Информатика</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
    {
      ...size,
      fonts: [
        { name: "Noto Sans", data: regular, weight: 400, style: "normal" },
        { name: "Noto Sans", data: bold, weight: 700, style: "normal" },
      ],
    },
  );
}




FILE: app/(public)/zadachi/[id]/page.tsx

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { PageHero } from "@/components/layout/page-hero";
import { Section } from "@/components/layout/section";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { DemoNotice } from "@/components/ui/demo-notice";
import { JsonLd } from "@/components/seo/json-ld";
import { AnswerForm } from "@/features/tasks/components/answer-form";
import { DifficultyMeter } from "@/features/tasks/components/difficulty-meter";
import { RelatedTasks } from "@/features/tasks/components/related-tasks";
import { SolutionSteps } from "@/features/tasks/components/solution-steps";
import { TaskStatement } from "@/features/tasks/components/task-statement";
import { getRelatedTasks, getTaskById, getTaskIds } from "@/features/tasks/api";
import { SUBJECT_LABEL, SUBJECT_PATH } from "@/features/tasks/labels";
import { learningResourceJsonLd } from "@/lib/seo/json-ld";
import { buildMetadata } from "@/lib/seo/metadata";
import { formatMinutes } from "@/lib/utils/format";

type TaskPageProps = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };


export async function generateStaticParams() {
  const ids = await getTaskIds();
  return ids.map((id) => ({ id }));
}

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: TaskPageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { id } = await params;
  const task = await getTaskById(id);

  if (!task) {
    return buildMetadata({
      title: "Задача не найдена",
      description: "Запрашиваемая задача не найдена в банке.",
      path: `/zadachi/${id}`,
      noIndex: true,
    });
  }

  return buildMetadata({
    title: `${task.title} — задача #${task.id}`,
    description: `${task.summary} ${SUBJECT_LABEL[task.subject]}, ${task.examLabel}. Ответ и решение по шагам.`,
    path: `/zadachi/${task.id}`,
    type: "article",
  });
}

export default async function TaskPage({ params }: TaskPageProps) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const task = await getTaskById(id);
  if (!task) notFound();

  const related = await getRelatedTasks(task, 3);

  return (
    <>
      <JsonLd
        data={learningResourceJsonLd({
          name: `${task.title} — задача #${task.id}`,
          description: task.summary,
          path: `/zadachi/${task.id}`,
          educationalLevel: task.examLabel,
        })}
      />

      <PageHero
        breadcrumbs={[
          { name: "Главная", href: "/" },
          { name: "Задачи", href: "/zadachi" },
          { name: `#${task.id}` },
        ]}
        eyebrow={`Задача #${task.id}`}
        title={task.title}
      >
        <div className="text-ink-soft mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-3 text-sm">
          <Badge>{task.examLabel}</Badge>
          <Link
            href={SUBJECT_PATH[task.subject]}
            className="hover:text-accent transition-colors"
          >
            {SUBJECT_LABEL[task.subject]}
          </Link>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
            <DifficultyMeter level={task.level} />
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span>{formatMinutes(task.minutes)}</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </PageHero>

      <Section bordered>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-12 items-start gap-4 md:gap-6">
          <div className="desk:col-span-7 col-span-12">
            <h2 className="heading-3 mb-6">Условие</h2>
            <TaskStatement blocks={task.statement} />

            {task.topics.length > 0 ? (
              <p className="text-ink-soft mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <span>Темы:</span>
                {task.topics.map((topic) => (
                  <Badge key={topic} tone="neutral">
                    {topic}
                  </Badge>
                ))}
              </p>
            ) : null}

            {task.isDemo ? (
              <div className="mt-8">
                <DemoNotice>
                  Это тестовая задача из встроенного набора. Ответ проверен вручную и
                  помечен как демонстрационный.
                </DemoNotice>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>

          <div className="desk:col-span-5 col-span-12">
            <AnswerForm
              taskId={task.id}
              expected={task.answer.value}
              kind={task.answer.kind}
              answerNote={task.answer.explanation}
              solution={<SolutionSteps steps={task.steps} />}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Section>

      <RelatedTasks tasks={related} />

      <Section bordered={false} className="py-12 md:py-16">
        <Button asChild variant="secondary">
          <Link href="/zadachi">Вернуться в банк задач</Link>
        </Button>
      </Section>
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

export function SiteHeader() {

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 8);

    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);

  return (
    <header
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-50 border-b backdrop-blur-[14px] transition-colors duration-300",
        scrolled ? "border-line bg-canvas/88" : "bg-canvas/60 border-transparent",
      )}

      <Container>
        <nav
          aria-label="Основная навигация"
          className="desk:gap-12 flex h-16 items-center gap-8"
        >
          <Link href="/" className="text-lg font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em]">
            {brand.text}
            <span className="text-accent hidden sm:inline">{brand.accent}</span>
          </Link>

          <ul className="text-ink-soft hidden list-none gap-8 p-0 text-[15px] md:flex">

              <li key={item.href}>
                <Link
                  href={item.href}
                  className="hover:text-ink block py-2 transition-colors"
                >
                  {item.label}
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>

          <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
            <ThemeToggle className="hidden md:inline-flex" />

            <Button asChild size="sm" className="hidden md:inline-flex">
              <Link href={REQUEST_PATH}>Записаться на пробное</Link>
            </Button>

              <Link href={REQUEST_PATH}>Записаться</Link>
            </Button>

            <div className="md:hidden">
              <MobileNav />
            </div>
          </div>
        </nav>
      </Container>
    </header>
  );

FILE: components/seo/breadcrumbs.tsx

import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import type { ComponentProps } from "react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

export type BreadcrumbItem = {
  name: string;
  href?: ComponentProps<typeof Link>["href"];
};

export function Breadcrumbs({
  items,
  className,
}: {
  items: readonly BreadcrumbItem[];
  className?: string;
}) {
  if (items.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Хлебные крошки" className={className}>
      <ol className="text-ink-soft flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1 text-sm">
        {items.map((item, index) => (
          <li key={`${item.name}-${index}`} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {index > 0 ? (
              <ChevronRight aria-hidden className="text-ink-faint size-3.5" />
            ) : null}
            {item.href ? (
              <Link href={item.href} className="hover:text-accent transition-colors">
                {item.name}
              </Link>
            ) : (
              <span aria-current="page" className={cn("text-ink")}>
                {item.name}
              </span>
            )}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}

import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import type { ComponentProps } from "react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

export const buttonVariants = cva(
  "group/btn inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 rounded-md border border-transparent font-medium transition-[background-color,border-color,color,transform] duration-150 active:translate-y-px disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-60",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary: "bg-accent text-white hover:bg-accent-strong",
        secondary:
          "border-line-strong bg-transparent text-ink hover:border-ink hover:bg-surface",
        ghost: "text-ink hover:bg-surface-subtle",
      },
      size: {
        md: "min-h-12 px-[22px] text-base",
        sm: "min-h-10 px-4 text-[15px]",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "primary", size: "md" },
  },
);

export type ButtonProps = ComponentProps<"button"> &
  VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> & {
    asChild?: boolean;
  };

export function Button({
  className,
  variant,
  size,
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button";
  return (
    <Comp className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), className)} {...props} />
  );
}

export function ButtonArrow({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  return (
    <ArrowRight
      aria-hidden
      className={cn(
        "size-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-150 group-hover/btn:translate-x-1",
        className,
      )}
    />
  );
}

FILE: components/ui/math.tsx

import katex from "katex";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

type MathProps = {
  tex: string;
  display?: boolean;
  className?: string;
};

/**
 * Server-friendly KaTeX renderer. Invalid LaTeX never throws on the page: the
 * raw TeX is shown in a `<code>` fallback instead.
 *
 * `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` is used only for KaTeX's own output, never for
 * article-authored HTML.
 */
export function Math({ tex, display = false, className }: MathProps) {
  if (typeof tex !== "string" || tex.trim() === "") return null;

  let html: string;
  try {
    html = katex.renderToString(tex, {
      throwOnError: true,
      displayMode: display,
      output: "htmlAndMathml",
      strict: "ignore",
    });
  } catch {
    return (
      <code
        data-katex-fallback="true"
        className={cn(
          "bg-surface-subtle rounded px-1 font-mono text-[0.9em]",
          className,
        )}
      >
        {tex}
      </code>
    );
  }

  if (display) {
    return <div className={className} dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }} />;
  }

  return (
    <span
      className={cn("inline-block", className)}
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}
    />
  );
}




FILE: components/ui/rich-text.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { Math } from "@/components/ui/math";

export function RichText({ text, className }: { text: string; className?: string }) {
  const token = /\$([^$]+)\$/g;
  const nodes: ReactNode[] = [];
  let cursor = 0;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

  while ((match = token.exec(text)) !== null) {
    if (match.index > cursor) {
      nodes.push(text.slice(cursor, match.index));
    }
    nodes.push(<Math key={`${match.index}-${match[1]}`} tex={match[1]} />);
    cursor = match.index + match[0].length;
  }

  if (cursor < text.length) {
    nodes.push(text.slice(cursor));
  }

  return <span className={className}>{nodes}</span>;
}




FILE: components/ui/scroll-reveal.tsx

"use client";

import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect } from "react";

const OFFSET = 40;

/**
 * Progressive enhancement for `.reveal` sections.
 *
 * Normal scrolling is handled by a single IntersectionObserver; an
 * rAF-throttled scroll/resize flush covers elements that an instant jump (or a
 * layout shift) skipped over — those never cross an intersection threshold on
 * their own. Browsers with CSS scroll-driven animations animate the reveal in
 * CSS as well; this component only marks `data-reveal-in` and never runs
 * per-frame geometry reads.
 */
export function ScrollReveal() {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  useEffect(() => {
    const all = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(".reveal"));
    if (all.length === 0) return;

    if (
      window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches ||
      typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined"
    ) {
      for (const element of all) element.dataset.revealIn = "";
      return;
    }

    const pending = new Set(all);
    let frame = 0;

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        for (const entry of entries) {
          if (entry.isIntersecting) reveal(entry.target as HTMLElement);
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: `0px 0px -${OFFSET}px 0px`, threshold: 0 },
    );

    function reveal(element: HTMLElement) {
      if (!pending.delete(element)) return;
      element.dataset.revealIn = "";
      observer.unobserve(element);
      if (pending.size === 0) detach();
    }

    function flush() {
      frame = 0;
      const limit = window.innerHeight - OFFSET;
      for (const element of Array.from(pending)) {
        if (element.getBoundingClientRect().top < limit) reveal(element);
      }
    }

    function schedule() {
      if (frame === 0) frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(flush);
    }

    function detach() {
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", schedule);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", schedule);
      if (frame) window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
      observer.disconnect();
    }

    function rescan() {
      for (const element of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(".reveal")) {
        if (element.dataset.revealIn !== undefined || pending.has(element)) continue;
        pending.add(element);
        observer.observe(element);
      }
    }

    for (const element of pending) observer.observe(element);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", schedule, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("resize", schedule);

    // Sections rendered by async server components can appear after this effect.
    const timers = [250, 800, 1800].map((delay) =>
      window.setTimeout(rescan, delay),
    );

    flush();

    return () => {
      for (const timer of timers) window.clearTimeout(timer);
      detach();
    };
  }, [pathname]);

  return null;
}

FILE: components/ui/theme-toggle.tsx

"use client";

import { Moon, Sun, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useRef, useSyncExternalStore, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";

import {
  getThemePreference,
  setThemePreference,
  subscribeTheme,
  type ThemePreference,
} from "@/lib/theme";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

const OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<{
  value: ThemePreference;
  label: string;
  Icon: LucideIcon;
}> = [
  { value: "light", label: "Светлая тема", Icon: Sun },
  { value: "dark", label: "Тёмная тема", Icon: Moon },
];

const NAV_KEYS = new Set(["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "ArrowUp", "ArrowDown"]);

export function ThemeToggle({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const preference = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeTheme,
    getThemePreference,
    (): ThemePreference => "light",
  );
  const buttons = useRef<Array<HTMLButtonElement | null>>([]);

  function onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, index: number) {
    if (!NAV_KEYS.has(event.key)) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    const step = event.key === "ArrowRight" || event.key === "ArrowDown" ? 1 : -1;
    const next = (index + step + OPTIONS.length) % OPTIONS.length;
    setThemePreference(OPTIONS[next].value);
    buttons.current[next]?.focus();
  }

  return (
    <div
      role="radiogroup"
      aria-label="Тема оформления"
      className={cn(
        "border-line bg-surface-subtle/70 inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full border p-0.5",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {OPTIONS.map(({ value, label, Icon }, index) => {
        const active = preference === value;
        return (
          <button
            key={value}
            ref={(node) => {
              buttons.current[index] = node;
            }}
            type="button"
            role="radio"
            aria-checked={active}
            aria-label={label}
            title={label}
            tabIndex={active ? 0 : -1}
            onClick={() => setThemePreference(value)}
            onKeyDown={(event) => onKeyDown(event, index)}
            className="theme-option inline-flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full"
            data-value={value}
          >
            <Icon aria-hidden className="size-4" />
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}




FILE: features/content-fixtures.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { demoArticles } from "@/features/articles/fixtures/articles";
import { getArticleToc } from "@/features/articles/toc";
import { demoTasks } from "@/features/tasks/fixtures/tasks";

describe("demo task fixtures", () => {
  it("have unique ids", () => {
    const ids = demoTasks.map((task) => task.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });

  it("are complete enough to render a task page", () => {
    for (const task of demoTasks) {
      expect(task.title.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(task.statement.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(task.steps.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(task.answer.value.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(task.summary.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("uses numeric answers that parse as finite numbers", () => {
    for (const task of demoTasks) {
      if (task.answer.kind !== "number") continue;
      expect(Number.isFinite(Number(task.answer.value))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("features exactly the three tasks shown on the home page", () => {
    expect(demoTasks.filter((task) => task.featured)).toHaveLength(3);
  });
});

describe("demo article fixtures", () => {
  it("have unique slugs and resolvable headings", () => {
    const slugs = demoArticles.map((article) => article.slug);
    expect(new Set(slugs).size).toBe(slugs.length);
  });

  it("builds a table of contents only when there are enough headings", () => {
    for (const article of demoArticles) {
      const headings = article.blocks.filter((block) => block.type === "heading");
      const toc = getArticleToc(article);
      if (headings.length >= 3) {
        expect(toc.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
      } else {
        expect(toc).toHaveLength(0);
      }
    }
  });

  it("uses unique heading ids inside an article", () => {
    for (const article of demoArticles) {
      const ids = article.blocks.flatMap((block) =>
        block.type === "heading" ? [block.id] : [],
      );
      expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    }
  });
});

const mockedRequest = vi.mocked(serverRequest);

const article: Article = {
  slug: "interactive",
  title: "Interactive",
  description: "d",
  subject: "matematika",
  exam: "ЕГЭ",
  readingMinutes: 5,
  published: "2026-01-01",
  blocks: [],
  content: { version: 2, blocks: [] },
  isDemo: false,
};

  mockedConfigured.mockReturnValue(true);
});

  it("returns an article with structured content from the API", async () => {
    mockedRequest.mockResolvedValue(article);

    await expect(getArticleBySlug("interactive")).resolves.toEqual(article);
    expect(mockedRequest).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/articles/interactive");
  });

  it("returns null when the API responds with 404", async () => {
    mockedRequest.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("http", "not found", { status: 404 }));

    await expect(getArticleBySlug("missing")).resolves.toBeNull();
  });

  it("rethrows non-404 API errors", async () => {
    mockedRequest.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("network", "down"));

    await expect(getArticleBySlug("interactive")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
  });

  it("returns the published list from the API", async () => {
    mockedRequest.mockResolvedValue([article]);

    await expect(getArticles()).resolves.toEqual({
      items: [article],
      isDemo: false,
    });
  });

  it("falls back to local fixtures when the API is not configured", async () => {
    mockedConfigured.mockReturnValue(false);

    const result = await getArticleBySlug("proizvodnaya-ne-pro-formuly");

    expect(result?.slug).toBe("proizvodnaya-ne-pro-formuly");
    expect(mockedRequest).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});




FILE: features/articles/api.ts

import { isApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";
import { isServerApiConfigured, serverRequest } from "@/lib/api/server";
import { demoArticles } from "@/features/articles/fixtures/articles";
import type { Article } from "@/features/articles/types";
import type { TaskSubject } from "@/features/tasks/types";

export type ArticleListResult = {
  items: Article[];
  isDemo: boolean;
};

export async function getArticles(): Promise<ArticleListResult> {
  if (isServerApiConfigured()) {
    const items = await serverRequest<Article[]>("/articles");
    return { items, isDemo: items.some((article) => article.isDemo) };
  }

  // Build-time / offline fallback only. In development the API origin defaults
  // to localhost, so normally this branch is never taken and articles always
  // come from the backend.
  return { items: [...demoArticles], isDemo: true };
}

export async function getArticleBySlug(slug: string): Promise<Article | null> {
  if (isServerApiConfigured()) {
    try {
      return await serverRequest<Article>(`/articles/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`);
    } catch (error) {
      if (isApiError(error) && error.status === 404) return null;
      throw error;
    }
  }

  return demoArticles.find((article) => article.slug === slug) ?? null;
}

export async function getArticlesBySubject(
  subject: TaskSubject,
  limit = 3,
): Promise<Article[]> {
  const { items } = await getArticles();
  return items.filter((article) => article.subject === subject).slice(0, limit);
}

export async function getRelatedArticles(
  article: Article,
  limit = 2,
): Promise<Article[]> {
  const { items } = await getArticles();
  const others = items.filter((candidate) => candidate.slug !== article.slug);

  const matching = others.filter(
    (candidate) =>
      candidate.subject === article.subject || candidate.exam === article.exam,
  );

  const rest = others.filter((candidate) => !matching.includes(candidate));
  return [...matching, ...rest].slice(0, limit);
}

export async function getArticleSlugs(): Promise<string[]> {
  if (!isServerApiConfigured()) {
    return demoArticles.map((article) => article.slug);
  }
  const items = await serverRequest<Article[]>("/articles");
  return items.map((article) => article.slug);
}

export function getArticlePath(slug: string): string {
  return `/articles/${slug}`;
}

export { getArticleToc } from "@/features/articles/toc";

FILE: features/articles/exercises-api.ts

import type { InlineNode } from "@/features/articles/types";
import { clientRequest } from "@/lib/api/client";

export type ExerciseCheckResult = {
  isCorrect: boolean;
  checkedAt: string;
  explanation: InlineNode[] | null;
  attempts: number | null;
  solved: boolean | null;
};

/** Backend-authoritative check for a published article exercise. */
export async function checkArticleExercise(
  slug: string,
  exerciseId: string,
  answer: string,
): Promise<ExerciseCheckResult> {
  return clientRequest<ExerciseCheckResult>(
    `/articles/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}/exercises/${encodeURIComponent(exerciseId)}/check`,
    { method: "POST", body: { answer } },
  );
}

FILE: features/articles/runtime.ts

/** Runtime guards and helpers shared by the article renderer and the TOC. */

export function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

export function asArray<T>(value: unknown): T[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? (value as T[]) : [];
}

export function asString(value: unknown, fallback = ""): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : fallback;
}

/** Allow only internal paths and absolute HTTPS links (defence in depth). */
export function safeHref(value: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (typeof value !== "string") return undefined;
  if (value.startsWith("/") && !value.startsWith("//")) return value;
  if (value.startsWith("https://") && value.length > "https://".length) {
    return value;
  }
  return undefined;
}

export function isExternalHref(href: string): boolean {
  return href.startsWith("https://");
}

/** Flatten structured inline nodes to plain text (used for TOC and labels). */
export function inlineNodesToText(nodes: unknown): string {
  if (!Array.isArray(nodes)) return "";

  return nodes
    .map((node) => {
      if (!isRecord(node)) return "";
      switch (node.type) {
        case "text":
        case "code":
          return asString(node.text);
        case "math":
          return asString(node.tex);
        case "br":
          return " ";
        case "strong":
        case "em":
        case "link":
          return inlineNodesToText(node.children);
        default:
          return "";
      }
    })
    .join("")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}




FILE: features/articles/toc.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { getArticleToc } from "@/features/articles/toc";
import type { Article } from "@/features/articles/types";

function makeArticle(overrides: Partial<Article> = {}): Article {
  return {
    slug: "article",
    title: "Article",
    description: "",
    subject: "matematika",
    exam: "ЕГЭ",
    readingMinutes: 5,
    published: "2026-01-01",
    blocks: [],
    isDemo: false,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("getArticleToc", () => {
  it("builds a table of contents from v2 rich_text headings", () => {
    const toc = getArticleToc(
      makeArticle({
        content: {
          version: 2,
          blocks: [
            {
              id: "h1",
              type: "rich_text",
              data: {
                kind: "heading",
                level: 2,
                children: [{ type: "text", text: "Первый" }],
              },
            },
            {
              id: "h2",
              type: "rich_text",
              data: {
                kind: "heading",
                level: 3,
                children: [
                  { type: "strong", children: [{ type: "text", text: "Второй" }] },
                ],
              },
            },
            {
              id: "h3",
              type: "rich_text",
              data: {
                kind: "heading",
                level: 2,
                children: [{ type: "text", text: "Третий" }],
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      }),
    );

    expect(toc).toEqual([
      { id: "h1", text: "Первый" },
      { id: "h2", text: "Второй" },
      { id: "h3", text: "Третий" },
    ]);
  });

  it("falls back to v1 headings for legacy articles", () => {
    const toc = getArticleToc(
      makeArticle({
        blocks: [
          { type: "heading", id: "a", text: "A" },
          { type: "heading", id: "b", text: "B" },
          { type: "heading", id: "c", text: "C" },
        ],
      }),
    );

    expect(toc.map((item) => item.id)).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
  });

  it("returns an empty table of contents for fewer than three headings", () => {
    const toc = getArticleToc(
      makeArticle({
        content: {
          version: 2,
          blocks: [
            {
              id: "h1",
              type: "rich_text",
              data: {
                kind: "heading",
                level: 2,
                children: [{ type: "text", text: "Один" }],
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      }),
    );

    expect(toc).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("does not fall back to v1 blocks when v2 content is present", () => {
    const toc = getArticleToc(
      makeArticle({
        blocks: [
          { type: "heading", id: "a", text: "A" },
          { type: "heading", id: "b", text: "B" },
          { type: "heading", id: "c", text: "C" },
        ],
        content: {
          version: 2,
          blocks: [
            {
              id: "h1",
              type: "rich_text",
              data: {
                kind: "heading",
                level: 2,
                children: [{ type: "text", text: "Один" }],
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      }),
    );

    expect(toc).toEqual([]);
  });
});




FILE: features/articles/toc.ts

import { inlineNodesToText } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type { Article, ArticleTocItem } from "@/features/articles/types";

export function getArticleToc(article: Article): ArticleTocItem[] {
  const v2Blocks =
    article.content && Array.isArray(article.content.blocks)
      ? article.content.blocks
      : null;

  const headings = v2Blocks
    ? v2Blocks.flatMap((block) => {
        if (block.type !== "rich_text" || block.data.kind !== "heading") {
          return [];
        }
        const text = inlineNodesToText(block.data.children);
        return block.id && text ? [{ id: block.id, text }] : [];
      })
    : article.blocks.flatMap((block) =>
        block.type === "heading" ? [{ id: block.id, text: block.text }] : [],
      );

  return headings.length >= 3 ? headings : [];
}




FILE: features/articles/types.ts

import type { TaskSubject } from "@/features/tasks/types";

/** Legacy (v1) article blocks, still served in `Article.blocks`. */
export type ArticleBlock =
  | { type: "paragraph"; text: string }
  | { type: "heading"; text: string; id: string }
  | { type: "list"; ordered?: boolean; items: string[] }
  | { type: "formula"; tex: string; caption?: string }
  | { type: "quote"; text: string };

/** Inline nodes of the structured rich text (contract v2). */
export type InlineNode =
  | { type: "text"; text: string }
  | { type: "strong"; children: InlineNode[] }
  | { type: "em"; children: InlineNode[] }
  | { type: "code"; text: string }
  | { type: "link"; href: string; children: InlineNode[] }
  | { type: "math"; tex: string }
  | { type: "br" };

export type RichTextBlockData = {
  kind: "paragraph" | "heading" | "list";
  children?: InlineNode[];
  level?: 2 | 3;
  ordered?: boolean;
  items?: InlineNode[][];
};

export type MathBlockData = {
  tex: string;
  display?: boolean;
  caption?: string;
};

export type GraphExpression = {
  id: string;
  expr: string;
  label?: string;
  color?: string;
};

export type GraphParameter = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  step: number;
  default: number;
};

export type GraphViewport = {
  x_min: number;
  x_max: number;
  y_min: number;
  y_max: number;
};

export type InteractiveGraphBlockData = {
  engine: "svg";
  expressions: GraphExpression[];
  parameters?: GraphParameter[];
  viewport?: GraphViewport;
  interactions?: Array<"pan" | "zoom" | "trace">;
};

export type ChoiceOption = {
  id: string;
  label: InlineNode[];
};

/** Public projection: the backend strips the correct answer and explanation. */
export type SingleChoiceBlockData = {
  prompt: InlineNode[];
  options: ChoiceOption[];
  points?: number;
};

/** Public projection: the backend strips the correct value, tolerance and explanation. */
export type NumericInputBlockData = {
  prompt: InlineNode[];
  unit?: string;
  points?: number;
};

export type StepMath = {
  tex: string;
  display?: boolean;
};

export type SolutionStepV2 = {
  id: string;
  note: InlineNode[];
  math?: StepMath;
};

export type StepSolutionBlockData = {
  steps: SolutionStepV2[];
};

export type HintBlockData = {
  title?: string;
  body: InlineNode[];
};

export type CalloutKind = "info" | "tip" | "warning" | "danger";

export type CalloutBlockData = {
  kind?: CalloutKind;
  title?: string;
  body: InlineNode[];
};

export type ImageBlockData = {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  caption?: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
};

export type DividerBlockData = {
  variant?: "solid" | "spaced";
};

export type ArticleBlockV2 =
  | { id: string; type: "rich_text"; data: RichTextBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "math"; data: MathBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "interactive_graph"; data: InteractiveGraphBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "single_choice"; data: SingleChoiceBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "numeric_input"; data: NumericInputBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "step_solution"; data: StepSolutionBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "hint"; data: HintBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "callout"; data: CalloutBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "image"; data: ImageBlockData }
  | { id: string; type: "divider"; data: DividerBlockData };

export type ArticleContent = {
  version: 2;
  blocks: ArticleBlockV2[];
};

export type Article = {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  subject: TaskSubject | null;
  exam: string;
  readingMinutes: number;
  published: string;
  updated?: string;
  /** Legacy (v1) blocks. */
  blocks: ArticleBlock[];
  /** Structured interactive content (contract v2), `null` for legacy articles. */
  content?: ArticleContent | null;
  isDemo: boolean;
};

export type ArticleTocItem = {
  id: string;
  text: string;
};




FILE: features/articles/components/article-body.tsx

import { Fragment, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { Math } from "@/components/ui/math";
import { RichText } from "@/components/ui/rich-text";
import type { ArticleBlock } from "@/features/articles/types";

function ArticleBlockView({ block }: { block: ArticleBlock }) {
  switch (block.type) {
    case "paragraph":
      return (
        <p className="text-ink-soft text-lg leading-relaxed">
          <RichText text={block.text} />
        </p>
      );
    case "heading":
      return (
        <h2 id={block.id} className="heading-3 mt-6 scroll-mt-24 text-balance">
          {block.text}
        </h2>
      );
    case "list":
      return block.ordered ? (
        <ol className="text-ink-soft m-0 list-decimal space-y-2 pl-6 text-lg">
          {block.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item}>
              <RichText text={item} />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      ) : (
        <ul className="text-ink-soft m-0 list-disc space-y-2 pl-6 text-lg">
          {block.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item}>
              <RichText text={item} />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      );
    case "formula":
      return (
        <figure className="border-accent-soft my-2 flex flex-col gap-2 overflow-x-auto border-l-2 pl-4">
          <Math display tex={block.tex} />
          {block.caption ? (
            <figcaption className="text-ink-faint text-sm">{block.caption}</figcaption>
          ) : null}
        </figure>
      );
    case "quote":
      return (
        <blockquote className="border-accent text-ink border-l-2 pl-5 text-xl leading-snug font-medium">
          {block.text}
        </blockquote>
      );
  }
}

export function ArticleBody({
  blocks,
  midSlot,
}: {
  blocks: readonly ArticleBlock[];
  /** Inline conversion block inserted around the middle of the article. */
  midSlot?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const midIndex = globalThis.Math.floor((blocks.length - 1) / 2);

  return (
    <div className="flex max-w-[68ch] flex-col gap-5">
      {blocks.map((block, index) => (
        <Fragment key={`${block.type}-${"id" in block ? block.id : index}`}>
          <ArticleBlockView block={block} />
          {midSlot && index === midIndex ? midSlot : null}
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

import { SUBJECT_LABEL } from "@/features/tasks/labels";

export function ArticleMeta({ article }: { article: Article }) {

  );

export function ArticleToc({ items }: { items: readonly ArticleTocItem[] }) {

  return (
    <nav aria-labelledby="toc-title" className="desk:sticky desk:top-24">
      <h2
        id="toc-title"
        className="text-ink-faint mb-4 text-sm font-medium tracking-[0.08em] uppercase"
      >

      </h2>
      <ol className="m-0 flex list-none flex-col gap-1 p-0 text-[15px]">

          <li key={item.id}>
            <a
              href={`#${item.id}`}
              className="text-ink-soft hover:text-accent flex gap-3 rounded-sm py-1 transition-colors"
            >
              <span className="text-ink-faint tabular-nums">{index + 1}</span>
              <span>{item.text}</span>
            </a>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}

  return (

      aria-labelledby="related-articles-title"
      className="border-line desk:py-[140px] border-t py-20 md:py-[104px]"
    >

            <li key={article.slug} className="flex">
              <ArticleCard article={article} />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Container>
    </section>
  );
}

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { ArticleRenderer } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/article-renderer";
import type { ArticleBlockV2, ArticleContent } from "@/features/articles/types";

vi.mock(
  "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/interactive-graph-loader",
  () => ({
    InteractiveGraphLoader: () => null,
  }),
);

function content(blocks: unknown[]): ArticleContent {
  return { version: 2, blocks: blocks as ArticleBlockV2[] };
}

describe("ArticleRenderer", () => {
  it("renders structured rich text, links and inline math", () => {
    render(
      <ArticleRenderer
        content={content([
          {
            id: "rt",
            type: "rich_text",
            data: {
              kind: "paragraph",
              children: [
                { type: "text", text: "Привет, " },
                { type: "strong", children: [{ type: "text", text: "мир" }] },
                { type: "text", text: " и " },
                {
                  type: "link",
                  href: "/zadachi",
                  children: [{ type: "text", text: "задачи" }],
                },
                { type: "math", tex: "x^2" },
              ],
            },
          },
        ])}
      />,
    );

    expect(screen.getByText(/Привет/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("мир").tagName).toBe("STRONG");
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "задачи" }).getAttribute("href")).toBe(
      "/zadachi",
    );

  });

        content={content([

            id: "intro",

              kind: "heading",
              level: 2,

            },
          },

            type: "rich_text",

              kind: "list",
              ordered: true,
              items: [
                [{ type: "text", text: "Первый" }],
                [{ type: "text", text: "Второй" }],
              ],
            },
          },
        ])}
      />,
    );

      "id",
      "intro",
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("list")).toBeInTheDocument();

        ])}
      />,

    expect(document.querySelector(".katex")).toBeTruthy();

    expect(screen.getByText("Формула недоступна")).toBeInTheDocument();

  });

  it("renders a fallback for unsupported content versions", () => {

        content={{ version: 3, blocks: [] } as unknown as ArticleContent}
      />,
    );

  });

  it("does not create javascript: links", () => {
    render(

        content={content([

            id: "rt",
            type: "rich_text",
            data: {

  it("produces server-rendered HTML with text and formulas", () => {

      <ArticleRenderer
        content={content([
          {
            id: "p",
            type: "rich_text",

            },

        ])}

  });

  it("does not render exercise explanations before checking is available", () => {

    );

FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/article-renderer.tsx

import { Fragment, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { CalloutBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/callout-block";
import { DividerBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/divider-block";
import { HintBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/hint-block";
import { ImageBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/image-block";
import { InteractiveGraphBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/interactive-graph-block";
import { MathBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/math-block";
import { NumericInputBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/numeric-input-block";
import { RichTextBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/rich-text-block";
import { SingleChoiceBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/single-choice-block";
import { StepSolutionBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/step-solution-block";
import { UnknownBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/unknown-block";
import { isRecord } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type { ArticleBlockV2, ArticleContent } from "@/features/articles/types";

export function ArticleRenderer({
  content,
  articleSlug,
  midSlot,
}: {
  content: ArticleContent | null | undefined;
  /** Slug passed to exercise blocks for backend checks. */
  articleSlug?: string;
  /** Conversion block inserted around the middle of the article. */
  midSlot?: ReactNode;
}) {
  if (!content || content.version !== 2 || !Array.isArray(content.blocks)) {
    return <UnknownBlock label="Контент статьи недоступен" />;
  }

  const blocks = content.blocks;
  if (blocks.length === 0) return null;

  const midIndex = Math.floor((blocks.length - 1) / 2);

  return (
    <div className="flex max-w-[68ch] flex-col gap-5">
      {blocks.map((block, index) => (
        <Fragment
          key={
            isRecord(block) && typeof block.id === "string"
              ? block.id
              : `block-${index}`
          }
        >
          <ArticleBlockView block={block} articleSlug={articleSlug} />
          {midSlot && index === midIndex ? midSlot : null}
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function ArticleBlockView({
  block,
  articleSlug,
}: {
  block: unknown;
  articleSlug?: string;
}) {
  if (!isRecord(block) || typeof block.type !== "string" || !isRecord(block.data)) {
    return <UnknownBlock label="Некорректный блок" />;
  }

  const typed = block as unknown as ArticleBlockV2;

  switch (typed.type) {
    case "rich_text":
      return <RichTextBlock block={typed} />;
    case "math":
      return <MathBlock block={typed} />;
    case "interactive_graph":
      return <InteractiveGraphBlock block={typed} />;
    case "single_choice":
      return <SingleChoiceBlock block={typed} articleSlug={articleSlug} />;
    case "numeric_input":
      return <NumericInputBlock block={typed} articleSlug={articleSlug} />;
    case "step_solution":
      return <StepSolutionBlock block={typed} />;
    case "hint":
      return <HintBlock block={typed} />;
    case "callout":
      return <CalloutBlock block={typed} />;
    case "image":
      return <ImageBlock block={typed} />;
    case "divider":
      return <DividerBlock block={typed} />;
    default: {
      const unknownType = (block as { type?: unknown }).type;
      return (
        <UnknownBlock label={`Блок «${String(unknownType)}» пока не поддерживается`} />
      );
    }
  }
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/inline-nodes.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { Math } from "@/components/ui/math";
import { isExternalHref, isRecord, safeHref } from "@/features/articles/runtime";

/**
 * Renders structured inline nodes as React elements. Article-authored HTML is
 * never injected: every branch below produces a React element and text is
 * escaped by React automatically.
 */
export function InlineNodes({
  nodes,
}: {
  nodes: readonly unknown[] | undefined;
}): ReactNode {
  if (!Array.isArray(nodes) || nodes.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <>
      {nodes.map((node, index) => (
        <InlineNodeView key={index} node={node} />
      ))}
    </>
  );
}

function InlineNodeView({ node }: { node: unknown }): ReactNode {
  if (!isRecord(node) || typeof node.type !== "string") return null;

  switch (node.type) {
    case "text":
      return typeof node.text === "string" ? node.text : null;
    case "strong":
      return (
        <strong className="text-ink font-semibold">
          <InlineNodes nodes={node.children as readonly unknown[] | undefined} />
        </strong>
      );
    case "em":
      return (
        <em>
          <InlineNodes nodes={node.children as readonly unknown[] | undefined} />
        </em>
      );
    case "code":
      return (
        <code className="bg-surface-subtle rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.9em]">
          {typeof node.text === "string" ? node.text : ""}
        </code>
      );
    case "link": {
      const children = (
        <InlineNodes nodes={node.children as readonly unknown[] | undefined} />
      );
      const href = safeHref(node.href);
      if (!href) return <span>{children}</span>;
      const external = isExternalHref(href);
      return (
        <a
          href={href}
          className="text-accent hover:text-accent-strong underline underline-offset-4"
          {...(external ? { target: "_blank", rel: "noopener noreferrer" } : {})}
        >
          {children}
        </a>
      );
    }
    case "math":
      return <Math tex={typeof node.tex === "string" ? node.tex : ""} />;
    case "br":
      return <br />;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/unknown-block.tsx

/** Controlled fallback for unknown or malformed blocks. */
export function UnknownBlock({ label }: { label: string }) {
  return (
    <div
      role="note"
      className="border-line bg-surface-subtle text-ink-soft rounded-lg border border-dashed px-4 py-3 text-sm"
    >
      <p className="text-ink font-medium">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-1">
        Этот блок не поддерживается текущей версией страницы. Остальная статья доступна.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/callout-block.tsx

import { InlineNodes } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/inline-nodes";
import { asArray, asString } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type { ArticleBlockV2, CalloutKind } from "@/features/articles/types";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

type Block = Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "callout" }>;

const CALLOUT_LABEL: Record<CalloutKind, string> = {
  info: "К сведению",
  tip: "Совет",
  warning: "Важно",
  danger: "Осторожно",
};

const CALLOUT_STYLES: Record<CalloutKind, string> = {
  info: "border-accent/25 bg-accent-soft/60",
  tip: "border-success/30 bg-success-soft",
  warning: "border-warning/40 bg-warning/10",
  danger: "border-danger/30 bg-danger-soft",
};

function asCalloutKind(value: unknown): CalloutKind {
  return value === "tip" || value === "warning" || value === "danger" ? value : "info";
}

export function CalloutBlock({ block }: { block: Block }) {
  const kind = asCalloutKind(block.data.kind);
  const title = asString(block.data.title, CALLOUT_LABEL[kind]);
  const body = asArray(block.data.body);

  return (
    <aside
      role="note"
      className={cn("rounded-xl border px-5 py-4", CALLOUT_STYLES[kind])}
    >
      <p className="text-ink text-sm font-semibold">{title}</p>
      <div className="text-ink-soft mt-2 text-base leading-relaxed">
        <InlineNodes nodes={body} />
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/client-inline-nodes.tsx

"use client";

import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { isExternalHref, isRecord, safeHref } from "@/features/articles/runtime";

/**
 * Client-side renderer for exercise explanations returned after a correct
 * answer. KaTeX is loaded lazily (only when an explanation actually contains a
 * formula), so the main article bundle never includes the math engine. KaTeX's
 * output is the only HTML injected with `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`.
 */
const ClientMath = dynamic<{ tex: string; display?: boolean; className?: string }>(
  () => import("@/components/ui/math").then((module) => module.Math),
  { ssr: false, loading: () => <span aria-hidden="true">…</span> },
);

export function ClientInlineNodes({
  nodes,
}: {
  nodes: readonly unknown[] | undefined;
}): ReactNode {
  if (!Array.isArray(nodes) || nodes.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <>
      {nodes.map((node, index) => (
        <ClientInlineNodeView key={index} node={node} />
      ))}
    </>
  );
}

function ClientInlineNodeView({ node }: { node: unknown }): ReactNode {
  if (!isRecord(node) || typeof node.type !== "string") return null;

  switch (node.type) {
    case "text":
      return typeof node.text === "string" ? node.text : null;
    case "strong":
      return (
        <strong className="text-ink font-semibold">
          <ClientInlineNodes nodes={node.children as readonly unknown[] | undefined} />
        </strong>
      );
    case "em":
      return (
        <em>
          <ClientInlineNodes nodes={node.children as readonly unknown[] | undefined} />
        </em>
      );
    case "code":
      return (
        <code className="bg-surface-subtle rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.9em]">
          {typeof node.text === "string" ? node.text : ""}
        </code>
      );
    case "link": {
      const children = (
        <ClientInlineNodes nodes={node.children as readonly unknown[] | undefined} />
      );
      const href = safeHref(node.href);
      if (!href) return <span>{children}</span>;
      const external = isExternalHref(href);
      return (
        <a
          href={href}
          className="text-accent hover:text-accent-strong underline underline-offset-4"
          {...(external ? { target: "_blank", rel: "noopener noreferrer" } : {})}
        >
          {children}
        </a>
      );
    }
    case "math":
      return <ClientMath tex={typeof node.tex === "string" ? node.tex : ""} />;
    case "br":
      return <br />;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

import type { ArticleBlockV2 } from "@/features/articles/types";
