FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/exercise-form.test.tsx

import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

vi.mock("@/features/articles/exercises-api", () => ({
  checkArticleExercise: vi.fn(),
}));

import { NumericInputBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/numeric-input-block";
import { SingleChoiceBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/single-choice-block";
import {
  checkArticleExercise,
  type ExerciseCheckResult,
} from "@/features/articles/exercises-api";
import type { ArticleBlockV2 } from "@/features/articles/types";
import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";

const mockedCheck = vi.mocked(checkArticleExercise);

function choiceBlock(): Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "single_choice" }> {
  return {
    id: "ex-choice",
    type: "single_choice",
    data: {
      prompt: [{ type: "text", text: "2 + 2?" }],
      options: [
        { id: "a", label: [{ type: "text", text: "4" }] },
        { id: "b", label: [{ type: "text", text: "5" }] },
      ],
    },
  };
}

function numericBlock(): Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "numeric_input" }> {
  return {
    id: "ex-number",
    type: "numeric_input",
    data: {
      prompt: [{ type: "text", text: "3.5" }],
      unit: "см",
    },
  };
}

function result(overrides: Partial<ExerciseCheckResult> = {}): ExerciseCheckResult {
  return {
    isCorrect: true,
    checkedAt: "2026-10-19T00:00:00Z",
    explanation: null,
    attempts: 1,
    solved: false,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.resetAllMocks();
});

describe("exercise blocks", () => {
  it("checks a selected option and shows the explanation", async () => {
    mockedCheck.mockResolvedValue(
      result({
        explanation: [{ type: "text", text: "2 + 2 = 4" }],
        solved: true,
      }),
    );

    render(<SingleChoiceBlock block={choiceBlock()} articleSlug="demo" />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "4" }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));

    await screen.findByText("Верно.");
    expect(mockedCheck).toHaveBeenCalledWith("demo", "ex-choice", "a");
    expect(screen.getByText("2 + 2 = 4")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Прогресс сохранён.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Попытка 1")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("allows retrying after a wrong answer", async () => {
    mockedCheck.mockResolvedValue(
      result({ isCorrect: false, attempts: 1, solved: false }),
    );

    render(<SingleChoiceBlock block={choiceBlock()} articleSlug="demo" />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "5" }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));

    await screen.findByText("Пока неверно. Попробуйте ещё раз.");
    expect(screen.queryByText("Объяснение")).toBeNull();

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Попробовать снова" }));
    expect(screen.queryByText("Пока неверно. Попробуйте ещё раз.")).toBeNull();
    expect((screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "5" }) as HTMLInputElement).checked).toBe(
      false,
    );
  });

  it("checks a numeric answer and shows the explanation", async () => {
    mockedCheck.mockResolvedValue(
      result({ explanation: [{ type: "text", text: "Ответ 3.5" }] }),
    );

    render(<NumericInputBlock block={numericBlock()} articleSlug="demo" />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole("textbox"), {
      target: { value: "3,5" },
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));

    await screen.findByText("Верно.");
    expect(mockedCheck).toHaveBeenCalledWith("demo", "ex-number", "3,5");
    expect(screen.getByText("Ответ 3.5")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows an accessible error when the check fails", async () => {
    mockedCheck.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("network", "down"));

    render(<NumericInputBlock block={numericBlock()} articleSlug="demo" />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole("textbox"), {
      target: { value: "3.5" },
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(/Нет связи с сервером/);
  });

  it("disables checking when no article slug is available", () => {
    render(<SingleChoiceBlock block={choiceBlock()} />);

    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText(/доступна только на странице статьи/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("disables the submit while a check is in flight", async () => {
    let resolveCheck!: (value: ExerciseCheckResult) => void;
    mockedCheck.mockReturnValue(
      new Promise<ExerciseCheckResult>((resolve) => {
        resolveCheck = resolve;
      }),
    );

    render(<SingleChoiceBlock block={choiceBlock()} articleSlug="demo" />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "4" }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));

    const pending = await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Проверяем…" });
    expect(pending).toBeDisabled();

    resolveCheck(result({ solved: true }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("Верно.")).toBeInTheDocument());
  });
});




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/exercise-form.tsx

"use client";

import { useId, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { ClientInlineNodes } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/client-inline-nodes";
import { checkArticleExercise } from "@/features/articles/exercises-api";
import type { ExerciseCheckResult } from "@/features/articles/exercises-api";
import { describeApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

type Status = "idle" | "checking" | "correct" | "wrong";

export type ExerciseFormProps = {
  articleSlug?: string;
  exerciseId: string;
  kind: "choice" | "numeric";
  name: string;
  inputId?: string;
  unit?: string;
  /** Server-rendered prompt (and options for choice exercises). */
  children: ReactNode;
};

export function ExerciseForm({
  articleSlug,
  exerciseId,
  kind,
  name,
  inputId,
  unit,
  children,
}: ExerciseFormProps) {
  const formRef = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null);
  const statusId = useId();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("idle");
  const [explanation, setExplanation] = useState<unknown[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [attempts, setAttempts] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [numericValue, setNumericValue] = useState("");
  const [solved, setSolved] = useState<boolean | null>(null);

  const configured = Boolean(articleSlug?.trim());
  const disabled = status === "checking" || status === "correct";

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (disabled || !configured || !articleSlug) return;

    let answer = "";
    if (kind === "choice") {
      const data = formRef.current ? new FormData(formRef.current) : null;
      answer = String(data?.get(name) ?? "").trim();
    } else {
      answer = numericValue.trim();
    }

    if (!answer) {
      setStatus("idle");
      setError("Выберите или введите ответ.");
      return;
    }

    setStatus("checking");
    setError(null);
    setExplanation(null);

    try {
      const result: ExerciseCheckResult = await checkArticleExercise(
        articleSlug,
        exerciseId,
        answer,
      );
      setStatus(result.isCorrect ? "correct" : "wrong");
      setExplanation(result.explanation);
      setAttempts(result.attempts ?? (attempts ?? 0) + 1);
      setSolved(result.solved);
    } catch (caught) {
      setStatus("idle");
      setError(describeApiError(caught));
    }
  }

  function reset() {
    setStatus("idle");
    setExplanation(null);
    setError(null);
    setSolved(null);
    setNumericValue("");
    formRef.current?.reset();
  }

  const statusMessage =
    status === "checking"
      ? "Проверяем…"
      : status === "correct"
        ? "Верно."
        : status === "wrong"
          ? "Пока неверно. Попробуйте ещё раз."
          : "";

  return (
    <form ref={formRef} onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
      {children}

      {kind === "numeric" ? (
        <div className="mt-4">
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="text-ink-soft block text-sm font-medium">
            Ваш ответ{unit ? `, ${unit}` : ""}
          </label>
          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3">
            <Input
              id={inputId}
              name={name}
              type="text"
              inputMode="decimal"
              autoComplete="off"
              value={numericValue}
              onChange={(event) => setNumericValue(event.target.value)}
              disabled={disabled}
              aria-invalid={status === "wrong"}
              aria-describedby={statusId}
              className="max-w-48"
            />
            {unit ? <span className="text-ink-soft text-sm">{unit}</span> : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <Button type="submit" disabled={disabled || !configured}>
          {status === "checking" ? "Проверяем…" : "Проверить"}
        </Button>
        {status === "wrong" || status === "correct" ? (
          <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={reset}>
            Попробовать снова
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {attempts !== null ? (
          <span className="text-ink-faint text-sm tabular-nums">
            Попытка {attempts}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <p
        id={statusId}
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
        className={cn(
          "mt-3 min-h-6 text-sm",
          status === "correct" && "text-success font-medium",
          status === "wrong" && "text-danger",
          status === "checking" && "text-ink-faint",
        )}
      >
        {statusMessage}
      </p>

      {!configured ? (
        <p className="text-ink-faint mt-2 text-sm">
          Проверка ответа доступна только на странице статьи с подключённым API.
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {error ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-danger mt-2 text-sm">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {status === "correct" && explanation ? (
        <div className="border-line bg-surface-subtle mt-4 rounded-md border px-4 py-3">
          <p className="text-ink text-sm font-medium">Объяснение</p>
          <div className="text-ink-soft mt-2 text-base leading-relaxed">
            <ClientInlineNodes nodes={explanation} />
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {solved === true ? (
        <p className="text-success mt-2 text-sm">Прогресс сохранён.</p>
      ) : null}
    </form>
  );
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/hint-block.tsx

import { InlineNodes } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/inline-nodes";
import { asArray, asString } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type { ArticleBlockV2 } from "@/features/articles/types";

type Block = Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "hint" }>;

export function HintBlock({ block }: { block: Block }) {
  const title = asString(block.data.title, "Подсказка");
  const body = asArray(block.data.body);

  return (
    <details className="border-line bg-surface-subtle/60 rounded-md border px-4 py-3">
      <summary className="text-ink cursor-pointer text-sm font-medium">{title}</summary>
      <div className="text-ink-soft mt-3 text-base leading-relaxed">
        <InlineNodes nodes={body} />
      </div>
    </details>
  );
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/image-block.tsx

import { UnknownBlock } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/unknown-block";
import { safeHref } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type { ArticleBlockV2 } from "@/features/articles/types";

type Block = Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "image" }>;

export function ImageBlock({ block }: { block: Block }) {
  const src = safeHref(block.data.src);
  if (!src) {
    return <UnknownBlock label="Изображение недоступно" />;
  }

  const alt = typeof block.data.alt === "string" ? block.data.alt : "";
  const caption = typeof block.data.caption === "string" ? block.data.caption : "";
  const width =
    typeof block.data.width === "number" && block.data.width > 0
      ? block.data.width
      : undefined;
  const height =
    typeof block.data.height === "number" && block.data.height > 0
      ? block.data.height
      : undefined;

  return (
    <figure className="m-0 flex flex-col gap-2">
      {/* The content contract allows arbitrary HTTPS sources; image optimisation
          domains are not configured for the site, so a plain <img> is used. */}
      {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
      <img
        src={src}
        alt={alt}
        width={width}
        height={height}
        loading="lazy"
        decoding="async"
        className="border-line h-auto max-w-full rounded-lg border"
      />
      {caption ? (
        <figcaption className="text-ink-faint text-sm">{caption}</figcaption>
      ) : null}
    </figure>
  );
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/interactive-graph-block.tsx

import { InteractiveGraphLoader } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/interactive-graph-loader";
import { asArray, asString } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type { ArticleBlockV2, GraphExpression } from "@/features/articles/types";

type Block = Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "interactive_graph" }>;

export function InteractiveGraphBlock({ block }: { block: Block }) {
  const expressions = asArray<GraphExpression>(block.data.expressions);
  const labels = expressions.map((expression, index) =>
    asString(expression.label, asString(expression.id, `f${index + 1}`)),
  );

  return (
    <figure className="border-line bg-surface m-0 rounded-xl border p-4 md:p-6">
      <figcaption className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="text-ink text-sm font-medium">Интерактивный график</span>
        {labels.length > 0 ? (
          <span className="text-ink-faint text-sm">{labels.join(" · ")}</span>
        ) : null}
      </figcaption>

      <InteractiveGraphLoader data={block.data} />

      <p className="text-ink-faint mt-3 text-sm">
        Настройте параметры — график перестроится локально. Выражения ограничены
        разрешённой грамматикой; произвольный код не выполняется.
      </p>
    </figure>
  );
}

FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/interactive-graph.test.tsx

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { InteractiveGraph } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/interactive-graph";
import type { InteractiveGraphBlockData } from "@/features/articles/types";

function makeData(
  overrides: Partial<InteractiveGraphBlockData> = {},
): InteractiveGraphBlockData {
  return {
    engine: "svg",
    expressions: [{ id: "f", expr: "a*x", label: "f(x)" }],
    parameters: [{ id: "a", label: "a", min: 0, max: 2, step: 0.5, default: 1 }],
    viewport: { x_min: -5, x_max: 5, y_min: -5, y_max: 5 },
    interactions: ["zoom", "trace"],
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("InteractiveGraph", () => {
  it("renders curves and updates them when a parameter changes", () => {
    render(<InteractiveGraph data={makeData()} />);

    const before = document.querySelector("path[data-graph-curve]")?.getAttribute("d");
    expect(before).toBeTruthy();

    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole("slider", { name: "Параметр a" }), {
      target: { value: "2" },
    });

    const after = document.querySelector("path[data-graph-curve]")?.getAttribute("d");
    expect(after).not.toBe(before);
  });

  it("shows a trace readout for pointer moves", () => {
    render(<InteractiveGraph data={makeData()} />);

    const svg = screen.getByRole("img", {
      name: /Интерактивный график/,
    });
    vi.spyOn(svg, "getBoundingClientRect").mockReturnValue({
      width: 480,
      height: 280,
      left: 0,
      top: 0,
      right: 480,
      bottom: 280,
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      toJSON: () => ({}),
    } as DOMRect);

    fireEvent.pointerMove(svg, { clientX: 240, clientY: 140 });

    expect(screen.getByText(/x = 0/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("isolates a broken expression without dropping the valid one", () => {
    render(
      <InteractiveGraph
        data={makeData({
          expressions: [
            { id: "bad", expr: "alert(1)", label: "bad" },
            { id: "good", expr: "x", label: "good" },
          ],
        })}
      />,
    );

    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(/bad/);
    expect(document.querySelector("path[data-graph-curve]")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("exposes accessible controls", () => {
    render(<InteractiveGraph data={makeData()} />);

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("img", { name: /Интерактивный график/ }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("slider", { name: "Параметр a" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Приблизить график" }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Отдалить график" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/interactive-graph.tsx

"use client";

import {
  Component,
  useId,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type PointerEvent,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";

import { asArray } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type {
  GraphExpression,
  GraphParameter,
  GraphViewport,
  InteractiveGraphBlockData,
} from "@/features/articles/types";

import {
  compileExpression,
  type CompiledExpression,
} from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/graph/expression";
import {
  buildPath,
  mapX,
  mapY,
  sampleValue,
  type PlotLayout,
} from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/graph/plot";

const WIDTH = 480;
const HEIGHT = 280;
const PADDING = 32;
const LAYOUT: PlotLayout = { width: WIDTH, height: HEIGHT, padding: PADDING };
const DEFAULT_VIEWPORT: GraphViewport = {
  x_min: -5,
  x_max: 5,
  y_min: -5,
  y_max: 5,
};
const PALETTE = ["#1f6feb", "#c83b3b", "#208a5a", "#b7791f", "#7c3aed"];

type CompiledItem = {
  expression: GraphExpression;
  compiled: CompiledExpression | null;
  error: string | null;
  color: string;
};

function finite(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : fallback;
}

function normalizeViewport(value: unknown): GraphViewport {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return DEFAULT_VIEWPORT;
  const raw = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const xMin = finite(raw.x_min, DEFAULT_VIEWPORT.x_min);
  const xMax = finite(raw.x_max, DEFAULT_VIEWPORT.x_max);
  const yMin = finite(raw.y_min, DEFAULT_VIEWPORT.y_min);
  const yMax = finite(raw.y_max, DEFAULT_VIEWPORT.y_max);
  if (xMin >= xMax || yMin >= yMax) return DEFAULT_VIEWPORT;
  return { x_min: xMin, x_max: xMax, y_min: yMin, y_max: yMax };
}

function ticks(min: number, max: number, count = 6): number[] {
  const step = (max - min) / count;
  return Array.from({ length: count + 1 }, (_, index) => min + step * index);
}

function formatNumber(value: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return "—";
  if (value !== 0 && (Math.abs(value) >= 1e6 || Math.abs(value) < 1e-4)) {
    return value.toExponential(2);
  }
  return Number.isInteger(value)
    ? String(value)
    : value.toFixed(2).replace(/0+$/, "").replace(/\.$/, "");
}

function colorFor(expression: GraphExpression, index: number): string {
  return typeof expression.color === "string" &&
    /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(expression.color)
    ? expression.color
    : PALETTE[index % PALETTE.length];
}

function initialValues(parameters: GraphParameter[]): Record<string, number> {
  const entries: Array<[string, number]> = [];
  for (const parameter of parameters) {
    if (typeof parameter.id !== "string" || parameter.id.length === 0) continue;
    if (
      !Number.isFinite(parameter.min) ||
      !Number.isFinite(parameter.max) ||
      parameter.min >= parameter.max
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    entries.push([
      parameter.id,
      Math.min(
        parameter.max,
        Math.max(parameter.min, finite(parameter.default, parameter.min)),
      ),
    ]);
  }
  return Object.fromEntries(entries);
}

class GraphErrorBoundary extends Component<
  { children: ReactNode },
  { failed: boolean }
> {
  state = { failed: false };

  static getDerivedStateFromError() {
    return { failed: true };
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.failed) {
      return (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="border-danger/30 bg-danger-soft text-ink-soft rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm"
        >
          Не удалось построить график. Остальная статья доступна.
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

export function InteractiveGraph({ data }: { data: InteractiveGraphBlockData }) {
  return (
    <GraphErrorBoundary>
      <InteractiveGraphContent data={data} />
    </GraphErrorBoundary>
  );
}

function InteractiveGraphContent({ data }: { data: InteractiveGraphBlockData }) {
  const clipId = `graph-clip-${useId().replace(/:/g, "")}`;
  const svgRef = useRef<SVGSVGElement>(null);

  const rawParameters = asArray<GraphParameter>(data.parameters);
  const initialViewport = useMemo(
    () => normalizeViewport(data.viewport),
    [data.viewport],
  );
  const [view, setView] = useState<GraphViewport>(initialViewport);
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, number>>(() =>
    initialValues(rawParameters),
  );
  const [traceX, setTraceX] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const interactions = asArray<string>(data.interactions);
  const traceEnabled = interactions.includes("trace");
  const zoomEnabled = interactions.includes("zoom");

  const parameterIds = useMemo(
    () =>
      asArray<GraphParameter>(data.parameters)
        .map((parameter) => parameter.id)
        .filter((id): id is string => typeof id === "string" && id.length > 0),
    [data.parameters],
  );

  const compiled = useMemo<CompiledItem[]>(() => {
    const parameterSet = new Set(parameterIds);
    return asArray<GraphExpression>(data.expressions).map((expression, index) => {
      try {
        return {
          expression,
          compiled: compileExpression(expression.expr, parameterSet),
          error: null,
          color: colorFor(expression, index),
        };
      } catch (error) {
        return {
          expression,
          compiled: null,
          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Ошибка выражения",
          color: colorFor(expression, index),
        };
      }
    });
  }, [data.expressions, parameterIds]);

  const paths = useMemo(
    () =>
      compiled.map((item) =>
        item.compiled ? buildPath(item.compiled, values, view, LAYOUT) : null,
      ),
    [compiled, values, view],
  );

  const traceValues = useMemo(() => {
    if (traceX === null) return [];
    return compiled.map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      value: item.compiled ? sampleValue(item.compiled, traceX, values) : null,
    }));
  }, [compiled, traceX, values]);

  const parameters = rawParameters.filter(
    (parameter) =>
      parameter &&
      typeof parameter.id === "string" &&
      Number.isFinite(parameter.min) &&
      Number.isFinite(parameter.max) &&
      parameter.min < parameter.max,
  );

  const xTicks = ticks(view.x_min, view.x_max);
  const yTicks = ticks(view.y_min, view.y_max);
  const axisY =
    view.y_min <= 0 && view.y_max >= 0 ? mapY(0, view, LAYOUT) : HEIGHT - PADDING;
  const axisX = view.x_min <= 0 && view.x_max >= 0 ? mapX(0, view, LAYOUT) : PADDING;
  const plotLeft = PADDING;
  const plotRight = WIDTH - PADDING;

  function handlePointerMove(event: PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) {
    if (!traceEnabled) return;
    const svg = svgRef.current;
    if (!svg) return;
    const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.width <= 0) return;

    const relativeX = ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * WIDTH;
    if (relativeX < plotLeft || relativeX > plotRight) {
      setTraceX(null);
      return;
    }

    const x =
      view.x_min +
      ((relativeX - plotLeft) / (plotRight - plotLeft)) * (view.x_max - view.x_min);
    setTraceX(x);
  }

  function handlePointerLeave() {
    setTraceX(null);
  }

  function zoom(factor: number) {
    const centerX = (view.x_min + view.x_max) / 2;
    const centerY = (view.y_min + view.y_max) / 2;
    const halfX = ((view.x_max - view.x_min) / 2) * factor;
    const halfY = ((view.y_max - view.y_min) / 2) * factor;
    if (halfX < 1e-4 || halfY < 1e-4 || halfX > 1e6 || halfY > 1e6) {
      return;
    }
    setView({
      x_min: centerX - halfX,
      x_max: centerX + halfX,
      y_min: centerY - halfY,
      y_max: centerY + halfY,
    });
    setTraceX(null);
  }

  function resetView() {
    setView(initialViewport);
    setTraceX(null);
  }

  const expressionErrors = compiled.filter((item) => item.error);
  const ariaLabel = compiled.length
    ? `Интерактивный график функций: ${compiled
        .map((item) => item.expression.label || item.expression.id)
        .join(", ")}`
    : "Интерактивный график";

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <svg
        ref={svgRef}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${WIDTH} ${HEIGHT}`}
        role="img"
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        focusable="false"
        className="border-line bg-canvas w-full rounded-lg border"
        style={{ touchAction: "pan-y" }}
        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
        onPointerLeave={handlePointerLeave}
      >
        <title>{ariaLabel}</title>
        <defs>
          <clipPath id={clipId}>
            <rect
              x={PADDING}
              y={PADDING}
              width={WIDTH - 2 * PADDING}
              height={HEIGHT - 2 * PADDING}
            />
          </clipPath>
        </defs>

        <g>
          {xTicks.map((tick) => (
            <line
              key={`x-${tick}`}
              x1={mapX(tick, view, LAYOUT)}
              y1={PADDING}
              x2={mapX(tick, view, LAYOUT)}
              y2={HEIGHT - PADDING}
              stroke="var(--color-line)"
              strokeOpacity={0.55}
              strokeWidth={1}
            />
          ))}
          {yTicks.map((tick) => (
            <line
              key={`y-${tick}`}
              x1={PADDING}
              y1={mapY(tick, view, LAYOUT)}
              x2={WIDTH - PADDING}
              y2={mapY(tick, view, LAYOUT)}
              stroke="var(--color-line)"
              strokeOpacity={0.55}
              strokeWidth={1}
            />
          ))}
        </g>

        <line
          x1={PADDING}
          y1={axisY}
          x2={WIDTH - PADDING}
          y2={axisY}
          stroke="var(--color-ink-faint)"
          strokeWidth={1}
        />
        <line
          x1={axisX}
          y1={PADDING}
          x2={axisX}
          y2={HEIGHT - PADDING}
          stroke="var(--color-ink-faint)"
          strokeWidth={1}
        />
        <text
          x={WIDTH - PADDING}
          y={axisY - 6}
          textAnchor="end"
          fontSize={11}
          fill="var(--color-ink-faint)"
        >
          x
        </text>
        <text x={axisX + 6} y={PADDING + 4} fontSize={11} fill="var(--color-ink-faint)">
          y
        </text>

        <g clipPath={`url(#${clipId})`}>
          {paths.map((path, index) =>
            path ? (
              <path
                key={compiled[index]?.expression.id ?? index}
                data-graph-curve=""
                d={path}
                fill="none"
                stroke={compiled[index]?.color ?? PALETTE[0]}
                strokeWidth={2}
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeLinejoin="round"
              />
            ) : null,
          )}
        </g>

        {traceX !== null ? (
          <g clipPath={`url(#${clipId})`}>
            <line
              x1={mapX(traceX, view, LAYOUT)}
              y1={PADDING}
              x2={mapX(traceX, view, LAYOUT)}
              y2={HEIGHT - PADDING}
              stroke="var(--color-ink-faint)"
              strokeDasharray="4 4"
              strokeWidth={1}
            />
            {traceValues.map((item, index) =>
              item.value === null ? null : (
                <circle
                  key={item.expression.id ?? index}
                  cx={mapX(traceX, view, LAYOUT)}
                  cy={mapY(item.value, view, LAYOUT)}
                  r={4}
                  fill={item.color}
                />
              ),
            )}
          </g>
        ) : null}
      </svg>

      {compiled.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm">
          {compiled.map((item, index) => (
            <span
              key={item.expression.id ?? index}
              className="text-ink-soft inline-flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="inline-block size-2 rounded-full"
                style={{ backgroundColor: item.color }}
              />
              {item.expression.label || item.expression.id}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div aria-live="polite" className="text-ink-soft min-h-6 text-sm tabular-nums">
        {traceX === null ? (
          traceEnabled ? (
            "Проведите по графику, чтобы увидеть значения."
          ) : null
        ) : (
          <span>
            x = {formatNumber(traceX)}
            {traceValues.map((item, index) => (
              <span key={item.expression.id ?? index}>
                {" · "}
                {item.expression.label || item.expression.id}: y ={" "}
                {item.value === null ? "—" : formatNumber(item.value)}
              </span>
            ))}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {zoomEnabled ? (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => zoom(0.8)}
            aria-label="Приблизить график"
            className="border-line-strong text-ink hover:bg-surface-subtle min-h-9 rounded-md border px-3 text-sm"
          >
            +
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => zoom(1.25)}
            aria-label="Отдалить график"
            className="border-line-strong text-ink hover:bg-surface-subtle min-h-9 rounded-md border px-3 text-sm"
          >
            −
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={resetView}
            className="border-line-strong text-ink hover:bg-surface-subtle min-h-9 rounded-md border px-3 text-sm"
          >
            Сбросить вид
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {parameters.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {parameters.map((parameter) => {
            const value =
              values[parameter.id] ?? finite(parameter.default, parameter.min);
            const step =
              Number.isFinite(parameter.step) && parameter.step > 0
                ? parameter.step
                : 0.1;
            return (
              <label key={parameter.id} className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
                <span className="text-ink-soft flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                  <span>{parameter.label || parameter.id}</span>
                  <span className="tabular-nums">{formatNumber(value)}</span>
                </span>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={parameter.min}
                  max={parameter.max}
                  step={step}
                  value={value}
                  aria-label={`Параметр ${parameter.label || parameter.id}`}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    setValues((previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      [parameter.id]: Number(event.target.value),
                    }));
                    setTraceX(null);
                  }}
                  className="accent-accent"
                />
              </label>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {expressionErrors.length > 0 ? (
        <ul className="text-danger text-sm" role="alert">
          {expressionErrors.map((item, index) => (
            <li key={item.expression.id ?? index}>
              {item.expression.label || item.expression.id}: {item.error}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

type Block = Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "math" }>;

export function MathBlock({ block }: { block: Block }) {
  const { tex, display, caption } = block.data;

  if (typeof tex !== "string" || tex.trim() === "") {
    return <UnknownBlock label="Формула недоступна" />;

      ) : null}
    </figure>
  );
}

FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/blocks/rich-text-block.tsx

import { InlineNodes } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/inline-nodes";
import { asArray } from "@/features/articles/runtime";
import type { ArticleBlockV2, InlineNode } from "@/features/articles/types";

type Block = Extract<ArticleBlockV2, { type: "rich_text" }>;

export function RichTextBlock({ block }: { block: Block }) {
  const { kind, children, level, ordered, items } = block.data;

  if (kind === "heading") {
    const Tag = level === 3 ? "h3" : "h2";
    const className =
      level === 3
        ? "text-ink mt-6 scroll-mt-24 text-2xl font-medium tracking-[-0.02em] text-balance"
        : "heading-3 mt-6 scroll-mt-24 text-balance";
    return (
      <Tag id={block.id} className={className}>
        <InlineNodes nodes={children} />
      </Tag>
    );
  }

  if (kind === "list") {
    const listItems = asArray<InlineNode[]>(items);
    const Tag = ordered ? "ol" : "ul";
    const className = ordered
      ? "text-ink-soft m-0 list-decimal space-y-2 pl-6 text-lg"
      : "text-ink-soft m-0 list-disc space-y-2 pl-6 text-lg";

    return (
      <Tag className={className}>
        {listItems.map((item, index) => (
          <li key={index}>
            <InlineNodes nodes={asArray<InlineNode>(item)} />
          </li>
        ))}
      </Tag>
    );
  }

  return (
    <p className="text-ink-soft text-lg leading-relaxed">
      <InlineNodes nodes={children} />
    </p>
  );
}

                  <InlineNodes nodes={asArray(option.label)} />
                </span>
              </label>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </fieldset>
    </ExerciseForm>
  );
}

                    tex={step.math.tex}
                  />
                ) : null}
              </div>
            </details>
          </li>
        ))}

    </section>
  );
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/graph/expression.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import {
  compileExpression,
  ExpressionError,
} from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/graph/expression";

function evaluate(
  expr: string,
  x: number,
  parameters: Record<string, number> = {},
  parameterIds: string[] = [],
): number {
  return compileExpression(expr, parameterIds).evaluate(x, parameters);
}

describe("compileExpression", () => {
  it("respects precedence and right-associative powers", () => {
    expect(evaluate("1 + 2 * 3", 0)).toBe(7);
    expect(evaluate("2 ^ 3 ^ 2", 0)).toBe(512);
    expect(evaluate("-2 ^ 2", 0)).toBe(-4);
    expect(evaluate("(1 + 2) * 3", 0)).toBe(9);
  });

  it("supports constants, functions and parameters", () => {
    expect(evaluate("sin(pi / 2)", 0)).toBeCloseTo(1);
    expect(evaluate("sqrt(9) + abs(-2)", 0)).toBe(5);
    expect(evaluate("ln(e)", 0)).toBeCloseTo(1);
    expect(evaluate("log2(8) + log10(100)", 0)).toBe(5);
    expect(evaluate("a * x ^ 2 + b", 2, { a: 1, b: 3 }, ["a", "b"])).toBe(7);
  });

  it("supports implicit multiplication", () => {
    expect(evaluate("2x", 3)).toBe(6);
    expect(evaluate("2(x + 1)", 3)).toBe(8);
    expect(evaluate("(x + 1)(x - 1)", 3)).toBe(8);
    expect(evaluate("2sin(pi / 2)", 0)).toBeCloseTo(2);
  });

  it("rejects unknown identifiers and arbitrary code", () => {
    expect(() => compileExpression("alert(1)")).toThrow(ExpressionError);
    expect(() => compileExpression("globalThis.__x")).toThrow(ExpressionError);
    expect(() => compileExpression("x; alert(1)")).toThrow(ExpressionError);
    expect(() => compileExpression("a", [])).toThrow(ExpressionError);
    expect(() => compileExpression("", [])).toThrow(ExpressionError);
  });

  it("rejects malformed expressions", () => {
    expect(() => compileExpression("1 +")).toThrow(ExpressionError);
    expect(() => compileExpression("(1 + 2", [])).toThrow(ExpressionError);
    expect(() => compileExpression("1 2 )", [])).toThrow(ExpressionError);
    expect(() => compileExpression("x".repeat(513), [])).toThrow(ExpressionError);
  });

  it("returns non-finite values for division by zero (plot skips them)", () => {
    expect(Number.isFinite(evaluate("1 / 0", 0))).toBe(false);
  });
});




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/graph/expression.ts

/**
 * Safe evaluator for the declarative graph grammar.
 *
 * Only the operators/functions allowed by the article contract are parsed. There
 * is no `eval`, `new Function`, property access or arbitrary code: unknown
 * identifiers and characters are rejected before evaluation.
 */

export class ExpressionError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ExpressionError";
  }
}

const FUNCTION_NAMES = new Set([
  "sin",
  "cos",
  "tan",
  "asin",
  "acos",
  "atan",
  "exp",
  "ln",
  "log2",
  "log10",
  "sqrt",
  "abs",
]);

const CONSTANTS: Record<string, number> = {
  pi: Math.PI,
  e: Math.E,
};

const FUNCTIONS: Record<string, (value: number) => number> = {
  sin: Math.sin,
  cos: Math.cos,
  tan: Math.tan,
  asin: Math.asin,
  acos: Math.acos,
  atan: Math.atan,
  exp: Math.exp,
  ln: Math.log,
  log2: Math.log2,
  log10: Math.log10,
  sqrt: Math.sqrt,
  abs: Math.abs,
};

type Token =
  | { kind: "number"; value: number }
  | { kind: "identifier"; value: string }
  | { kind: "operator"; value: "+" | "-" | "*" | "/" | "^" }
  | { kind: "paren"; value: "(" | ")" };

type Node =
  | { kind: "number"; value: number }
  | { kind: "constant"; name: string }
  | { kind: "variable"; name: string }
  | { kind: "negate"; operand: Node }
  | {
      kind: "binary";
      op: "+" | "-" | "*" | "/" | "^";
      left: Node;
      right: Node;
    }
  | { kind: "call"; name: string; argument: Node };

const TOKEN_PATTERN = /\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?|[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*|[+\-*/^()])/g;
const MAX_EXPRESSION_LENGTH = 512;

function tokenize(expression: string): Token[] {
  const tokens: Token[] = [];
  let index = 0;

  while (index < expression.length) {
    if (/\s/.test(expression[index])) {
      index += 1;
      continue;
    }

    TOKEN_PATTERN.lastIndex = index;
    const match = TOKEN_PATTERN.exec(expression);
    if (!match || match.index !== index) {
      throw new ExpressionError(
        `Неподдерживаемый символ в выражении: «${expression[index]}»`,
      );
    }

    const raw = match[1];
    if (/^\d/.test(raw)) {
      tokens.push({ kind: "number", value: Number(raw) });
    } else if (/^[A-Za-z_]/.test(raw)) {
      tokens.push({ kind: "identifier", value: raw });
    } else if (raw === "(" || raw === ")") {
      tokens.push({ kind: "paren", value: raw });
    } else {
      tokens.push({
        kind: "operator",
        value: raw as "+" | "-" | "*" | "/" | "^",
      });
    }

    index = TOKEN_PATTERN.lastIndex;
  }

  return tokens;
}

class Parser {
  private position = 0;

  constructor(
    private readonly tokens: Token[],
    private readonly parameters: Set<string>,
  ) {}

  parse(): Node {
    const node = this.parseExpression();
    if (this.position < this.tokens.length) {
      throw new ExpressionError("Лишние символы в конце выражения");
    }
    return node;
  }

  private peek(): Token | undefined {
    return this.tokens[this.position];
  }

  private consume(): Token | undefined {
    const token = this.tokens[this.position];
    this.position += 1;
    return token;
  }

  private startsPrimary(token: Token | undefined): boolean {
    if (!token) return false;
    return (
      token.kind === "number" ||
      token.kind === "identifier" ||
      (token.kind === "paren" && token.value === "(")
    );
  }

  private parseExpression(): Node {
    let node = this.parseTerm();
    while (true) {
      const token = this.peek();
      if (token?.kind === "operator" && (token.value === "+" || token.value === "-")) {
        this.consume();
        node = {
          kind: "binary",
          op: token.value,
          left: node,
          right: this.parseTerm(),
        };
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
    return node;
  }

  private parseTerm(): Node {
    let node = this.parseUnary();
    while (true) {
      const token = this.peek();
      if (token?.kind === "operator" && (token.value === "*" || token.value === "/")) {
        this.consume();
        node = {
          kind: "binary",
          op: token.value,
          left: node,
          right: this.parseUnary(),
        };
      } else if (this.startsPrimary(token)) {
        // Implicit multiplication: 2x, 2(x+1), (x+1)(x-1).
        node = {
          kind: "binary",
          op: "*",
          left: node,
          right: this.parseUnary(),
        };
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
    return node;
  }

  private parseUnary(): Node {
    const token = this.peek();
    if (token?.kind === "operator" && (token.value === "+" || token.value === "-")) {
      this.consume();
      const operand = this.parseUnary();
      return token.value === "-" ? { kind: "negate", operand } : operand;
    }
    return this.parsePower();
  }

  private parsePower(): Node {
    let node = this.parsePrimary();
    const token = this.peek();
    if (token?.kind === "operator" && token.value === "^") {
      this.consume();
      node = {
        kind: "binary",
        op: "^",
        left: node,
        right: this.parseUnary(),
      };
    }
    return node;
  }

  private parsePrimary(): Node {
    const token = this.consume();
    if (!token) throw new ExpressionError("Неожиданный конец выражения");

    if (token.kind === "number") {
      return { kind: "number", value: token.value };
    }

    if (token.kind === "identifier") {
      const name = token.value;

      if (FUNCTION_NAMES.has(name)) {
        const open = this.consume();
        if (!open || open.kind !== "paren" || open.value !== "(") {
          throw new ExpressionError(`После функции ${name} ожидается «(»`);
        }
        const argument = this.parseExpression();
        const close = this.consume();
        if (!close || close.kind !== "paren" || close.value !== ")") {
          throw new ExpressionError("Не закрыта скобка");
        }
        return { kind: "call", name, argument };
      }

      if (name in CONSTANTS) {
        return { kind: "constant", name };
      }

      if (name === "x" || this.parameters.has(name)) {
        return { kind: "variable", name };
      }

      throw new ExpressionError(`Неизвестная переменная: ${name}`);
    }

    if (token.kind === "paren" && token.value === "(") {
      const node = this.parseExpression();
      const close = this.consume();
      if (!close || close.kind !== "paren" || close.value !== ")") {
        throw new ExpressionError("Не закрыта скобка");
      }
      return node;
    }

    throw new ExpressionError("Ожидалось число, переменная или «(»");
  }
}

function parameterValue(
  parameters: Record<string, number>,
  name: string,
): number | undefined {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parameters, name)
    ? parameters[name]
    : undefined;
}

function evaluateNode(
  node: Node,
  x: number,
  parameters: Record<string, number>,
): number {
  switch (node.kind) {
    case "number":
      return node.value;
    case "constant":
      return CONSTANTS[node.name];
    case "variable": {
      if (node.name === "x") return x;
      const value = parameterValue(parameters, node.name);
      if (value === undefined) {
        throw new ExpressionError(`Нет значения параметра: ${node.name}`);
      }
      return value;
    }
    case "negate":
      return -evaluateNode(node.operand, x, parameters);
    case "binary": {
      const left = evaluateNode(node.left, x, parameters);
      const right = evaluateNode(node.right, x, parameters);
      switch (node.op) {
        case "+":
          return left + right;
        case "-":
          return left - right;
        case "*":
          return left * right;
        case "/":
          return left / right;
        case "^":
          return left ** right;
      }
      break;
    }
    case "call":
      return FUNCTIONS[node.name](evaluateNode(node.argument, x, parameters));
  }

  throw new ExpressionError("Некорректное выражение");
}

export type CompiledExpression = {
  evaluate: (x: number, parameters: Record<string, number>) => number;
};

export function compileExpression(
  expression: string,
  parameterIds: Iterable<string> = [],
): CompiledExpression {
  if (typeof expression !== "string" || expression.trim() === "") {
    throw new ExpressionError("Пустое выражение");
  }
  if (expression.length > MAX_EXPRESSION_LENGTH) {
    throw new ExpressionError("Выражение слишком длинное");
  }

  const tokens = tokenize(expression);
  const ast = new Parser(tokens, new Set(parameterIds)).parse();

  return {
    evaluate: (x, parameters) => evaluateNode(ast, x, parameters),
  };
}




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/graph/plot.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { GraphViewport } from "@/features/articles/types";

import { compileExpression } from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/graph/expression";
import {
  buildPath,
  mapX,
  mapY,
  sampleValue,
  type PlotLayout,
} from "@/features/articles/components/renderer/graph/plot";

const viewport: GraphViewport = {
  x_min: -5,
  x_max: 5,
  y_min: -5,
  y_max: 5,
};
const layout: PlotLayout = { width: 480, height: 280, padding: 32 };

describe("plot", () => {
  it("maps viewport corners to the padded plot area", () => {
    expect(mapX(-5, viewport, layout)).toBe(32);
    expect(mapX(5, viewport, layout)).toBe(448);
    expect(mapY(-5, viewport, layout)).toBe(248);
    expect(mapY(5, viewport, layout)).toBe(32);
  });

  it("builds an SVG path for a function", () => {
    const path = buildPath(compileExpression("x^2"), {}, viewport, layout, 20);
    expect(path).toContain("M");
    expect(path).toContain("L");
  });

  it("starts new segments at asymptotes", () => {
    const path = buildPath(compileExpression("1 / x"), {}, viewport, layout, 200);
    expect(path).not.toBeNull();
    expect((path?.match(/M/g) ?? []).length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
  });

  it("returns null for an invalid viewport", () => {
    expect(
      buildPath(
        compileExpression("x"),
        {},
        { x_min: 1, x_max: 1, y_min: -1, y_max: 1 },
        layout,
      ),
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  it("samples values and returns null outside the domain", () => {
    const sqrt = compileExpression("sqrt(x)");
    expect(sampleValue(sqrt, 4, {})).toBe(2);
    expect(sampleValue(sqrt, -1, {})).toBeNull();
  });
});




FILE: features/articles/components/renderer/graph/plot.ts

import type { GraphViewport } from "@/features/articles/types";

import type { CompiledExpression } from "./expression";

export type PlotLayout = {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  padding: number;
};

export function mapX(x: number, viewport: GraphViewport, layout: PlotLayout): number {
  return (
    layout.padding +
    ((x - viewport.x_min) / (viewport.x_max - viewport.x_min)) *
      (layout.width - 2 * layout.padding)
  );
}

export function mapY(y: number, viewport: GraphViewport, layout: PlotLayout): number {
  return (
    layout.height -
    layout.padding -
    ((y - viewport.y_min) / (viewport.y_max - viewport.y_min)) *
      (layout.height - 2 * layout.padding)
  );
}

/**
 * Sample an expression into an SVG path. Non-finite values and large jumps
 * (asymptotes) start a new segment instead of drawing a vertical line.
 */
export function buildPath(
  expression: CompiledExpression,
  parameters: Record<string, number>,
  viewport: GraphViewport,
  layout: PlotLayout,
  samples = 180,
): string | null {
  if (!(viewport.x_min < viewport.x_max) || !(viewport.y_min < viewport.y_max)) {
    return null;
  }

  const plotHeight = layout.height - 2 * layout.padding;
  let path = "";
  let previousPixelY: number | null = null;

  for (let index = 0; index <= samples; index += 1) {
    const x = viewport.x_min + (index / samples) * (viewport.x_max - viewport.x_min);

    let y: number;
    try {
      y = expression.evaluate(x, parameters);
    } catch {
      previousPixelY = null;
      continue;
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(y)) {
      previousPixelY = null;
      continue;
    }

    const pixelX = mapX(x, viewport, layout);
    const pixelY = mapY(y, viewport, layout);

    if (previousPixelY === null) {
      path += `M${pixelX.toFixed(1)} ${pixelY.toFixed(1)}`;
    } else if (Math.abs(pixelY - previousPixelY) > plotHeight * 1.5) {
      path += `M${pixelX.toFixed(1)} ${pixelY.toFixed(1)}`;
    } else {
      path += `L${pixelX.toFixed(1)} ${pixelY.toFixed(1)}`;
    }

    previousPixelY = pixelY;
  }

  return path || null;
}

export function sampleValue(
  expression: CompiledExpression,
  x: number,
  parameters: Record<string, number>,
): number | null {
  try {
    const value = expression.evaluate(x, parameters);
    return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

FILE: features/home/components/about-illustration.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, type CSSProperties } from "react";

const delay = (ms: number, duration?: number) =>
  ({
    "--d": `${ms}ms`,
    ...(duration ? { "--dur": `${duration}ms` } : {}),
  }) as CSSProperties;

const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

/*
 * Geometry, in SVG user units. The unit circle and the sine plot share the
 * same vertical scale (radius = amplitude = R) and the same zero line
 * (CY = OY), so the horizontal projection from a point on the circle always
 * lands exactly on the curve y = sin x at x = θ. That is what makes the
 * picture numerically honest rather than decorative.
 */
const CX = 104;
const CY = 196;
const R = 76;

const OX = 246;
const OY = 196;
const PPU = 30; // horizontal pixels per radian on the sine plot

const START_THETA = Math.PI / 2;
const PERIOD_MS = 7000;

const X_TICKS: ReadonlyArray<{ t: number; label: string }> = [
  { t: 0, label: "0" },
  { t: Math.PI / 2, label: "π/2" },
  { t: Math.PI, label: "π" },
  { t: (3 * Math.PI) / 2, label: "3π/2" },
  { t: TAU, label: "2π" },
];

const sinePoint = (t: number) => ({
  x: OX + t * PPU,
  y: OY - R * Math.sin(t),
});

function buildSinePath(): string {
  const steps = 120;
  let d = "";
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i += 1) {
    const { x, y } = sinePoint((i / steps) * TAU);
    d += `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${x.toFixed(1)} ${y.toFixed(1)}`;
  }
  return d;
}

function sinePathUpTo(theta: number): string {
  const steps = Math.max(2, Math.round((theta / TAU) * 120));
  let d = "";
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i += 1) {
    const { x, y } = sinePoint((i / steps) * theta);
    d += `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${x.toFixed(1)} ${y.toFixed(1)}`;
  }
  return d;
}

const SINE_PATH = buildSinePath();

const initialCircleX = CX + R * Math.cos(START_THETA);
const initialCircleY = CY - R * Math.sin(START_THETA);
const initialSineX = OX + START_THETA * PPU;
const initialSineY = OY - R * Math.sin(START_THETA);

const labelHalo = {
  paintOrder: "stroke",
  stroke: "var(--color-canvas)",
  strokeWidth: 4,
  strokeLinejoin: "round",
} as CSSProperties;

export function AboutIllustration() {
  const radiusRef = useRef<SVGLineElement>(null);
  const circlePointRef = useRef<SVGCircleElement>(null);
  const projectRef = useRef<SVGLineElement>(null);
  const sinePointRef = useRef<SVGCircleElement>(null);
  const dropRef = useRef<SVGLineElement>(null);
  const trailRef = useRef<SVGPathElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const apply = (theta: number) => {
      const sin = Math.sin(theta);
      const circleX = CX + R * Math.cos(theta);
      const circleY = CY - R * sin;
      const sineX = OX + theta * PPU;
      const sineY = OY - R * sin; // === circleY, because CY === OY

      const round = (value: number) => value.toFixed(2);

      radiusRef.current?.setAttribute("x2", round(circleX));
      radiusRef.current?.setAttribute("y2", round(circleY));

      circlePointRef.current?.setAttribute("cx", round(circleX));
      circlePointRef.current?.setAttribute("cy", round(circleY));

      projectRef.current?.setAttribute("x1", round(circleX));
      projectRef.current?.setAttribute("y1", round(circleY));
      projectRef.current?.setAttribute("x2", round(sineX));
      projectRef.current?.setAttribute("y2", round(circleY));

      sinePointRef.current?.setAttribute("cx", round(sineX));
      sinePointRef.current?.setAttribute("cy", round(sineY));

      dropRef.current?.setAttribute("x1", round(sineX));
      dropRef.current?.setAttribute("y1", round(sineY));
      dropRef.current?.setAttribute("x2", round(sineX));
      dropRef.current?.setAttribute("y2", round(OY));

      trailRef.current?.setAttribute("d", sinePathUpTo(theta));
    };

    if (window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) {
      apply(START_THETA);
      return;
    }

    let frame = 0;
    let start: number | null = null;

    const tick = (now: number) => {
      if (start === null) start = now;
      const progress = ((now - start) % PERIOD_MS) / PERIOD_MS;
      apply(START_THETA + progress * TAU);
      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };

    frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, []);

  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 480 380"
      role="img"
      aria-label="Анимация связи единичной окружности и синуса: точка движется по окружности, её высота переносится вправо и вычерчивает график y = sin x. Оси размечены в радианах и в единицах от −1 до 1."
      className="block h-auto w-full"
    >
      {/* Faint grid on the sine side (vertical lines at the tick values). */}
      <g className="draw stroke-line" strokeWidth={1} fill="none" style={delay(0, 700)}>
        {X_TICKS.map(({ t }) => {
          const x = OX + t * PPU;
          return (
            <path
              key={`gx-${t}`}
              d={`M${x.toFixed(1)} ${OY - R - 14}V${OY + R + 14}`}
            />
          );
        })}
        <path d={`M${OX - 12} ${OY - R}H${(OX + TAU * PPU + 6).toFixed(1)}`} />
        <path d={`M${OX - 12} ${OY + R}H${(OX + TAU * PPU + 6).toFixed(1)}`} />
      </g>

      {/* Axes: sine plot and unit circle. */}
      <g
        className="draw stroke-line-strong"
        strokeWidth={1.25}
        fill="none"
        style={delay(150, 700)}
      >
        <path d={`M${OX - 12} ${OY}H${(OX + TAU * PPU + 6).toFixed(1)}`} />
        <path d={`M${OX} ${OY - R - 20}V${OY + R + 20}`} />
        <path d={`M${CX} ${CY - R - 20}V${CY + R + 20}`} />
        <path d={`M${CX - R - 20} ${CY}H${CX + R + 20}`} />
      </g>

      {/* Tick marks. */}
      <g
        className="a-fade stroke-line-strong"
        strokeWidth={1}
        fill="none"
        style={delay(500, 500)}
      >
        {X_TICKS.map(({ t }) => {
          const x = OX + t * PPU;
          return <path key={`tx-${t}`} d={`M${x.toFixed(1)} ${OY - 5}V${OY + 5}`} />;
        })}
        <path d={`M${OX - 5} ${OY - R}H${OX + 5}`} />
        <path d={`M${OX - 5} ${OY + R}H${OX + 5}`} />
        <path d={`M${CX - 5} ${CY - R}H${CX + 5}`} />
        <path d={`M${CX - 5} ${CY + R}H${CX + 5}`} />
      </g>

      {/* Numeric labels. */}
      <g
        className="a-fade fill-ink-faint"
        style={{
          ...delay(600, 500),
          fontFamily: "var(--font-sans)",
          fontSize: 12,
        }}
      >
        {X_TICKS.map(({ t, label }) => (
          <text
            key={`lx-${t}`}
            x={(OX + t * PPU).toFixed(1)}
            y={OY + 18}
            textAnchor="middle"
            style={labelHalo}
          >
            {label}
          </text>
        ))}
        <text x={OX - 10} y={OY - R + 4} textAnchor="end" style={labelHalo}>
          1
        </text>
        <text x={OX - 10} y={OY + R + 4} textAnchor="end" style={labelHalo}>
          −1
        </text>
        <text x={CX + 12} y={CY - R + 4} style={labelHalo}>
          1
        </text>
        <text x={CX + 12} y={CY + R + 4} style={labelHalo}>
          −1
        </text>
        <text x={CX - 14} y={CY + 16} style={labelHalo}>
          0
        </text>
      </g>

      {/* Curve and circle names. */}
      <text
        className="a-fade fill-accent"
        x={(OX + TAU * PPU).toFixed(1)}
        y={OY - R - 26}
        textAnchor="end"
        style={{
          ...delay(700, 500),
          fontFamily: "var(--font-serif)",
          fontStyle: "italic",
          fontSize: 20,
        }}
      >
        y = sin x
      </text>
      <text
        className="a-fade fill-ink-soft"
        x={CX}
        y={CY - R - 30}
        textAnchor="middle"
        style={{
          ...delay(700, 500),
          fontFamily: "var(--font-serif)",
          fontStyle: "italic",
          fontSize: 18,
        }}
      >
        sin θ
      </text>

      {/* The function itself, drawn once on reveal. */}
      <path
        className="draw stroke-accent"
        d={SINE_PATH}
        pathLength={1}
        fill="none"
        strokeWidth={2.25}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        opacity={0.22}
        style={delay(300, 1100)}
      />

      <circle
        className="draw stroke-ink"
        cx={CX}
        cy={CY}
        r={R}
        pathLength={1}
        fill="none"
        strokeWidth={1.75}
        style={delay(200, 900)}
      />

      {/* Dynamic layer: rotating radius, projection, drop and tracing point. */}
      <g className="a-fade" style={delay(1100, 700)}>
        <path
          ref={trailRef}
          className="stroke-accent"
          d={sinePathUpTo(START_THETA)}
          fill="none"
          strokeWidth={2.5}
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
        <line
          ref={projectRef}
          className="stroke-accent"
          x1={initialCircleX}
          y1={initialCircleY}
          x2={initialSineX}
          y2={initialSineY}
          strokeWidth={1}
          strokeDasharray="4 5"
          opacity={0.85}
        />
        <line
          ref={radiusRef}
          className="stroke-accent"
          x1={CX}
          y1={CY}
          x2={initialCircleX}
          y2={initialCircleY}
          strokeWidth={1.5}
          opacity={0.45}
        />
        <line
          ref={dropRef}
          className="stroke-line-strong"
          x1={initialSineX}
          y1={initialSineY}
          x2={initialSineX}
          y2={OY}
          strokeWidth={1}
          strokeDasharray="3 4"
        />
        <circle
          ref={circlePointRef}
          className="fill-accent"
          cx={initialCircleX}
          cy={initialCircleY}
          r={5.5}
        />
        <circle
          ref={sinePointRef}
          className="fill-accent"
          cx={initialSineX}
          cy={initialSineY}
          r={6}
        />
      </g>
    </svg>
  );
}

          href={getArticlePath(lead.slug)}
          aria-label={`Читать: ${lead.title}`}
          className="group reveal border-line bg-surface hover:border-ink desk:col-span-7 desk:min-h-[480px] col-span-12 flex min-h-[340px] flex-col justify-between rounded-xl border p-6 transition-colors duration-300 md:p-10"
        >
          <div>
            <span className="text-accent desk:text-[96px] font-serif text-6xl leading-none italic">
              1
            </span>
            <h3 className="my-6 max-w-[11em] text-[clamp(32px,4vw,52px)] leading-[1.05] font-medium tracking-[-0.035em]">
              {lead.title}
            </h3>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <span className="text-ink-soft text-sm">
              {lead.subject ? `${SUBJECT_LABEL[lead.subject]} · ` : ""}
              {lead.exam}
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-medium">
              Читать
              <ArrowRight
                aria-hidden
                className="size-4 transition-transform duration-150 group-hover:translate-x-1"
              />
            </span>
          </div>
        </Link>

        <div
          className="reveal desk:col-span-4 desk:col-start-9 desk:mt-0 col-span-12 mt-4 flex flex-col"
          style={step(1)}
        >
          {side.map((article) => (
            <Link
              key={article.slug}
              href={getArticlePath(article.slug)}
              className="group border-line desk:py-8 flex flex-1 flex-col border-t py-6 first:border-t-0 first:pt-0"
            >
              <span className="text-ink-soft text-sm">
                {article.subject ? `${SUBJECT_LABEL[article.subject]} · ` : ""}
                {article.exam}
              </span>
              <h3 className="heading-3 group-hover:text-accent my-3 transition-colors duration-150">
                {article.title}
              </h3>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-medium">
                Читать
                <ArrowRight
                  aria-hidden
                  className="size-4 transition-transform duration-150 group-hover:translate-x-1"
                />
              </span>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Section>
  );
}




FILE: features/home/components/code-demo.tsx

"use client";

import { Pause, Play, SkipForward } from "lucide-react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  highlightPython,
  solveTask5,
  TASK5_CODE,
  TASK5_SOLUTION,
  type Task5Run,
} from "@/features/home/lib/task5";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

type Status = "idle" | "typing" | "running" | "done";

const STATUS_TONE: Record<Status, "neutral" | "accent" | "success"> = {
  idle: "neutral",
  typing: "neutral",
  running: "accent",
  done: "success",
};

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<Status, string> = {
  idle: "Python",
  typing: "Python",
  running: "Выполняется",
  done: "Готово",
};

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    window.setTimeout(resolve, ms);
  });
}

const VAR_CELL = "min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-canvas px-3.5 py-2.5";
const VAR_LABEL = "block text-xs text-ink-faint";
const VAR_VALUE =
  "block font-mono text-xl leading-snug tabular-nums [overflow-wrap:anywhere]";

const CONTROL =
  "border-line-strong text-ink hover:border-ink hover:bg-surface inline-flex min-h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-4 text-[15px] font-medium transition-colors";

export function CodeDemo() {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const runIdRef = useRef(0);
  const pausedRef = useRef(false);
  const skipRef = useRef(false);

  const [typedLines, setTypedLines] = useState<string[]>(() =>
    TASK5_CODE.map(() => ""),
  );
  const [typingLine, setTypingLine] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("idle");
  const [activeLine, setActiveLine] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false);
  const [vars, setVars] = useState<Partial<Task5Run>>({});
  const [log, setLog] = useState<Task5Run[]>([]);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [insightVisible, setInsightVisible] = useState(false);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setTypedLines(TASK5_CODE.map(() => ""));
    setTypingLine(null);
    setStatus("idle");
    setActiveLine(null);
    setPaused(false);
    pausedRef.current = false;
    skipRef.current = false;
    setVars({});
    setLog([]);
    setResult(null);
    setInsightVisible(false);
  }, []);

  const showFinal = useCallback(() => {
    setTypedLines([...TASK5_CODE]);
    setTypingLine(null);
    setActiveLine(null);
    setVars({
      n: TASK5_SOLUTION.n,
      binary: TASK5_SOLUTION.n.toString(2),
      r: TASK5_SOLUTION.r,
    });
    setLog([
      {
        n: TASK5_SOLUTION.n,
        binary: TASK5_SOLUTION.n.toString(2),
        r: TASK5_SOLUTION.r,
        hit: true,
      },
    ]);
    setResult(TASK5_SOLUTION.r);
    setStatus("done");
    setInsightVisible(true);
  }, []);

  const play = useCallback(async () => {
    const runId = runIdRef.current + 1;
    runIdRef.current = runId;
    const alive = () => runIdRef.current === runId;

    reset();

    if (window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) {
      showFinal();
      return;
    }

    /** Resolves in small steps so pause/skip apply immediately. */
    const pauseable = async (ms: number) => {
      let waited = 0;
      while (waited < ms) {
        if (skipRef.current) return;
        if (!pausedRef.current) waited += 40;
        await sleep(40);
      }
    };

    /** Returns true when the run was cancelled or skipped. */
    const bail = () => {
      if (!alive()) return true;
      if (skipRef.current) {
        showFinal();
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    };

    setStatus("typing");

    for (let lineIndex = 0; lineIndex < TASK5_CODE.length; lineIndex += 1) {
      const line = TASK5_CODE[lineIndex];
      setTypingLine(lineIndex);
      for (let charIndex = 1; charIndex <= line.length; charIndex += 1) {
        if (bail()) return;
        const slice = line.slice(0, charIndex);
        setTypedLines((previous) => {
          const next = [...previous];
          next[lineIndex] = slice;
          return next;
        });
        await pauseable(line.trimStart().startsWith("#") ? 5 : 16);
      }
      if (bail()) return;
      setTypedLines((previous) => {
        const next = [...previous];
        next[lineIndex] = line;
        return next;
      });
      setTypingLine(null);
      await pauseable(line ? 140 : 40);
    }

    if (bail()) return;
    setStatus("running");
    setActiveLine(7);

    for (const run of solveTask5()) {
      if (bail()) return;
      setActiveLine(7);
      setVars(run);
      setLog((previous) => [...previous, run].slice(-6));
      await pauseable(run.n >= 18 ? 400 : 110);
      if (run.hit) break;
      setActiveLine(8);
      await pauseable(run.n >= 18 ? 250 : 60);
    }

    if (bail()) return;
    setActiveLine(10);
    await pauseable(500);
    if (bail()) return;

    setResult(TASK5_SOLUTION.r);
    setStatus("done");
    setActiveLine(null);
    await pauseable(600);
    if (alive()) setInsightVisible(true);
  }, [reset, showFinal]);

  const playRef = useRef(play);

  useEffect(() => {
    playRef.current = play;
  }, [play]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = containerRef.current;
    if (!element) return;

    if (window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) {
      playRef.current();
      return;
    }

    if (typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") {
      playRef.current();
      return;
    }

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (!entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)) return;
        observer.disconnect();
        void playRef.current();
      },
      { threshold: 0.45 },
    );

    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const isRunning = status === "typing" || status === "running";

  function togglePause() {
    pausedRef.current = !pausedRef.current;
    setPaused(pausedRef.current);
  }

  function skip() {
    skipRef.current = true;
    pausedRef.current = false;
    setPaused(false);
  }

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className="grid justify-items-start gap-4">
      <div className="js-only grid w-full justify-items-start gap-4">
        <div
          className="editor"
          role="img"
          aria-label="Код на Python, который перебирает N и находит наименьшее R, большее 83"
        >
          <div className="editor-bar">
            <span>task5.py</span>
            <Badge tone={STATUS_TONE[status]}>{STATUS_LABEL[status]}</Badge>
          </div>

          <pre className="code" aria-hidden="true">
            {TASK5_CODE.map((line, index) => (
              <span
                key={index}
                className="ln"
                data-active={activeLine === index ? "" : undefined}
                dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
                  __html:
                    highlightPython(typedLines[index] ?? "") +
                    (typingLine === index ? '<span class="caret"></span>' : ""),
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </pre>
        </div>

        <div className="border-line bg-surface grid w-full gap-4 rounded-lg border p-5">
          <div className="desk:grid-cols-[1fr_2fr_1fr] grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className={cn(VAR_CELL, "order-1")}>
              <small className={VAR_LABEL}>N</small>
              <b className={VAR_VALUE}>{vars.n ?? "—"}</b>
            </div>
            <div
              className={cn(
                VAR_CELL,
                "desk:order-2 desk:col-span-1 order-3 col-span-2",
              )}
            >
              <small className={VAR_LABEL}>bin(N)</small>
              <b className={VAR_VALUE}>{vars.binary ?? "—"}</b>
            </div>
            <div className={cn(VAR_CELL, "desk:order-3 order-2")}>
              <small className={VAR_LABEL}>R</small>
              <b className={VAR_VALUE}>{vars.r ?? "—"}</b>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="log min-h-[120px]" aria-label="Ход выполнения">
            {log.map((row) => (
              <div key={row.n} className="log-row" data-hit={row.hit ? "" : undefined}>
                <span>N={row.n}</span>
                <span>R={row.r}</span>
                <span>
                  {row.r} {row.hit ? ">" : "≤"} 83
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <p
            role="status"
            aria-live="polite"
            aria-atomic="true"
            className={cn(
              "m-0 transition-opacity duration-500",
              result !== null ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0",
            )}
          >
            <span className="text-ink-soft">Вывод:</span>{" "}
            <b className="text-success ml-2 font-mono text-2xl font-semibold">
              {result ?? ""}
            </b>
          </p>

          <p
            className={cn(
              "border-line text-ink-soft m-0 border-t pt-3.5 text-[15px] transition-opacity duration-500",
              insightVisible ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0",
            )}
          >
            Что заметить: вторая дописанная цифра всегда 0 — после первой чётность
            единиц уже выровнена. Поэтому R всегда чётное, и 84 и 85 отпадают сразу.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          {isRunning ? (
            <>
              <button type="button" onClick={togglePause} className={CONTROL}>
                {paused ? (
                  <Play aria-hidden className="size-4" />
                ) : (
                  <Pause aria-hidden className="size-4" />
                )}
                {paused ? "Продолжить" : "Пауза"}
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={skip} className={CONTROL}>
                <SkipForward aria-hidden className="size-4" />
                Пропустить
              </button>
            </>
          ) : null}

          {status === "done" ? (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => void play()} className={CONTROL}>
              Повторить
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="no-js-only">
        <div className="grid w-full gap-3">
          <div className="editor">
            <div className="editor-bar">
              <span>task5.py</span>
              <Badge tone="neutral">Python</Badge>
            </div>
            <pre className="code" data-static-code="">
              {TASK5_CODE.map((line, index) => (
                <span
                  key={`static-${index}`}
                  className="ln"
                  dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: highlightPython(line) }}
                />
              ))}
            </pre>
          </div>
          <p className="text-ink-soft text-sm">
            Программа выводит <b className="text-ink">86</b>: первое значение R, большее
            83, достигается при N = 21. Анимация выполнения требует JavaScript.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
