FILE: features/home/components/derivative-graph.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from "react";

function f(x: number): number {
  return x ** 3 - 4 * x ** 2 + 7 * x;
}

function df(x: number): number {
  return 3 * x ** 2 - 8 * x + 7;
}

const mapX = (x: number): number => 40 + x * 115;
const mapY = (y: number): number => 340 - y * (310 / 28);

function buildCurvePath(): string {
  let path = "";
  for (let x = 0; x <= 4.001; x += 0.05) {
    const command = path ? "L" : "M";
    path += `${command}${mapX(x).toFixed(1)} ${mapY(f(x)).toFixed(1)}`;
  }
  return path;
}

const CURVE_PATH = buildCurvePath();

const X_TICKS = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4];
const Y_TICKS = [7, 14, 21, 28];

const MIN_X = 0.3;
const MAX_X = 3.7;

const clamp = (value: number, min: number, max: number): number =>
  Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

function formatValue(value: number): string {
  return Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
}

export function DerivativeGraph({ formulas }: { formulas: ReactNode }) {
  const [x, setX] = useState(2);

  const slope = df(x);
  const pointX = mapX(x);
  const pointY = mapY(f(x));

  const tangentStartX = clamp(x - 1.5, 0, 4);
  const tangentEndX = clamp(x + 1.5, 0, 4);
  const tangentStartY = mapY(f(x) + slope * (tangentStartX - x));
  const tangentEndY = mapY(f(x) + slope * (tangentEndX - x));

  function update(next: number) {
    setX(clamp(next, MIN_X, MAX_X));
  }

  return (
    <figure className="viz m-0">
      <div className="viz-top">
        <b>Математика · Производная</b>
        <output aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true" className="tabular-nums">
          x = {x.toFixed(1)} · f′(x) = {formatValue(slope)}
        </output>
      </div>

      {formulas}

      <div className="graph-wrap relative">
        <svg
          className="graph"
          viewBox="0 0 520 380"
          role="img"
          aria-label="График функции f от x равно x в кубе минус 4 x в квадрате плюс 7 x. Точка на кривой двигается, касательная и значение производной пересчитываются."
          focusable="false"
        >
          <g className="g-grid">
            {X_TICKS.map((tick) => (
              <line key={`vx-${tick}`} x1={mapX(tick)} y1={20} x2={mapX(tick)} y2={340} />
            ))}
            {[0, ...Y_TICKS].map((tick) => (
              <line key={`hy-${tick}`} x1={40} y1={mapY(tick)} x2={500} y2={mapY(tick)} />
            ))}
          </g>

          <line className="g-axis" x1={40} y1={340} x2={500} y2={340} />
          <line className="g-axis" x1={40} y1={20} x2={40} y2={340} />

          <g className="g-text">
            {X_TICKS.map((tick) => (
              <text key={`tx-${tick}`} x={mapX(tick)} y={360} textAnchor="middle">
                {tick}
              </text>
            ))}
            {Y_TICKS.map((tick) => (
              <text key={`ty-${tick}`} x={30} y={mapY(tick) + 4} textAnchor="end">
                {tick}
              </text>
            ))}
          </g>

          <text className="g-text" x={498} y={330} textAnchor="end">
            x
          </text>
          <text className="g-text" x={48} y={30}>
            y
          </text>

          <path
            className="g-curve draw-in"
            d={CURVE_PATH}
            pathLength={1}
            style={{ "--dur": "900ms" } as CSSProperties}
          />

          <line
            className="g-drop fade-el"
            x1={pointX}
            x2={pointX}
            y1={pointY}
            y2={340}
            style={{ "--d": "250ms" } as CSSProperties}
          />

          <line
            className="g-tan draw-in"
            x1={mapX(tangentStartX)}
            y1={tangentStartY}
            x2={mapX(tangentEndX)}
            y2={tangentEndY}
            pathLength={1}
            style={{ "--d": "350ms", "--dur": "500ms" } as CSSProperties}
          />

          <circle
            className="g-pt fade-el"
            cx={pointX}
            cy={pointY}
            r={7}
            style={{ "--d": "250ms" } as CSSProperties}
          />

          <text
            className="g-label fade-el"
            x={pointX + 14}
            y={pointY + 28}
            style={{ "--d": "250ms" } as CSSProperties}
          >
            f′({formatValue(x)}) = {formatValue(slope)}
          </text>
        </svg>

        <input
          type="range"
          min={MIN_X}
          max={MAX_X}
          step={0.05}
          value={x}
          onChange={(event) => update(Number(event.target.value))}
          aria-label="Положение точки на графике"
          aria-valuetext={`x = ${x.toFixed(1)}, производная f′(x) = ${formatValue(slope)}`}
          className="absolute inset-0 z-10 h-full w-full cursor-ew-resize opacity-0 outline-none focus:outline-none focus-visible:outline-none"
        />
      </div>
    </figure>
  );
}




FILE: features/home/components/derivative-viz.tsx

import type { CSSProperties } from "react";

import { Math } from "@/components/ui/math";
import { DerivativeGraph } from "@/features/home/components/derivative-graph";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

const FORMULA_LINES = [
  {
    id: "f1",
    delay: 0,
    derivative: false,
    parts: [
      { tex: "f(x)\\,=\\,", delay: 150 },
      { tex: "x^3", delay: 260 },
      { tex: "-\\,4x^2", delay: 370 },
      { tex: "+\\,7x", delay: 480 },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: "f2",
    delay: 800,
    derivative: true,
    parts: [
      { tex: "f'(x)\\,=\\,", delay: 950 },
      { tex: "3x^2", delay: 1060 },
      { tex: "-\\,8x", delay: 1170 },
      { tex: "+\\,7", delay: 1280 },
    ],
  },
] as const;

const delay = (ms: number) => ({ "--d": `${ms}ms` }) as CSSProperties;

/**
 * Hero visualisation. The formulas are rendered on the server (KaTeX) and
 * passed as nodes into the interactive client graph, so the heavy math library
 * never reaches the client bundle. The graph is a real range input overlaid on
 * the SVG: draggable with the pointer and operable with arrow keys.
 */
export function DerivativeViz() {
  return (
    <DerivativeGraph
      formulas={
        <div className="formulas" aria-hidden="true">
          {FORMULA_LINES.map((line) => (
            <div
              key={line.id}
              className={cn("f", line.derivative && "derivative")}
              style={delay(line.delay)}
            >
              {line.parts.map((part) => (
                <span key={part.tex} className="part" style={delay(part.delay)}>
                  <Math tex={part.tex} />
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      }
    />
  );
}




FILE: features/home/components/final-cta.tsx

import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import type { CSSProperties } from "react";

import { Container } from "@/components/layout/container";
import { TutorLeadPanel } from "@/features/tutor-leads/components/tutor-lead-panel";
import { AI_POSITIONING, CTA_CONTENT } from "@/features/tutor-leads/content";

const step = (index: number) => ({ "--i": index }) as CSSProperties;

/**
 * Final conversion block. It offers a trial lesson (with the request form) and
 * keeps the task bank as a secondary, non-competing link.
 */
export function FinalCta() {
  const copy = CTA_CONTENT["home-final"];

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="final-title"

FILE: features/home/components/task-quiz.test.tsx

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { TaskQuiz } from "@/features/home/components/task-quiz";

function renderQuiz() {
  return render(
    <TaskQuiz
      tag="ЕГЭ · Профиль"
      title="Производная"
      prompt={<span>Найдите значение f′(2)</span>}
      formula={<span>f(x)=x³−4x²+7x</span>}
      options={[
        { value: "0", label: "0" },
        { value: "3", label: "3" },
        { value: "7", label: "7" },
        { value: "12", label: "12" },
      ]}
      correctValue="3"
      hints={{ "0": "Проверьте степень." }}
      successNote="Сначала производная, потом подстановка."
      steps={[{ note: "Производная", content: <span>f′(x)=3x²−8x+7</span> }]}
      moreHref="/zadachi"
    />,
  );
}

describe("TaskQuiz", () => {
  it("renders all answer options as enabled buttons", () => {
    renderQuiz();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("button")).toHaveLength(4);
    for (const button of screen.getAllByRole("button")) {
      expect(button).toBeEnabled();
    }
  });

  it("shows the hint for a wrong answer and marks the correct one", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderQuiz();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "0" }));

    expect(await screen.findByText("Не совсем.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Проверьте степень\./)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "3" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "data-state",
      "right",
    );
  });

  it("confirms a correct answer and reveals the solution", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderQuiz();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "3" }));

    expect(await screen.findByText("Верно.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText("f′(x)=3x²−8x+7")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: /Разобрать подробнее/ })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/zadachi",
    );
  });

  it("allows retrying after answering", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderQuiz();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "0" }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Попробовать снова" }));

    expect(screen.queryByText("Не совсем.")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "0" })).toBeEnabled();
  });
});




FILE: features/home/components/task-quiz.tsx

"use client";

import { ArrowRight, CircleCheck } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { TutorCta } from "@/features/tutor-leads/components/tutor-cta";
import { ANALYTICS_EVENTS, track } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";
import { usePrefersReducedMotion } from "@/lib/utils/use-prefers-reduced-motion";

export type QuizOption = { value: string; label: string };
export type QuizStep = { note: string; content: ReactNode };

type TaskQuizProps = {
  tag: string;
  title: string;
  prompt: ReactNode;
  formula: ReactNode;
  options: readonly QuizOption[];
  correctValue: string;
  hints: Record<string, string>;
  successNote: string;
  steps: readonly QuizStep[];
  moreHref: string;
};

type Answer = { value: string; correct: boolean };

export function TaskQuiz({
  tag,
  title,
  prompt,
  formula,
  options,
  correctValue,
  hints,
  successNote,
  steps,
  moreHref,
}: TaskQuizProps) {
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState<Answer | null>(null);
  const [visibleSteps, setVisibleSteps] = useState(0);
  const reducedMotion = usePrefersReducedMotion();
  const timersRef = useRef<number[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      for (const timer of timersRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, []);

  function clearTimers() {
    for (const timer of timersRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timer);
    timersRef.current = [];
  }

  function choose(value: string) {
    if (answer) return;
    clearTimers();
    const correct = value === correctValue;
    setAnswer({ value, correct });
    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.quizAnswer, { correct });

    if (reducedMotion) {
      setVisibleSteps(steps.length);
      return;
    }

    setVisibleSteps(0);
    timersRef.current = steps.map((_, index) =>
      window.setTimeout(() => setVisibleSteps(index + 1), 450 + index * 650),
    );
  }

  function retry() {
    clearTimers();
    setAnswer(null);
    setVisibleSteps(0);
  }

  function stateFor(value: string): "right" | "wrong" | undefined {
    if (!answer) return undefined;
    if (value === correctValue) return "right";
    if (value === answer.value) return "wrong";
    return undefined;
  }

  return (
    <article
      aria-labelledby="try-q"
      className="border-line bg-surface shadow-card rounded-xl border p-6 md:p-10"
    >
      <Badge>{tag}</Badge>
      <h3 className="heading-3 mt-5 mb-1.5" id="try-q">
        {title}
      </h3>

      <p className="mt-6 mb-2 text-[22px] leading-snug">{prompt}</p>
      <div className="py-[18px] text-[26px]">{formula}</div>

      <div
        role="group"
        aria-label="Варианты ответа"
        className="js-only mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 md:grid-cols-4"
      >
        {options.map((option) => (
          <button
            key={option.value}
            type="button"
            className="option"
            disabled={answer !== null}
            data-state={stateFor(option.value)}
            onClick={() => choose(option.value)}
          >
            {option.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <p className="no-js-only text-ink-soft mt-4 text-sm">
        Интерактивная проверка требует JavaScript.{" "}
        <Link href={moreHref} className="text-accent underline underline-offset-4">
          Разобрать задачу целиком
        </Link>
        .
      </p>

      <div className="feedback" data-open={answer ? "true" : "false"}>
        <div>
          <div className="border-line mt-6 border-t pt-6 text-base">
            {answer ? (
              <>
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "mb-2.5 flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold",
                    answer.correct ? "text-success" : "text-danger",
                  )}
                >
                  {answer.correct ? (
                    <CircleCheck aria-hidden className="quiz-check size-5" />
                  ) : null}
                  {answer.correct ? "Верно." : "Не совсем."}
                </span>

                <p className="text-ink-soft">
                  {answer.correct
                    ? successNote
                    : `${hints[answer.value] ?? "Проверьте вычисления."} Правильный ход решения:`}
                </p>

                <div className="my-3 flex flex-col gap-1 text-[18px]">
                  {steps.map((step, index) => (
                    <div
                      key={step.note}
                      className="step"
                      data-visible={visibleSteps > index ? "" : undefined}
                      data-final={index === steps.length - 1 ? "" : undefined}
                    >
                      <small className="text-ink-soft block text-[13px]">
                        {step.note}
                      </small>
                      <span>{step.content}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center">
                  <Link
                    href={moreHref}
                    className="text-accent hover:text-accent-strong inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 font-medium transition-colors"
                  >
                    Разобрать подробнее
                    <ArrowRight aria-hidden className="size-4" />
                  </Link>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={retry}
                    className="text-ink-soft hover:text-ink ml-5 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors"
                  >
                    Попробовать снова
                  </button>
                </div>

                {answer.correct ? (
                  <TutorCta placement="quiz-success" size="compact" className="mt-6" />
                ) : null}
              </>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: features/home/lib/task5.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import {
  countOnes,
  highlightPython,
  rOf,
  solveTask5,
  TASK5_CODE,
  TASK5_SOLUTION,
} from "@/features/home/lib/task5";

describe("rOf", () => {
  it("reproduces the program from the statement", () => {
    expect(rOf(1)).toBe(6);
    expect(rOf(21)).toBe(86);
  });

  it("always produces an even number (second appended bit is 0)", () => {
    for (let n = 1; n <= 40; n += 1) {
      expect(rOf(n) % 2).toBe(0);
    }
  });
});

describe("solveTask5", () => {
  it("finds the first R greater than 83 at N = 21", () => {
    const runs = solveTask5();
    const last = runs.at(-1);
    expect(last).toEqual({
      n: TASK5_SOLUTION.n,
      binary: "10101",
      r: TASK5_SOLUTION.r,
      hit: true,
    });
    expect(runs).toHaveLength(TASK5_SOLUTION.n);
    expect(runs.filter((run) => run.hit)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("never reports a hit for R <= 83", () => {
    for (const run of solveTask5()) {
      if (!run.hit) expect(run.r).toBeLessThanOrEqual(83);
    }
  });
});

describe("countOnes", () => {
  it("counts bits in a binary string", () => {
    expect(countOnes("10101")).toBe(3);
    expect(countOnes("0000")).toBe(0);
    expect(countOnes("1111")).toBe(4);
  });
});

describe("highlightPython", () => {
  it("escapes HTML before adding markup", () => {
    expect(highlightPython("while r(n) <= 83:")).toContain("&lt;=");
  });

  it("marks keywords, builtins, strings, numbers and comments", () => {
    expect(highlightPython("def r(n):")).toContain('class="tok-keyword"');
    expect(highlightPython("print(r(n))  # 86")).toContain('class="tok-comment"');
    expect(highlightPython("s = bin(n)[2:]")).toContain('class="tok-fn"');
    expect(highlightPython("s += str(s.count('1') % 2)")).toContain(
      'class="tok-string"',
    );
  });

  it("covers every line of the demo program", () => {
    for (const line of TASK5_CODE) {
      expect(() => highlightPython(line)).not.toThrow();
    }
  });
});




FILE: features/home/lib/task5.ts

export const TASK5_CODE: readonly string[] = [
  "def r(n):",
  "    s = bin(n)[2:]",
  "    s += str(s.count('1') % 2)",
  "    s += str(s.count('1') % 2)",
  "    return int(s, 2)",
  "",
  "n = 1",
  "while r(n) <= 83:",
  "    n += 1",
  "",
  "print(r(n))  # 86",
];

export const TASK5_THRESHOLD = 83;

export const TASK5_SOLUTION = { n: 21, r: 86 } as const;

export function isPythonKeyword(word: string): boolean {
  return word === "def" || word === "return" || word === "while";
}

export function isPythonBuiltin(word: string): boolean {
  return (
    word === "bin" ||
    word === "str" ||
    word === "int" ||
    word === "print" ||
    word === "count"
  );
}

export function countOnes(binary: string): number {
  let count = 0;
  for (const char of binary) {
    if (char === "1") count += 1;
  }
  return count;
}

export function rOf(n: number): number {
  let binary = n.toString(2);
  for (let step = 0; step < 2; step += 1) {
    binary += String(countOnes(binary) % 2);
  }
  return Number.parseInt(binary, 2);
}

export function highlightPython(line: string): string {
  const escaped = line
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;");

  return escaped.replace(
    /(#.*$)|('[^']*')|\b(def|return|while)\b|\b(bin|str|int|print|count)\b|\b(\d+)\b/g,
    (
      match,
      comment?: string,
      string?: string,
      keyword?: string,
      fn?: string,
      num?: string,
    ) => {
      if (comment) return `<span class="tok-comment">${match}</span>`;
      if (string) return `<span class="tok-string">${match}</span>`;
      if (keyword && isPythonKeyword(keyword))
        return `<span class="tok-keyword">${match}</span>`;
      if (fn && isPythonBuiltin(fn)) return `<span class="tok-fn">${match}</span>`;
      if (num) return `<span class="tok-number">${match}</span>`;
      return match;
    },
  );
}

export type Task5Run = {
  n: number;
  binary: string;
  r: number;
  hit: boolean;
};

export function solveTask5(threshold = TASK5_THRESHOLD): Task5Run[] {
  const runs: Task5Run[] = [];
  for (let n = 1; n <= 10_000; n += 1) {
    const r = rOf(n);
    const hit = r > threshold;
    runs.push({ n, binary: n.toString(2), r, hit });
    if (hit) break;
  }
  return runs;
}




FILE: features/tasks/api-client.ts

FILE: features/tasks/api.ts

import { isApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";
import { isServerApiConfigured, serverRequest } from "@/lib/api/server";
import { demoTasks } from "@/features/tasks/fixtures/tasks";
import type { Task, TaskDifficulty, TaskSubject } from "@/features/tasks/types";

export const TASK_PAGE_SIZE = 4;

export type TaskFilters = {
  subject?: TaskSubject;
  level?: TaskDifficulty;
};

export type TaskListResult = {
  items: Task[];
  total: number;
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
  isDemo: boolean;
};

type TaskListDto = {
  items: Task[];
  total: number;
  page: number;
  page_size: number;
};

function listLocalTasks(filters: TaskFilters, page: number): TaskListResult {
  const filtered = demoTasks.filter((task) => {
    if (filters.subject && task.subject !== filters.subject) return false;
    if (filters.level && task.level !== filters.level) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / TASK_PAGE_SIZE));
  const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 1), pageCount);
  const start = (safePage - 1) * TASK_PAGE_SIZE;

  return {
    items: filtered.slice(start, start + TASK_PAGE_SIZE),
    total: filtered.length,
    page: safePage,
    pageSize: TASK_PAGE_SIZE,
    pageCount,
    isDemo: true,
  };
}

export async function getTasks(
  filters: TaskFilters = {},
  page = 1,
): Promise<TaskListResult> {
  if (isServerApiConfigured()) {
    const response = await serverRequest<TaskListDto>("/tasks", {
      searchParams: {
        subject: filters.subject,
        level: filters.level,
        page,
        page_size: TASK_PAGE_SIZE,
      },
    });

    return {
      items: response.items,
      total: response.total,
      page: response.page,
      pageSize: response.page_size,
      pageCount: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(response.total / response.page_size)),
      isDemo: false,
    };
  }

  // Build-time / offline fallback only. In development the API origin defaults
  // to localhost, so published tasks are always served by FastAPI.
  return listLocalTasks(filters, page);
}

export async function getTaskById(id: string): Promise<Task | null> {
  if (isServerApiConfigured()) {
    try {
      return await serverRequest<Task>(`/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`);
    } catch (error) {
      if (isApiError(error) && (error.status === 404 || error.status === 422)) {
        return null;
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }

  return demoTasks.find((task) => task.id === id) ?? null;
}

export async function getFeaturedTasks(limit = 3): Promise<Task[]> {
  if (isServerApiConfigured()) {
    const response = await serverRequest<TaskListDto>("/tasks", {
      searchParams: { featured: true, page_size: limit },
    });
    return response.items;
  }

  return demoTasks.filter((task) => task.featured).slice(0, limit);
}

export async function getTasksBySubject(
  subject: TaskSubject,
  limit = 6,
): Promise<Task[]> {
  const result = await getTasks({ subject }, 1);
  return result.items.slice(0, limit);
}

export async function getRelatedTasks(task: Task, limit = 3): Promise<Task[]> {
  if (isServerApiConfigured()) {
    const response = await serverRequest<TaskListDto>(
      `/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(task.id)}/related`,
      { searchParams: { limit } },
    );
    return response.items;
  }

  const scored = demoTasks
    .filter((candidate) => candidate.id !== task.id)
    .map((candidate) => {
      const sharedTopics = candidate.topics.filter((topic) =>
        task.topics.includes(topic),
      ).length;
      const score =
        sharedTopics * 2 +
        (candidate.subject === task.subject ? 1 : 0) +
        (candidate.level === task.level ? 1 : 0);
      return { candidate, score };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

  return scored.slice(0, limit).map((entry) => entry.candidate);
}

export function getTaskPath(id: string): string {
  return `/zadachi/${id}`;
}

export async function getTaskIds(): Promise<string[]> {
  if (!isServerApiConfigured()) {
    return demoTasks.map((task) => task.id);
  }

  const ids: string[] = [];
  const pageSize = 100;
  let page = 1;
  let total = 0;
  do {
    const response = await serverRequest<TaskListDto>("/tasks", {
      searchParams: { page, page_size: pageSize },
    });
    ids.push(...response.items.map((task) => task.id));
    total = response.total;
    page += 1;
  } while (ids.length < total);

  return ids;
}

import type { TaskDifficulty, TaskSubject } from "@/features/tasks/types";

export const DIFFICULTY_ORDER: Record<TaskDifficulty, number> = {
  easy: 1,
  medium: 2,
  hard: 3,
};

export const DIFFICULTY_LABEL: Record<TaskDifficulty, string> = {
  easy: "Лёгкая",
  medium: "Средняя",
  hard: "Сложная",
};

export const SUBJECT_LABEL: Record<TaskSubject, string> = {
  matematika: "Математика",
  informatika: "Информатика",
};

export const SUBJECT_PATH: Record<TaskSubject, string> = {
  matematika: "/matematika",
  informatika: "/informatika",
};

export const SUBJECTS: readonly TaskSubject[] = ["matematika", "informatika"];
export const DIFFICULTIES: readonly TaskDifficulty[] = ["easy", "medium", "hard"];

export function isTaskSubject(value: unknown): value is TaskSubject {
  return value === "matematika" || value === "informatika";
}

export function isTaskDifficulty(value: unknown): value is TaskDifficulty {
  return value === "easy" || value === "medium" || value === "hard";
}




FILE: features/tasks/search-params.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { parsePageParam, parseTaskFilters } from "@/features/tasks/search-params";

describe("parseTaskFilters", () => {
  it("reads valid filters", () => {
    expect(parseTaskFilters({ subject: "matematika", level: "hard" })).toEqual({
      subject: "matematika",
      level: "hard",
    });
  });

  it("uses the first value of a repeated parameter", () => {
    expect(parseTaskFilters({ subject: ["informatika", "matematika"] })).toEqual({
      subject: "informatika",
      level: undefined,
    });
  });

  it("ignores unknown values instead of failing", () => {
    expect(parseTaskFilters({ subject: "physics", level: "impossible" })).toEqual({
      subject: undefined,
      level: undefined,
    });
  });
});

describe("parsePageParam", () => {
  it("defaults to page 1 for missing, invalid or negative values", () => {
    expect(parsePageParam(undefined)).toBe(1);
    expect(parsePageParam("abc")).toBe(1);
    expect(parsePageParam("0")).toBe(1);
    expect(parsePageParam("-4")).toBe(1);
  });

  it("parses a valid page", () => {
    expect(parsePageParam("3")).toBe(3);
  });
});




FILE: features/tasks/search-params.ts

import { isTaskDifficulty, isTaskSubject } from "@/features/tasks/labels";
import type { TaskFilters } from "@/features/tasks/api";

export type RawSearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;

function first(value: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value[0];
  return value;
}

export function parseTaskFilters(params: RawSearchParams): TaskFilters {
  const subject = first(params.subject);
  const level = first(params.level);

  return {
    subject: isTaskSubject(subject) ? subject : undefined,
    level: isTaskDifficulty(level) ? level : undefined,
  };
}

export function parsePageParam(value: string | string[] | undefined): number {
  const raw = Number(first(value));
  if (!Number.isInteger(raw) || raw < 1) return 1;
  return raw;
}

FILE: features/tasks/components/answer-form.test.tsx

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { AnswerForm } from "@/features/tasks/components/answer-form";

function renderForm() {
  return render(
    <AnswerForm
      taskId="1042"
      expected="3"
      kind="number"
      answerNote="Сначала производная, потом подстановка."
      solution={<p>f′(x) = 3x² − 8x + 7</p>}
    />,
  );
}

function solutionPanel() {
  return document.querySelector("[data-solution]");
}

describe("AnswerForm", () => {
  it("confirms a correct answer", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderForm();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Ваш ответ"), "3");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));

    expect(await screen.findByText("Верно.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(solutionPanel()).toHaveAttribute("data-open", "true");
  });

  it("rejects a wrong answer and keeps the solution collapsed", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderForm();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Ваш ответ"), "12");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));

    expect(await screen.findByText(/Пока неверно/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(solutionPanel()).not.toHaveAttribute("data-open");
  });

  it("reveals the solution on demand, even without answering", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderForm();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Показать решение/ }));

    expect(solutionPanel()).toHaveAttribute("data-open", "true");
    expect(screen.getByText(/f′\(x\) = 3x² − 8x \+ 7/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("resets back to the initial state", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderForm();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Ваш ответ"), "3");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Проверить" }));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /Попробовать снова/ }));

    expect(screen.queryByText("Верно.")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Ваш ответ")).toHaveValue("");
    expect(solutionPanel()).not.toHaveAttribute("data-open");
  });
});




FILE: features/tasks/components/answer-form.tsx

"use client";

import { Eye, EyeOff, RotateCcw } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useId, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { TutorCta } from "@/features/tutor-leads/components/tutor-cta";
import { checkTaskAnswer } from "@/features/tasks/api-client";
import { ANALYTICS_EVENTS, track } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { isPublicApiConfigured } from "@/lib/api/config";
import { describeApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";
import { isAnswerCorrect, type AnswerKind } from "@/lib/utils/check-answer";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

type AnswerFormProps = {
  taskId: string;
  expected: string;
  kind: AnswerKind;
  answerNote: string;
  solution: ReactNode;
};

type Status = "idle" | "correct" | "wrong";

export function AnswerForm({ taskId, expected, kind, answerNote, solution }: AnswerFormProps) {
  const inputId = useId();
  const statusId = useId();
  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("idle");
  const [attempts, setAttempts] = useState(0);
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false);
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (revealed) track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.solutionOpen);
  }, [revealed]);

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      setStatus("wrong");
      setAttempts((count) => count + 1);
      track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.taskAnswerAttempt, {
        correct: false,
        attempt: attempts + 1,
      });
      return;
    }

    let correct: boolean;
    if (isPublicApiConfigured()) {
      setChecking(true);
      setFailure(null);
      try {
        const result = await checkTaskAnswer(taskId, trimmed);
        correct = result.isCorrect;
      } catch (error) {
        setChecking(false);
        setFailure(describeApiError(error));
        track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.taskAnswerAttempt, {
          correct: false,
          attempt: attempts + 1,
        });
        return;
      }
      setChecking(false);
    } else {
      correct = isAnswerCorrect(trimmed, expected, kind);
    }

    setStatus(correct ? "correct" : "wrong");
    setAttempts((count) => count + 1);
    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.taskAnswerAttempt, {
      correct,
      attempt: attempts + 1,
    });
    if (correct) setRevealed(true);
  }

  function handleReset() {
    setValue("");
    setStatus("idle");
    setAttempts(0);
    setRevealed(false);
    setFailure(null);
  }

  const statusMessage =
    status === "idle"
      ? ""
      : status === "correct"
        ? "Верно."
        : attempts >= 2
          ? "Пока неверно. Можно посмотреть решение."
          : "Пока неверно. Проверьте вычисления и попробуйте ещё раз.";

  return (
    <div className="border-line bg-surface rounded-xl border p-6 md:p-8">
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
        <Label htmlFor={inputId}>Ваш ответ</Label>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
          <Input
            id={inputId}
            name="answer"
            value={value}
            onChange={(event) => setValue(event.target.value)}
            inputMode={kind === "number" ? "decimal" : "text"}
            autoComplete="off"
            aria-describedby={statusId}
            disabled={status === "correct"}
            placeholder={kind === "number" ? "Число" : "Ответ"}
          />
          <Button
            type="submit"
            className="shrink-0 sm:w-auto"
            disabled={status === "correct" || checking}
          >
            {checking ? "Проверяем…" : "Проверить"}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>

      <p
        id={statusId}
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
        className={cn(
          "mt-3 min-h-6 text-sm",
          status === "correct" && "text-success font-medium",
          status === "wrong" && "text-danger",
          status === "idle" && "text-ink-faint",
        )}
      >
        {statusMessage}
      </p>

      {failure ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-danger mt-2 text-sm">
          {failure}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setRevealed((current) => !current)}
          className="solution-toggle js-only text-accent hover:text-accent-strong inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors"
          aria-expanded={revealed}
        >
          {revealed ? (
            <EyeOff aria-hidden className="size-4" />
          ) : (
            <Eye aria-hidden className="size-4" />
          )}
          {revealed ? "Скрыть решение" : "Показать решение"}
        </button>

        {status !== "idle" || revealed ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleReset}
            className="text-ink-soft hover:text-ink inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-sm underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors"
          >
            <RotateCcw aria-hidden className="size-4" />
            Попробовать снова
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <p className="no-js-only text-ink-soft mt-3 text-sm">
        Проверка ответа работает при включённом JavaScript. Решение показано ниже.
      </p>

      <div
        className="cta-reveal mt-6"
        data-open={attempts >= 2 && !revealed ? "true" : "false"}
      >
        <div>
          <TutorCta placement="after-wrong-answers" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        data-solution=""
        data-open={revealed ? "true" : undefined}
        className="solution-panel border-line mt-6 border-t pt-6"
      >
        <h2 className="heading-3 mb-4">Решение</h2>
        {solution}
        <p className="text-ink-soft mt-5">{answerNote}</p>
        <p className="text-ink-faint mt-4 text-sm">
          Ответ: <span className="text-ink font-medium">{expected}</span>
        </p>
        <TutorCta placement="after-solution" className="mt-6" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { DIFFICULTY_LABEL, DIFFICULTY_ORDER } from "@/features/tasks/labels";

export function DifficultyMeter({ level }: { level: TaskDifficulty }) {

  return (
    <>
      <span className="diff" aria-hidden="true">
        {[1, 2, 3].map((bar) => (
          <i key={bar} data-on={bar <= filled ? "" : undefined} />
        ))}
      </span>
      <span>{DIFFICULTY_LABEL[level]}</span>
    </>

FILE: features/tasks/components/pagination.tsx

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

const baseItem =
  "inline-flex min-h-10 min-w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-md border px-3 text-sm transition-colors";

function StepLink({
  href,
  label,
  icon,
  disabled,
}: {
  href: string;
  label: string;
  icon: "prev" | "next";
  disabled: boolean;
}) {
  const Icon = icon === "prev" ? ChevronLeft : ChevronRight;

  if (disabled) {
    return (
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          baseItem,
          "border-line text-ink-faint cursor-not-allowed opacity-60",
        )}
      >
        <Icon className="size-4" />
      </span>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      aria-label={label}
      className={cn(baseItem, "border-line bg-surface text-ink hover:border-ink")}
    >
      <Icon className="size-4" />
    </Link>
  );
}

export function Pagination({
  page,
  pageCount,
  hrefForPage,
  className,
}: {
  page: number;
  pageCount: number;
  hrefForPage: (page: number) => string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  if (pageCount <= 1) return null;

  const pages = Array.from({ length: pageCount }, (_, index) => index + 1);

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Постраничная навигация"
      className={cn("flex items-center justify-center gap-2", className)}
    >
      <StepLink
        href={hrefForPage(Math.max(1, page - 1))}
        label="Предыдущая страница"
        icon="prev"
        disabled={page <= 1}
      />

      <ul className="m-0 flex list-none items-center gap-2 p-0">
        {pages.map((item) => {
          const active = item === page;
          return (
            <li key={item}>
              {active ? (
                <span
                  aria-current="page"
                  className={cn(baseItem, "border-ink bg-ink text-canvas")}
                >
                  {item}
                </span>
              ) : (
                <Link
                  href={hrefForPage(item)}
                  aria-label={`Страница ${item}`}
                  className={cn(
                    baseItem,
                    "border-line bg-surface text-ink hover:border-ink",
                  )}
                >
                  {item}
                </Link>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>

      <StepLink
        href={hrefForPage(Math.min(pageCount, page + 1))}
        label="Следующая страница"
        icon="next"
        disabled={page >= pageCount}
      />
    </nav>
  );
}

  );

FILE: features/tasks/components/task-filters.tsx

import Link from "next/link";

import type { TaskFilters as TaskFiltersValue } from "@/features/tasks/api";
import {
  DIFFICULTIES,
  DIFFICULTY_LABEL,
  SUBJECTS,
  SUBJECT_LABEL,
} from "@/features/tasks/labels";
import type { TaskDifficulty, TaskSubject } from "@/features/tasks/types";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";
import { buildQueryHref } from "@/lib/utils/url";

type FilterOption<T extends string> = { value?: T; label: string };

function FilterGroup<T extends string>({
  label,
  options,
  current,
  hrefFor,
}: {
  label: string;
  options: readonly FilterOption<T>[];
  current?: T;
  hrefFor: (value?: T) => string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-2">
      <span className="text-ink-faint text-sm">{label}</span>
      <ul className="m-0 flex list-none flex-wrap gap-2 p-0">
        {options.map((option) => {
          const active = option.value === current;
          return (
            <li key={option.value ?? "all"}>
              <Link
                href={hrefFor(option.value)}
                aria-current={active ? "true" : undefined}
                className={cn(
                  "inline-flex min-h-9 items-center rounded-full border px-3.5 text-sm transition-colors",
                  active
                    ? "border-ink bg-ink text-canvas"
                    : "border-line bg-surface text-ink-soft hover:border-ink hover:text-ink",
                )}
              >
                {option.label}
              </Link>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

export function TaskFilters({
  basePath,
  filters,
}: {
  basePath: string;
  filters: TaskFiltersValue;
}) {
  const subjectOptions: readonly FilterOption<TaskSubject>[] = [
    { value: undefined, label: "Все предметы" },
    ...SUBJECTS.map((subject) => ({
      value: subject,
      label: SUBJECT_LABEL[subject],
    })),
  ];

  const levelOptions: readonly FilterOption<TaskDifficulty>[] = [
    { value: undefined, label: "Любая сложность" },
    ...DIFFICULTIES.map((level) => ({
      value: level,
      label: DIFFICULTY_LABEL[level],
    })),
  ];

  return (
    <div className="border-line mb-10 flex flex-col gap-3 border-b pb-6">
      <FilterGroup
        label="Предмет"
        options={subjectOptions}
        current={filters.subject}
        hrefFor={(subject) =>
          buildQueryHref(basePath, { subject, level: filters.level })
        }
      />
      <FilterGroup
        label="Сложность"
        options={levelOptions}
        current={filters.level}
        hrefFor={(level) =>
          buildQueryHref(basePath, { subject: filters.subject, level })
        }
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export function TaskList({

  className?: string;
}) {

    <ul
      className={cn(

FILE: features/tasks/components/task-statement.tsx

import { Math } from "@/components/ui/math";
import { RichText } from "@/components/ui/rich-text";
import type { StatementBlock } from "@/features/tasks/types";

function StatementBlockView({ block }: { block: StatementBlock }) {
  switch (block.type) {
    case "paragraph":
      return (
        <p className="text-ink text-lg leading-relaxed md:text-xl">
          <RichText text={block.text} />
        </p>
      );
    case "list":
      return block.ordered ? (
        <ol className="text-ink-soft m-0 list-decimal space-y-2 pl-6 text-lg">
          {block.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item}>
              <RichText text={item} />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      ) : (
        <ul className="text-ink-soft m-0 list-disc space-y-2 pl-6 text-lg">
          {block.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item}>
              <RichText text={item} />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      );
    case "formula":
      return (
        <figure className="border-accent-soft my-2 overflow-x-auto border-l-2 pl-4">
          <Math display tex={block.tex} />
          {block.caption ? (
            <figcaption className="text-ink-faint text-sm">{block.caption}</figcaption>
          ) : null}
        </figure>
      );
  }
}

export function TaskStatement({ blocks }: { blocks: readonly StatementBlock[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {blocks.map((block, index) => (
        <StatementBlockView key={`${block.type}-${index}`} block={block} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: features/topics/components/subject-hub.tsx

import Link from "next/link";

import { PageHero } from "@/components/layout/page-hero";
import { Section } from "@/components/layout/section";
import { Button, ButtonArrow } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ArticleCard } from "@/features/articles/components/article-card";
import { getArticlesBySubject } from "@/features/articles/api";
import { getTasks } from "@/features/tasks/api";
import { TaskList } from "@/features/tasks/components/task-list";
import { SUBJECT_LABEL } from "@/features/tasks/labels";
import type { TaskSubject } from "@/features/tasks/types";
import { SUBJECT_CONTENT } from "@/features/topics/content";
import { buildQueryHref } from "@/lib/utils/url";

export async function SubjectHub({ subject }: { subject: TaskSubject }) {
  const content = SUBJECT_CONTENT[subject];
  const [{ items: tasks, total }, articles] = await Promise.all([
    getTasks({ subject }, 1),
    getArticlesBySubject(subject, 2),
  ]);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        breadcrumbs={[{ name: "Главная", href: "/" }, { name: content.label }]}
        eyebrow={content.eyebrow}
        title={content.title}
        description={content.description}
      />

      <Section labelledBy="subject-covers-title">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-4 md:gap-6">
          <div className="desk:col-span-5 col-span-12">
            <h2 id="subject-covers-title" className="heading-3">
              Что разбираем
            </h2>
          </div>
          <ul className="text-ink-soft desk:col-span-6 desk:col-start-7 col-span-12 m-0 grid list-none gap-3 p-0 text-lg sm:grid-cols-2">
            {content.covers.map((item) => (
              <li
                key={item}
                className="border-line bg-surface flex items-start gap-3 rounded-md border px-4 py-3"
              >
                <span
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className="bg-accent mt-2 size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full"
                />
                <span>{item}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </Section>

      <Section labelledBy="subject-tasks-title">
        <div className="mb-10 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-6">
          <h2 id="subject-tasks-title" className="heading-3">
            Задачи по теме
          </h2>
          <p className="text-ink-soft text-sm">
            {total > 0 ? `Показано ${tasks.length} из ${total}` : "Пока нет задач"}
          </p>
        </div>

        {tasks.length > 0 ? (
          <TaskList tasks={tasks} />
        ) : (
          <p className="text-ink-soft">
            Задачи по этому предмету появятся здесь после подключения банка.
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="mt-10">
          <Button asChild variant="secondary">
            <Link href={buildQueryHref("/zadachi", { subject })}>
              Все задачи по предмету «{SUBJECT_LABEL[subject]}» <ButtonArrow />
            </Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Section>

      {articles.length > 0 ? (
        <Section labelledBy="subject-articles-title">
          <h2 id="subject-articles-title" className="heading-3 mb-10">
            Разборы по теме
          </h2>
          <ul className="desk:grid-cols-3 m-0 grid list-none gap-4 p-0 sm:grid-cols-2 md:gap-6">
            {articles.map((article) => (
              <li key={article.slug} className="flex">
                <ArticleCard article={article} />
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Section>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}




FILE: features/tutor-leads/api.ts

import { clientRequest } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";
import { buildLeadMailto, buildLeadTelegramHref } from "@/features/tutor-leads/content";
import type {
  ContactChannels,
  TutorLeadInput,
  TutorLeadDelivery,
} from "@/features/tutor-leads/types";

export async function submitTutorLead(
  input: TutorLeadInput,
  channels: ContactChannels,
): Promise<TutorLeadDelivery> {
  if (channels.apiConfigured) {
    await clientRequest<void>("/tutor-leads", { method: "POST", body: input });
    return "api";
  }

  if (channels.telegramHref) {
    window.location.assign(buildLeadTelegramHref(channels.telegramHref, input));
    return "telegram";
  }

  if (channels.emailHref) {
    window.location.assign(buildLeadMailto(channels.emailHref, input));
    return "email";
  }

  throw ApiError.unconfigured("No contact channel is configured for tutor leads");
}

  },

    answer:
      "Задача — чтобы вы решали сами. На экзамене готовый ответ не поможет: важен ход мысли, поэтому мы разбираем его, а не копируем.",

    answer: "Напишите заранее — согласуем другое время.",
  },

export const SUBJECT_OPTIONS: readonly {
  value: TutorLeadSubject;
  label: string;
}[] = [
  { value: "matematika", label: "Математика" },
  { value: "informatika", label: "Информатика" },
  { value: "both", label: "Математика и информатика" },
];

export const EXAM_OPTIONS: readonly { value: TutorLeadExam; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "oge", label: "ОГЭ" },
  { value: "ege", label: "ЕГЭ" },
  { value: "other", label: "Другое" },
];

export function subjectLabel(subject: TutorLeadSubject): string {
  return SUBJECT_OPTIONS.find((option) => option.value === subject)?.label ?? subject;
}

export function examLabel(exam: TutorLeadExam): string {
  return EXAM_OPTIONS.find((option) => option.value === exam)?.label ?? exam;
}

export function buildLeadMessage(input: TutorLeadInput): string {
  const lines = ["Заявка на занятия", "", `Экзамен: ${examLabel(input.exam)}`];

  if (input.name?.trim()) lines.push(`Имя: ${input.name.trim()}`);
  lines.push(`Связь: ${input.contact}`);
  if (input.subject) lines.push(`Предмет: ${subjectLabel(input.subject)}`);

  if (input.message?.trim()) {
    lines.push(`Комментарий: ${input.message.trim()}`);
  }

  return lines.join("\n");
}

export function buildLeadMailto(mailtoHref: string, input: TutorLeadInput): string {
  const subject = "Заявка на занятия";
  const body = buildLeadMessage(input);
  return `${mailtoHref}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(subject)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;
}

export function buildLeadTelegramHref(
  telegramHref: string,
  input: TutorLeadInput,
): string {
  return `${telegramHref}?text=${encodeURIComponent(buildLeadMessage(input))}`;
}

FILE: features/tutor-leads/reviews.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { selectPublishedReviews } from "@/features/tutor-leads/reviews";
import type { Review } from "@/features/tutor-leads/types";

const base: Omit<Review, "name" | "text" | "consentGiven"> = {
  exam: "ЕГЭ · профиль",
};

const withConsent: Review = {
  ...base,
  name: "A",
  text: "Понравилось.",
  consentGiven: true,
};

const withoutConsent: Review = {
  ...base,
  name: "B",
  text: "Тоже отзыв.",
  consentGiven: false,
};

const blank: Review = {
  ...base,
  name: "C",
  text: "   ",
  consentGiven: true,
};

describe("selectPublishedReviews", () => {
  it("keeps only consented reviews", () => {
    expect(selectPublishedReviews([withConsent, withoutConsent])).toEqual([
      withConsent,
    ]);
  });

  it("drops blank texts even with consent", () => {
    expect(selectPublishedReviews([blank])).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("returns an empty list for no reviews", () => {
    expect(selectPublishedReviews([])).toEqual([]);
  });
});




FILE: features/tutor-leads/reviews.ts

import type { Review } from "@/features/tutor-leads/types";

/**
 * Reviews may only be published with explicit consent. Kept as a pure function
 * so the filtering rule is covered by a unit test and cannot be bypassed in the
 * component.
 */
export function selectPublishedReviews(reviews: readonly Review[]): Review[] {
  return reviews.filter((review) => review.consentGiven && review.text.trim().length > 0);
}




FILE: features/tutor-leads/schema.ts

import { z } from "zod";

export const TUTOR_LEAD_EXAMS = ["oge", "ege", "other"] as const;

export const tutorLeadSchema = z.object({
  contact: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .min(3, "Оставьте Telegram, e‑mail или телефон для связи")
    .max(120, "Слишком длинный контакт"),
  exam: z.enum(TUTOR_LEAD_EXAMS, {
    message: "Выберите экзамен",
  }),
  name: z.string().trim().max(80, "Слишком длинное имя").optional(),
  subject: z.enum(["matematika", "informatika", "both"]).optional(),
  message: z.string().trim().max(1000, "Максимум 1000 символов").optional(),
  consent: z.boolean().refine((value) => value, {
    message: "Нужно согласие на обработку персональных данных",
  }),
});

export type TutorLeadFormValues = z.infer<typeof tutorLeadSchema>;




FILE: features/tutor-leads/types.ts

export type TutorLeadSubject = "matematika" | "informatika" | "both";
export type TutorLeadExam = "oge" | "ege" | "other";

export type TutorLeadInput = {
  /** Optional: the fast path only requires contact and exam. */
  name?: string;
  contact: string;
  exam: TutorLeadExam;
  subject?: TutorLeadSubject;
  message?: string;
  consent: true;
};

export type ContactChannels = {
  telegramHref: string | null;
  emailHref: string | null;
  apiConfigured: boolean;
};

export type TutorLeadDelivery = "api" | "telegram" | "email";

export type Review = {
  /** Reviewer name. */
  name: string;
  /** Exam the review is about, e.g. "ЕГЭ · профиль". */
  exam: string;
  text: string;
  /** Optional "before" level, only if the reviewer provided it. */
  before?: string;
  /** Optional "after" result, only if the reviewer provided it. */
  after?: string;
  /** Must be true; reviews without consent are never rendered. */
  consentGiven: boolean;
};

export type PricingInfo = {
  /** e.g. "от 2 000 ₽ за занятие". */
  from: string;
  /** e.g. "индивидуально, онлайн". */
  format?: string;
  /** e.g. "60 минут". */
  duration?: string;
  note?: string;
};

export type FaqItem = {
  question: string;
  answer: string;
};




FILE: features/tutor-leads/components/faq.tsx

import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";

import { FAQ_ITEMS } from "@/features/tutor-leads/content";
import type { FaqItem } from "@/features/tutor-leads/types";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

/**
 * Native `details`/`summary` disclosure: keyboard accessible and functional
 * without JavaScript. The same items are mirrored into FAQPage JSON-LD.
 */
export function Faq({
  items = FAQ_ITEMS,
  className,
}: {
  items?: readonly FaqItem[];
  className?: string;
}) {
  if (items.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className={cn("border-line border-t", className)}>
      {items.map((item) => (
        <details key={item.question} className="group border-line border-b">
          <summary className="text-ink hover:text-accent flex min-h-14 cursor-pointer list-none items-center justify-between gap-4 py-4 text-lg font-medium transition-colors [&::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden">
            {item.question}
            <ChevronDown
              aria-hidden
              className="text-ink-soft size-5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-open:rotate-180"
            />
          </summary>
          <p className="text-ink-soft pb-5 text-[17px] leading-relaxed">
            {item.answer}
          </p>
        </details>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}




FILE: features/tutor-leads/components/lead-form.test.tsx

import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { submitTutorLead } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  submitTutorLead: vi.fn(async () => "api" as const),
}));

vi.mock("@/features/tutor-leads/api", () => ({ submitTutorLead }));

import { TutorLeadForm } from "@/features/tutor-leads/components/lead-form";

const channels = {
  telegramHref: "https://t.me/tutor",
  emailHref: null,
  apiConfigured: true,
};

describe("TutorLeadForm", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    submitTutorLead.mockClear();
  });

  it("requires only contact and consent, leaving the rest optional", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<TutorLeadForm channels={channels} />);

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Отправить заявку" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Оставьте Telegram/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(submitTutorLead).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText("Как с вами связаться"),
      "@student",
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText(/Согласен на обработку/));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Отправить заявку" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(submitTutorLead).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Заявка отправлена")).toBeInTheDocument();

    const call = submitTutorLead.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [
      { name?: string; contact: string; exam: string },
    ];
    const input = call[0];
    expect(input.name).toBeUndefined();
    expect(input.contact).toBe("@student");
    expect(input.exam).toBe("ege");
  });

  it("offers a Telegram quick path with a prefilled message", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const open = vi.spyOn(window, "open").mockReturnValue(null);
    render(<TutorLeadForm channels={channels} />);

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /Написать в Telegram/ }));

    expect(open).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const url = String(open.mock.calls[0]?.[0] ?? "");
    expect(url).toContain("https://t.me/tutor?text=");
    expect(decodeURIComponent(url)).toContain("Заявка на занятия");
    open.mockRestore();
  });
});




FILE: features/tutor-leads/components/lead-form.tsx

"use client";

import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { CircleAlert, CircleCheck, Send, SendHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { submitTutorLead } from "@/features/tutor-leads/api";
import {
  buildLeadTelegramHref,
  EXAM_OPTIONS,
  SUBJECT_OPTIONS,
} from "@/features/tutor-leads/content";
import {
  tutorLeadSchema,
  type TutorLeadFormValues,
} from "@/features/tutor-leads/schema";
import type {
  ContactChannels,
  TutorLeadDelivery,
  TutorLeadInput,
} from "@/features/tutor-leads/types";
import { ANALYTICS_EVENTS, track } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { describeApiError } from "@/lib/api/errors";

const DELIVERY_MESSAGE: Record<TutorLeadDelivery, string> = {
  api: "Заявка отправлена. Свяжусь с вами, чтобы договориться о времени.",
  telegram: "Открываю Telegram с готовым сообщением — останется нажать «Отправить».",
  email: "Открываю почтовый клиент с готовым письмом — останется нажать «Отправить».",
};

const INPUT_CLASS =
  "border-line-strong bg-surface text-ink mt-2 min-h-12 w-full rounded-md border px-4 text-base";

function FieldError({ id, message }: { id: string; message?: string }) {
  if (!message) return null;
  return (
    <p id={id} role="alert" className="text-danger mt-1.5 text-sm">
      {message}
    </p>
  );
}

function toInput(values: TutorLeadFormValues): TutorLeadInput {
  return {
    name: values.name?.trim() || undefined,
    contact: values.contact,
    exam: values.exam,
    subject: values.subject,
    message: values.message,
    consent: true,
  };
}

export function TutorLeadForm({ channels }: { channels: ContactChannels }) {
  const [delivery, setDelivery] = useState<TutorLeadDelivery | null>(null);
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const startedRef = useRef(false);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    getValues,
    reset,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<TutorLeadFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(tutorLeadSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      contact: "",
      exam: "ege",
      name: "",
      subject: "both",
      message: "",
      consent: false,
    },
  });

  function markStarted() {
    if (startedRef.current) return;
    startedRef.current = true;
    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.leadFormStart);
  }

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit(async (values) => {
    setPending(true);
    setFailure(null);
    try {
      const result = await submitTutorLead(toInput(values), channels);
      setDelivery(result);
      track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.leadFormSubmit, { channel: result });
      reset();
    } catch (error) {
      setFailure(describeApiError(error));
      track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.leadFormError, { stage: "submit" });
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  });

  function openTelegram() {
    if (!channels.telegramHref) return;
    markStarted();
    window.open(
      buildLeadTelegramHref(channels.telegramHref, toInput(getValues())),
      "_blank",
      "noopener,noreferrer",
    );
    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.leadFormSubmit, { channel: "telegram" });
  }

  if (delivery) {
    return (
      <div
        role="status"
        data-lead-form=""
        className="border-success/40 bg-success-soft shadow-card flex flex-col items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border p-6 md:p-8"
      >
        <CircleCheck aria-hidden className="text-success size-6" />
        <p className="text-ink text-lg font-medium">
          {delivery === "api" ? "Заявка отправлена" : "Сообщение готово"}
        </p>
        <p className="text-ink-soft">{DELIVERY_MESSAGE[delivery]}</p>
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="secondary"
          size="sm"
          className="mt-2"
          onClick={() => setDelivery(null)}
        >
          Отправить ещё одну
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={onSubmit}
      onFocusCapture={markStarted}
      noValidate
      data-lead-form=""
      className="border-line bg-surface shadow-card flex flex-col gap-5 rounded-xl border p-6 md:p-8"
    >
      <p className="text-ink-soft text-sm">
        Достаточно контакта и экзамена — остальное можно не заполнять.
      </p>

      <div>
        <Label htmlFor="lead-contact">Как с вами связаться</Label>
        <Input
          id="lead-contact"
          className="mt-2"
          autoComplete="email"
          placeholder="Telegram, e‑mail или телефон"
          aria-invalid={errors.contact ? true : undefined}
          aria-describedby={errors.contact ? "lead-contact-error" : undefined}
          {...register("contact")}
        />
        <FieldError id="lead-contact-error" message={errors.contact?.message} />
      </div>

      <div>
        <Label htmlFor="lead-exam">Экзамен</Label>
        <select
          id="lead-exam"
          className={INPUT_CLASS}
          aria-invalid={errors.exam ? true : undefined}
          {...register("exam")}
        >
          {EXAM_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
            <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
              {option.label}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <FieldError id="lead-exam-error" message={errors.exam?.message} />
      </div>

      <details className="group border-line rounded-md border px-4 py-3">
        <summary className="text-ink cursor-pointer text-sm font-medium">
          Добавить детали (необязательно)
        </summary>
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-5">
          <div>
            <Label htmlFor="lead-name">Имя</Label>
            <Input
              id="lead-name"
              className="mt-2"
              autoComplete="name"
              {...register("name")}
            />
            <FieldError id="lead-name-error" message={errors.name?.message} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <Label htmlFor="lead-subject">Предмет</Label>
            <select id="lead-subject" className={INPUT_CLASS} {...register("subject")}>
              {SUBJECT_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
                <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                  {option.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <Label htmlFor="lead-message">Комментарий</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="lead-message"
              className="mt-2"
              placeholder="Класс, цель, что уже не получается"
              {...register("message")}
            />
            <FieldError id="lead-message-error" message={errors.message?.message} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </details>

      <div>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <input
            id="lead-consent"
            type="checkbox"
            className="accent-accent mt-1 size-5 shrink-0"
            aria-invalid={errors.consent ? true : undefined}
            aria-describedby={errors.consent ? "lead-consent-error" : undefined}
            {...register("consent")}
          />
          <Label htmlFor="lead-consent" className="leading-snug font-normal">
            Согласен на обработку персональных данных для ответа на заявку.{" "}
            <Link href="/privacy" className="underline underline-offset-2">
              Политика обработки данных
            </Link>
          </Label>
        </div>
        <FieldError id="lead-consent-error" message={errors.consent?.message} />
      </div>

      {failure ? (
        <p
          role="alert"
          className="border-danger/40 bg-danger-soft text-danger flex items-start gap-2 rounded-md border px-4 py-3 text-sm"
        >
          <CircleAlert aria-hidden className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" />
          <span>{failure}</span>
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <Button type="submit" disabled={pending}>
          <Send aria-hidden className="size-4" />
          {pending ? "Отправляем…" : "Отправить заявку"}
        </Button>

        {channels.telegramHref ? (
          <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={openTelegram}>
            <SendHorizontal aria-hidden className="size-4" />
            Написать в Telegram
          </Button>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <p className="text-ink-faint text-sm">
        {channels.apiConfigured
          ? "Заявка уходит на сервер и не публикуется."
          : "Сообщение формируется на вашем устройстве и отправляется из вашего Telegram или почтового клиента."}
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

FILE: features/tutor-leads/components/sticky-cta.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

import { REQUEST_PATH } from "@/features/tutor-leads/content";
import { ANALYTICS_EVENTS, track } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

/** Sticky CTA is only useful where a "warm" moment exists. */
function isRelevantPath(pathname: string): boolean {
  if (pathname === "/") return true;
  return pathname.startsWith("/zadachi/") || pathname.startsWith("/articles/");
}

/**
 * Mobile-only sticky request bar. It appears after the visitor scrolls past the
 * first screen and hides as soon as a lead form enters the viewport, so it never
 * covers the form itself. Layout properties reserve space at the end of the
 * document and respect the safe area on notched devices.
 */
export function StickyCta() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const relevant = isRelevantPath(pathname);
  const [scrolledPast, setScrolledPast] = useState(false);
  const [formVisible, setFormVisible] = useState(false);
  const viewedRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!relevant) return;

    const onScroll = () => setScrolledPast(window.scrollY > 360);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    const frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(onScroll);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
      window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
  }, [relevant]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!relevant) return;

    const forms = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-lead-form]"));
    if (forms.length === 0 || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") {
      const frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => setFormVisible(false));
      return () => window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
    }

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        setFormVisible(entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting));
      },
      { rootMargin: "0px 0px -15% 0px", threshold: 0.1 },
    );
    for (const form of forms) observer.observe(form);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [relevant, pathname]);

  const visible = scrolledPast && !formVisible;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible || viewedRef.current) return;
    viewedRef.current = true;
    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.ctaView, { placement: "sticky-mobile" });
  }, [visible]);

  if (!relevant) return null;

  return (
    <div className="md:hidden">
      <div aria-hidden className="h-20" />
      {visible ? (
        <nav
          aria-label="Быстрая запись на пробное"
          className={cn(
            "border-line bg-canvas/95 fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 border-t px-4 pt-3 backdrop-blur-[14px]",
            "pb-[calc(0.75rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))]",
          )}
        >
          <Link
            href={REQUEST_PATH}
            onClick={() =>
              track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.ctaClick, { placement: "sticky-mobile" })
            }
            className="bg-accent hover:bg-accent-strong flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md text-base font-medium text-white transition-colors"
          >
            Записаться на пробное
            <ArrowRight aria-hidden className="size-4" />
          </Link>
        </nav>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}




FILE: features/tutor-leads/components/tutor-cta.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  CTA_CONTENT,
  REQUEST_PATH,
  type CtaPlacement,
} from "@/features/tutor-leads/content";
import { ANALYTICS_EVENTS, track } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils/cn";

type TutorCtaProps = {
  placement: CtaPlacement;
  /** Tighter layout for dense spots such as the home quiz. */
  size?: "default" | "compact";
  className?: string;
};

/**
 * Placement-specific conversion block. It always points at the request form on
 * `/podgotovka` and is deliberately inline (never a modal) so it never
 * interrupts reading or keyboard flow.
 */
export function TutorCta({ placement, size = "default", className }: TutorCtaProps) {
  const copy = CTA_CONTENT[placement];
  const compact = size === "compact";
  const ref = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = ref.current;
    if (!element || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") return;

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (!entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)) return;
        observer.disconnect();
        track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.ctaView, { placement });
      },
      { threshold: 0.25 },
    );
    observer.observe(element);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [placement]);

  return (
    <aside
      ref={ref}
      aria-label={copy.title}
      data-cta-placement={placement}
      className={cn(
        "border-accent/25 bg-accent-soft/60 rounded-xl border",
        compact ? "p-5" : "p-6 md:p-7",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <span className="text-accent mb-2 block text-[12px] font-medium tracking-[0.08em] uppercase">
        {copy.eyebrow}
      </span>
      <p
        className={cn(
          "text-ink font-semibold",
          compact ? "text-base" : "text-lg md:text-xl",
        )}
      >
        {copy.title}
      </p>
      <p className="text-ink-soft mt-2 text-[15px] leading-relaxed">{copy.text}</p>
      <Button asChild size="sm" className="mt-4">
        <Link
          href={REQUEST_PATH}
          onClick={() => track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.ctaClick, { placement })}
        >
          {copy.action}
          <ArrowRight aria-hidden className="size-4" />
        </Link>
      </Button>
    </aside>
  );
}

    <TutorLeadForm
      channels={{ telegramHref: telegram, emailHref: email, apiConfigured }}
    />
  );
}

FILE: lib/theme.ts

/**
 * Theme preference: "light" or "dark".
 *
 * The choice is persisted in `localStorage` and reflected as a `data-theme`
 * attribute on <html>, which the stylesheet reads (see globals.css). On the
 * first visit (nothing stored yet) the OS preference is resolved and used as
 * the default, without persisting it until the user picks a button.
 *
 * `themeInitScript` must run before first paint to avoid a flash of the wrong
 * theme; it is injected with `strategy="beforeInteractive"` from the root
 * layout.
 */

export type ThemePreference = "light" | "dark";

export const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = "theme";

const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function emit(): void {
  for (const listener of listeners) listener();
}

function isTheme(value: unknown): value is ThemePreference {
  return value === "light" || value === "dark";
}

function systemTheme(): ThemePreference {
  return window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches ? "dark" : "light";
}

export function readStoredTheme(): ThemePreference | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  try {
    const value = window.localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (isTheme(value)) return value;
  } catch {
    // localStorage can be unavailable (private mode, blocked storage).
  }
  return null;
}

export function applyThemePreference(preference: ThemePreference): void {
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = preference;
}

export function setThemePreference(preference: ThemePreference): void {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, preference);
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage failures; the attribute below still updates.
  }
  applyThemePreference(preference);
  emit();
}

export function subscribeTheme(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);

  const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
    if (event.key === THEME_STORAGE_KEY || event.key === null) {
      applyThemePreference(readStoredTheme() ?? systemTheme());
      emit();
    }
  };
  window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);

  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
    window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
  };
}

/**
 * Snapshot for `useSyncExternalStore`. Reads the attribute (kept in sync with
 * storage by the init script and `setThemePreference`) so the value is stable
 * between emits.
 */
export function getThemePreference(): ThemePreference {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return "light";
  const value = document.documentElement.dataset.theme;
  return isTheme(value) ? value : systemTheme();
}

export const themeInitScript = `(function(){try{var value=localStorage.getItem(${JSON.stringify(
  THEME_STORAGE_KEY,
)});if(value!=="light"&&value!=="dark"){value=matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches?"dark":"light"}document.documentElement.dataset.theme=value}catch(e){}})();`;
