FILE: lib/analytics/track.test.ts

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { ANALYTICS_EVENTS, track } from "@/lib/analytics/track";

const ENV_KEY = "NEXT_PUBLIC_YM_ID";

describe("track", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    delete window.ym;
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    delete window.ym;
  });

  it("is a no-op without NEXT_PUBLIC_YM_ID", () => {
    vi.stubEnv(ENV_KEY, "");
    const ym = vi.fn();
    window.ym = ym;

    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.quizAnswer, { correct: true });

    expect(ym).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("does not throw when the tag is not loaded yet", () => {
    vi.stubEnv(ENV_KEY, "12345678");
    expect(() => track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.solutionOpen)).not.toThrow();
  });

  it("sends reachGoal with the counter id when configured", () => {
    vi.stubEnv(ENV_KEY, "12345678");
    const ym = vi.fn();
    window.ym = ym;

    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.ctaClick, { placement: "after-solution" });

    expect(ym).toHaveBeenCalledWith(12345678, "reachGoal", "cta_click", {
      placement: "after-solution",
    });
  });

  it("never forwards personal data from the form", () => {
    vi.stubEnv(ENV_KEY, "123");
    const ym = vi.fn();
    window.ym = ym;

    track(ANALYTICS_EVENTS.leadFormSubmit, {
      channel: "api",
      name: "Иван",
      contact: "@ivan",
      message: "текст",
    });

    expect(ym).toHaveBeenCalledWith(123, "reachGoal", "lead_form_submit", {
      channel: "api",
    });
  });
});




FILE: lib/analytics/track.ts
export type AnalyticsParams = Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined>;

export const ANALYTICS_EVENTS = {
  quizAnswer: "quiz_answer",
  taskAnswerAttempt: "task_answer_attempt",
  solutionOpen: "solution_open",
  ctaView: "cta_view",
  ctaClick: "cta_click",
  leadFormStart: "lead_form_start",
  leadFormSubmit: "lead_form_submit",
  leadFormError: "lead_form_error",
} as const;

export type AnalyticsEvent =
  (typeof ANALYTICS_EVENTS)[keyof typeof ANALYTICS_EVENTS];

declare global {
  interface Window {
    ym?: (
      counterId: number,
      action: string,
      event: string,
      params?: AnalyticsParams,
    ) => void;
  }
}

const PRIVATE_KEYS = new Set([
  "name",
  "contact",
  "email",
  "phone",
  "message",
  "comment",
  "answer",
]);

const MAX_VALUE_LENGTH = 64;

function sanitize(params?: AnalyticsParams): AnalyticsParams | undefined {
  if (!params) return undefined;

  const clean: AnalyticsParams = {};
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (value === undefined) continue;
    if (PRIVATE_KEYS.has(key.toLowerCase())) continue;
    clean[key] = typeof value === "string" ? value.slice(0, MAX_VALUE_LENGTH) : value;
  }
  return clean;
}

export function isAnalyticsEnabled(): boolean {
  return Boolean(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_YM_ID?.trim());
}

export function track(event: AnalyticsEvent, params?: AnalyticsParams): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

  const rawId = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_YM_ID?.trim();
  if (!rawId) return;

  const counterId = Number(rawId);
  if (!Number.isFinite(counterId)) return;

  const ym = window.ym;
  if (typeof ym !== "function") return;

  ym(counterId, "reachGoal", event, sanitize(params));
}


FILE: lib/api/client.ts

import { apiConfig, apiUrl, isPublicApiConfigured } from "./config";
import { ApiError, toApiError } from "./errors";

export type ClientRequestOptions = Omit<RequestInit, "body"> & {
  body?: unknown;
};

export async function clientRequest<T>(
  path: string,
  options: ClientRequestOptions = {},
): Promise<T> {
  if (!isPublicApiConfigured()) {
    throw ApiError.unconfigured(
      `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL is not configured, cannot request ${path}.`,
    );
  }

  const { body, headers, ...rest } = options;
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), apiConfig.timeoutMs);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(apiUrl(apiConfig.publicBaseUrl, path), {
      // Cookie-based sessions: send/receive the HttpOnly session cookie.
      credentials: "include",
      ...rest,
      signal: rest.signal ?? controller.signal,
      headers: {
        Accept: "application/json",
        ...(body !== undefined ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
        ...headers,
      },
      ...(body !== undefined ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw await ApiError.fromResponse(response);
    }

    if (response.status === 204) {
      return undefined as T;
    }

    return (await response.json()) as T;
  } catch (error) {
    throw toApiError(error);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
  }
}




FILE: lib/api/config.ts

/**
 * Local FastAPI backend used when no explicit origin is configured.
 *
 * The default applies to `next dev` only: production builds and deployments
 * must set the API origin explicitly, otherwise content pages have no source
 * of truth.
 */
export const DEFAULT_DEV_API_ORIGIN = "http://localhost:8000";

export function isDevelopment(): boolean {
  return process.env.NODE_ENV === "development";
}

function normalizeOrigin(value: string | undefined): string {
  return value?.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "") ?? "";
}

export const apiConfig = {
  publicBaseUrl:
    normalizeOrigin(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL) ||
    (isDevelopment() ? DEFAULT_DEV_API_ORIGIN : ""),
  timeoutMs: 10_000,
} as const;

/**
 * Versioned path prefix appended to every API endpoint. Both base URLs are
 * configured as a bare origin (e.g. `http://localhost:8000`); `/api/v1` is
 * added centrally here so it never has to be repeated in callers or env vars.
 */
export const API_PREFIX = "/api/v1";

export function isPublicApiConfigured(): boolean {
  return apiConfig.publicBaseUrl.length > 0;
}

export function joinUrl(base: string, path: string): string {
  return `${base.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/${path.replace(/^\/+/, "")}`;
}

/** Build the absolute URL for a versioned endpoint: `apiUrl(base, "/tasks")` → `base/api/v1/tasks`. */
export function apiUrl(base: string, path: string): string {
  return joinUrl(base, `${API_PREFIX}/${path.replace(/^\/+/, "")}`);
}




FILE: lib/api/errors.ts

export type ApiErrorKind = "unconfigured" | "network" | "timeout" | "http" | "unknown";

type ApiErrorOptions = {
  status?: number;
  details?: unknown;
  cause?: unknown;
};

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly kind: ApiErrorKind;
  readonly status?: number;
  readonly details?: unknown;

  constructor(kind: ApiErrorKind, message: string, options: ApiErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(message, { cause: options.cause });
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.kind = kind;
    this.status = options.status;
    this.details = options.details;
  }

  static unconfigured(message: string): ApiError {
    return new ApiError("unconfigured", message);
  }

  static async fromResponse(response: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
    let details: unknown;
    try {
      details = await response.json();
    } catch {
      details = await response.text().catch(() => undefined);
    }
    return new ApiError("http", `Request failed with status ${response.status}`, {
      status: response.status,
      details,
    });
  }
}

export function isApiError(error: unknown): error is ApiError {
  return error instanceof ApiError;
}

export function toApiError(error: unknown): ApiError {
  if (isApiError(error)) return error;
  if (error instanceof DOMException && error.name === "AbortError") {
    return new ApiError("timeout", "Request timed out", { cause: error });
  }
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    return new ApiError("network", error.message, { cause: error });
  }
  return new ApiError("unknown", "Unknown API error", { details: error });
}

export function describeApiError(error: unknown): string {
  if (!isApiError(error)) {
    return "Не удалось загрузить данные. Попробуйте обновить страницу.";
  }
  switch (error.kind) {
    case "unconfigured":
      return "Сервер данных ещё не подключён. Пока показываем встроенный демонстрационный набор.";
    case "timeout":
      return "Сервер отвечает слишком долго. Попробуйте ещё раз чуть позже.";
    case "http":
      return error.status === 404
        ? "Запрошенные данные не найдены."
        : "Сервер вернул ошибку. Попробуйте ещё раз чуть позже.";
    case "network":
      return "Нет связи с сервером. Проверьте подключение к сети.";
    default:
      return "Не удалось загрузить данные. Попробуйте обновить страницу.";
  }
}




FILE: lib/api/server.ts

import "server-only";

import { apiConfig, apiUrl, DEFAULT_DEV_API_ORIGIN, isDevelopment } from "./config";
import { ApiError, toApiError } from "./errors";

const configuredServerBaseUrl =
  process.env.API_BASE_URL?.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "") ?? "";

/**
 * In local development the backend runs on :8000 by default, so `pnpm dev`
 * needs no extra configuration. Production must provide `API_BASE_URL`.
 */
const serverBaseUrl =
  configuredServerBaseUrl || (isDevelopment() ? DEFAULT_DEV_API_ORIGIN : "");

export function isServerApiConfigured(): boolean {
  return serverBaseUrl.length > 0;
}

export type QueryValue = string | number | boolean | undefined | null;

export type ServerRequestOptions = {
  searchParams?: Record<string, QueryValue>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  tags?: string[];
};

export async function serverRequest<T>(
  path: string,
  options: ServerRequestOptions = {},
): Promise<T> {
  if (!isServerApiConfigured()) {
    throw ApiError.unconfigured(
      `API_BASE_URL is not configured, cannot request ${path}. ` +
        "Add it to .env.local once the backend is available.",
    );
  }

  const url = new URL(apiUrl(serverBaseUrl, path));
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(options.searchParams ?? {})) {
    if (value !== undefined && value !== null) {
      url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
    }
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), apiConfig.timeoutMs);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
      signal: options.signal ?? controller.signal,
      ...(options.tags ? { next: { tags: options.tags } } : {}),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw await ApiError.fromResponse(response);
    }

    return (await response.json()) as T;
  } catch (error) {
    throw toApiError(error);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
  }
}




FILE: lib/api/generated/README.md

# Generated API types

This directory is reserved for TypeScript types generated from the backend
OpenAPI schema.

Planned workflow once the FastAPI service exists:

```bash
# 1. Export the schema from the backend
curl http://localhost:8000/openapi.json -o openapi.json

# 2. Generate types into src/lib/api/generated/schema.d.ts
pnpm dlx openapi-typescript ./openapi.json -o src/lib/api/generated/schema.d.ts
```

Rules:

- Generated files are **not** edited by hand.
- Domain types used by components live next to their feature
  (`src/features/<feature>/types.ts`).
- The API functions in `server.ts` / `client.ts` map generated DTOs onto those
  domain types, so components never depend on raw HTTP shapes.
- `src/features/*/api.ts` always reads tasks and articles from the backend in
  normal operation (`http://localhost:8000` by default in `next dev`). Local
  fixtures are only a fallback for builds without a configured API origin.




FILE: lib/config/deploy-check.test.ts

import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const SCRIPT = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "scripts/check-production-env.mjs");
const tempDirs: string[] = [];

function makeDir(files: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "osnova-env-"));
  tempDirs.push(dir);
  for (const [name, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, name), content, "utf8");
  }
  return dir;
}

function run(env: Record<string, string>, args: string[] = [], cwd?: string) {
  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [SCRIPT, ...args], {
    cwd: cwd ?? makeDir(),
    env: {
      PATH: process.env.PATH,
      NODE_ENV: process.env.NODE_ENV ?? "production",
      ...env,
    },
    encoding: "utf8",
  });
  return {
    status: result.status,
    output: `${result.stdout ?? ""}${result.stderr ?? ""}`,
  };
}

afterEach(() => {
  for (const dir of tempDirs.splice(0)) {
    fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});

describe("check-production-env", () => {
  it("fails without a site URL and without a lead channel", () => {
    const result = run({});
    expect(result.status).toBe(1);
    expect(result.output).toContain("NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL is not set");
    expect(result.output).toContain("No lead channel");
  });

  it("treats a whitespace-only site URL as missing", () => {
    const result = run({
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "   ",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM: "@test",
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(1);
    expect(result.output).toContain("NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL is not set");
  });

  it("rejects localhost and non-HTTPS production URLs", () => {
    const local = run({
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "http://localhost:3000",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM: "@test",
    });
    expect(local.status).toBe(1);
    expect(local.output).toContain("local host");

    const http = run({
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "http://osnova-lab.ru",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM: "@test",
    });
    expect(http.status).toBe(1);
    expect(http.output).toContain("https");
  });

  it("rejects a site URL with a path", () => {
    const result = run({
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "https://osnova-lab.ru/app",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM: "@test",
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(1);
    expect(result.output).toContain("bare origin");
  });

  it("accepts osnova-lab.ru with a lead channel", () => {
    const result = run({
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "https://osnova-lab.ru",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM: "@test",
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(0);
    expect(result.output).toContain("Production environment check passed");
  });

  it("requires a lead channel in production", () => {
    const result = run({ NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "https://osnova-lab.ru" });
    expect(result.status).toBe(1);
    expect(result.output).toContain("No lead channel");
  });

  it("rejects an invalid public API origin", () => {
    const result = run({
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "https://osnova-lab.ru",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL: "not a url",
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(1);
    expect(result.output).toContain("NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL");
  });

  it("prefers .env.production.local over .env.production", () => {
    const dir = makeDir({
      ".env.production.local":
        "NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL=http://localhost:3000\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM=@test\n",
      ".env.production": "NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL=https://osnova-lab.ru\n",
    });
    const result = run({}, [], dir);
    expect(result.status).toBe(1);
    expect(result.output).toContain("local host");
  });

  it("lets process env override env files", () => {
    const dir = makeDir({
      ".env.production":
        "NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL=http://localhost:3000\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM=@test\n",
    });
    const result = run(
      {
        NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "https://osnova-lab.ru",
        NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM: "@test",
      },
      [],
      dir,
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(0);
  });

  it("allows http://localhost in --local mode", () => {
    const result = run({ NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "http://localhost:3000" }, ["--local"]);
    expect(result.status).toBe(0);
    expect(result.output).toContain("Local environment check passed");
  });

  it("never prints secret values", () => {
    const secret = "super-secret-token-value";
    const result = run({
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: "https://osnova-lab.ru",
      NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM: "@test",
      TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: secret,
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(0);
    expect(result.output).not.toContain(secret);
  });
});

FILE: lib/constants/site.ts


function normalizeSiteUrl(raw: string | undefined): string {
  const value = raw?.trim();
  if (!value) return "http://localhost:3000";
  const withProtocol = /^https?:\/\//i.test(value) ? value : `https://${value}`;
  return withProtocol.replace(/\/+$/, "");
}

const tutorName = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_NAME?.trim() ?? "";
const tutorTelegram = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_TELEGRAM?.trim() ?? "";
const tutorEmail = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_EMAIL?.trim() ?? "";
const tutorPhoto = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_PHOTO?.trim() ?? "";
const tutorVideo = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_VIDEO?.trim() ?? "";
const tutorAbout = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TUTOR_ABOUT?.trim() ?? "";

export const siteConfig = {
  name: "Математика и информатика",
  url: normalizeSiteUrl(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL),
  locale: "ru_RU",
  description:
    "Бесплатный банк задач по математике и информатике с пошаговыми разборами. Индивидуальные занятия для подготовки к ОГЭ и ЕГЭ.",
  tutor: {
    name: tutorName,
    telegram: tutorTelegram,
    email: tutorEmail,
    photo: tutorPhoto,
    video: tutorVideo,
    about: tutorAbout,
  },
} as const;

export const hasTutorContacts = tutorTelegram.length > 0 || tutorEmail.length > 0;

/** True when there is enough real data to render a teacher block. */
export const hasTutorProfile = tutorName.length > 0 && tutorAbout.length > 0;

export const brand: { text: string; accent: string } = tutorName
  ? { text: tutorName, accent: "." }
  : { text: "Математика", accent: "· Информатика" };

export function telegramHref(value: string): string | null {
  const handle = value.trim();
  if (!handle) return null;
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(handle)) return handle;
  return `https://t.me/${handle.replace(/^@/, "")}`;
}

export function mailHref(value: string): string | null {
  const email = value.trim();
  return email ? `mailto:${email}` : null;
}




FILE: lib/seo/json-ld.ts

import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/constants/site";
import { absoluteUrl } from "@/lib/seo/metadata";

export type JsonLdObject = Record<string, unknown>;

export function webSiteJsonLd(): JsonLdObject {
  return {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "WebSite",
    name: siteConfig.name,
    url: siteConfig.url,
    inLanguage: "ru-RU",
    description: siteConfig.description,
  };
}

export type Crumb = { name: string; path: string };

export function breadcrumbJsonLd(items: readonly Crumb[]): JsonLdObject {
  return {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "BreadcrumbList",
    itemListElement: items.map((item, index) => ({
      "@type": "ListItem",
      position: index + 1,
      name: item.name,
      item: absoluteUrl(item.path),
    })),
  };
}

export function articleJsonLd(input: {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  path: string;
  published: string;
  modified?: string;
}): JsonLdObject {
  return {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "Article",
    headline: input.title,
    description: input.description,
    mainEntityOfPage: absoluteUrl(input.path),
    inLanguage: "ru-RU",
    datePublished: input.published,
    ...(input.modified ? { dateModified: input.modified } : {}),
  };
}

export function learningResourceJsonLd(input: {
  name: string;
  description: string;
  path: string;
  educationalLevel?: string;
}): JsonLdObject {
  return {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "LearningResource",
    name: input.name,
    description: input.description,
    url: absoluteUrl(input.path),
    inLanguage: "ru-RU",
    learningResourceType: "Задача с решением",
    ...(input.educationalLevel ? { educationalLevel: input.educationalLevel } : {}),
  };
}

export function personJsonLd(input: {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  image?: string;
  sameAs?: readonly string[];
}): JsonLdObject {
  return {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "Person",
    name: input.name,
    ...(input.description ? { description: input.description } : {}),
    ...(input.image ? { image: absoluteUrl(input.image) } : {}),
    ...(input.sameAs && input.sameAs.length > 0
      ? { sameAs: [...input.sameAs] }
      : {}),
  };
}

export function serviceJsonLd(input: {
  name: string;
  description: string;
  providerName?: string;
  areaServed?: string;
}): JsonLdObject {
  return {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "Service",
    name: input.name,
    description: input.description,
    ...(input.providerName
      ? { provider: { "@type": "Person", name: input.providerName } }
      : {}),
    ...(input.areaServed ? { areaServed: input.areaServed } : {}),
  };
}

export function faqPageJsonLd(items: readonly { question: string; answer: string }[]): JsonLdObject {
  return {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "FAQPage",
    mainEntity: items.map((item) => ({
      "@type": "Question",
      name: item.question,
      acceptedAnswer: { "@type": "Answer", text: item.answer },
    })),
  };
}




FILE: lib/seo/metadata.ts

import type { Metadata } from "next";

import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/constants/site";

export function absoluteUrl(path: string): string {
  return new URL(
    path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`,
    `${siteConfig.url}/`,
  ).toString();
}

export type PageMetadataInput = {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  path: string;
  type?: "website" | "article";
  noIndex?: boolean;
  ogImage?: string;
  publishedTime?: string;
  modifiedTime?: string;
};

export function buildMetadata(input: PageMetadataInput): Metadata {
  const url = absoluteUrl(input.path);
  const images = input.ogImage
    ? [
        {
          url: absoluteUrl(input.ogImage),
          width: 1200,
          height: 630,
          alt: input.title,
        },
      ]
    : undefined;

  return {
    title: input.title,
    description: input.description,
    alternates: { canonical: url },
    robots: input.noIndex ? { index: false, follow: false } : undefined,
    openGraph: {
      type: input.type ?? "website",
      locale: siteConfig.locale,
      url,
      siteName: siteConfig.name,
      title: input.title,
      description: input.description,
      ...(images ? { images } : {}),
      ...(input.publishedTime ? { publishedTime: input.publishedTime } : {}),
      ...(input.modifiedTime ? { modifiedTime: input.modifiedTime } : {}),
    },
    twitter: {
      card: images ? "summary_large_image" : "summary",
      title: input.title,
      description: input.description,
      ...(images ? { images: images.map((image) => image.url) } : {}),
    },
  };
}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const SITE = "https://osnova-lab.ru";

async function loadSeoModules() {
  vi.stubEnv("NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL", SITE);
  vi.resetModules();

  const site = await import("@/lib/constants/site");
  const metadata = await import("@/lib/seo/metadata");
  const jsonLd = await import("@/lib/seo/json-ld");
  const robotsModule = await import("@/app/robots");

  return {
    siteConfig: site.siteConfig,
    absoluteUrl: metadata.absoluteUrl,
    buildMetadata: metadata.buildMetadata,
    ...jsonLd,
    robots: robotsModule.default,
  };
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
  vi.resetModules();
});

describe("SEO absolute URLs", () => {
  it("uses NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL for canonical, Open Graph and JSON-LD", async () => {
    const seo = await loadSeoModules();

    expect(seo.siteConfig.url).toBe(SITE);
    expect(seo.absoluteUrl("/zadachi")).toBe(`${SITE}/zadachi`);

    const meta = JSON.parse(
      JSON.stringify(
        seo.buildMetadata({
          title: "Банк задач",
          description: "Описание",
          path: "/zadachi",
          ogImage: "/og/test.png",
        }),
      ),
    ) as {
      alternates: { canonical: string };
      openGraph: { url: string; images: Array<{ url: string }> };
      twitter: { images: string[] };
    };

    expect(meta.alternates.canonical).toBe(`${SITE}/zadachi`);
    expect(meta.openGraph.url).toBe(`${SITE}/zadachi`);
    expect(meta.openGraph.images[0].url).toBe(`${SITE}/og/test.png`);
    expect(meta.twitter.images[0]).toBe(`${SITE}/og/test.png`);

    expect(seo.webSiteJsonLd().url).toBe(SITE);
    expect(
      String(
        seo.articleJsonLd({
          title: "Статья",
          description: "Описание",
          path: "/articles/razbor",
          published: "2026-01-01",
        }).mainEntityOfPage,
      ),
    ).toBe(`${SITE}/articles/razbor`);
    expect(
      String(
        seo.learningResourceJsonLd({
          name: "Задача",
          description: "Описание",
          path: "/zadachi/1",
        }).url,
      ),
    ).toBe(`${SITE}/zadachi/1`);

    const crumbs = seo.breadcrumbJsonLd([{ name: "Главная", path: "/" }])
      .itemListElement as Array<{ item: string }>;
    expect(crumbs[0].item).toBe(`${SITE}/`);

    expect(String(seo.personJsonLd({ name: "X", image: "/photo.jpg" }).image)).toBe(
      `${SITE}/photo.jpg`,
    );
  });

  it("builds robots.txt sitemap and host on the same domain", async () => {
    const seo = await loadSeoModules();
    const data = seo.robots();

    expect(data.sitemap).toBe(`${SITE}/sitemap.xml`);
    expect(data.host).toBe("osnova-lab.ru");

    const serialized = JSON.stringify(data);
    expect(serialized).not.toContain("localhost");
    expect(serialized).not.toContain("example.test");
  });
});




FILE: lib/utils/check-answer.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { isAnswerCorrect, normalizeAnswer } from "@/lib/utils/check-answer";

describe("normalizeAnswer", () => {
  it("strips whitespace and a leading plus for numbers", () => {
    expect(normalizeAnswer("  +12 ", "number")).toBe("12");
  });

  it("accepts a comma as a decimal separator", () => {
    expect(normalizeAnswer("3,5", "number")).toBe("3.5");
  });

  it("normalizes every unicode minus to an ascii hyphen", () => {
    expect(normalizeAnswer("−7", "number")).toBe("-7");
    expect(normalizeAnswer("–7", "number")).toBe("-7");
  });

  it("drops leading zeros but keeps the sign", () => {
    expect(normalizeAnswer("007", "number")).toBe("7");
    expect(normalizeAnswer("-0.5", "number")).toBe("-0.5");
  });

  it("collapses whitespace for text answers", () => {
    expect(normalizeAnswer("  a   b  ", "text")).toBe("a b");
  });
});

describe("isAnswerCorrect", () => {
  it("matches the exact numeric answer", () => {
    expect(isAnswerCorrect("3", "3", "number")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("matches numeric equivalences", () => {
    expect(isAnswerCorrect("3,0", "3", "number")).toBe(true);
    expect(isAnswerCorrect(" 3 ", "3", "number")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects a wrong numeric answer", () => {
    expect(isAnswerCorrect("12", "3", "number")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects an empty answer", () => {
    expect(isAnswerCorrect("   ", "3", "number")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("is case-insensitive for text answers", () => {
    expect(isAnswerCorrect("Да", "да", "text")).toBe(true);
  });
});




FILE: lib/utils/check-answer.ts

export type AnswerKind = "number" | "text";

const MINUS_CHARS = /[−–—]/g;

export function normalizeAnswer(value: string, kind: AnswerKind): string {
  let result = value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(MINUS_CHARS, "-");

  if (kind === "number") {
    result = result.replace(/\s+/g, "").replace(/,/g, ".").replace(/^\+/, "");
    result = result.replace(/^(-?)0+(?=\d)/, "$1");
    result = result.replace(/\.0+$/, "");
  } else {
    result = result.replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  }

  return result;
}

export function isAnswerCorrect(
  input: string,
  expected: string,
  kind: AnswerKind,
): boolean {
  const actual = normalizeAnswer(input, kind);
  if (!actual) return false;

  const target = normalizeAnswer(expected, kind);
  if (actual === target) return true;

  if (kind === "number") {
    const a = Number(actual);
    const b = Number(target);
    if (Number.isFinite(a) && Number.isFinite(b)) {
      return Math.abs(a - b) < 1e-9;
    }
  }

  return false;
}




FILE: lib/utils/cn.ts

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]): string {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}




FILE: lib/utils/format.ts

export function pluralize(
  count: number,
  one: string,
  few: string,
  many: string,
): string {
  const mod10 = count % 10;
  const mod100 = count % 100;
  if (mod10 === 1 && mod100 !== 11) return one;
  if (mod10 >= 2 && mod10 <= 4 && (mod100 < 12 || mod100 > 14)) return few;
  return many;
}

export function formatMinutes(minutes: number): string {
  return `~${minutes} ${pluralize(minutes, "минута", "минуты", "минут")}`;
}

export function formatTaskCount(count: number): string {
  return `${count} ${pluralize(count, "задача", "задачи", "задач")}`;
}

export function formatArticleCount(count: number): string {
  return `${count} ${pluralize(count, "статья", "статьи", "статей")}`;
}

const DATE_FORMATTER = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ru-RU", {
  day: "numeric",
  month: "long",
  year: "numeric",
});

export function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return iso;
  return DATE_FORMATTER.format(date);
}




FILE: lib/utils/url.ts

export function buildQueryHref(
  basePath: string,
  params: Record<string, string | number | undefined>,
): string {
  const search = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (value === undefined || value === "") continue;
    search.set(key, String(value));
  }
  const query = search.toString();
  return query ? `${basePath}?${query}` : basePath;
}




FILE: lib/utils/use-prefers-reduced-motion.ts

"use client";

import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

const QUERY = "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)";

function subscribe(onStoreChange: () => void): () => void {
  const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia(QUERY);
  mediaQuery.addEventListener("change", onStoreChange);
  return () => mediaQuery.removeEventListener("change", onStoreChange);
}

function getSnapshot(): boolean {
  return window.matchMedia(QUERY).matches;
}

function getServerSnapshot(): boolean {
  return false;
}

export function usePrefersReducedMotion(): boolean {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getSnapshot, getServerSnapshot);
}
